               A Complete FAQ/Walkthrough for Annalog on the NES


            OOOOOOOOOO
          OOOOOOOOOOOOOO
          OOOO----------                  ,-------------------------------,

|| Table of Contents                                                        ||
++====================================++====================================++
|| 00: Game Controls                  || 08: Ascending to the City          ||
|| 01: Orc Attack!                    || 09: Claiming the Remote            ||
|| 02: Helping the Mermaids           || 10: Secrets in the Shadows         ||
|| 03: Exploring the Temple           || 11: Showdown at the Core           ||
|| 04: Rescue Mission                 || 12: List of Upgrades               ||
|| 05: The Hidden Crypt               || 13: Collecting Flowers             ||
|| 06: Into the Pits                  || 14: Notebook Pages                 ||
|| 07: Through the Mines              || 15: Copyright Information          ||
++====================================++====================================++

Annalog is a Zach-like programming Metroidvania--a "Zachtroidvania", if you
will--for the NES, created over four years from 2022 through 2025 and released
publicly in 2026.  You play as Anna, a young girl living in a small medieval
town who gets captured by orcs and, in her efforts to escape, must rediscover
lost technology and learn to reprogram the various pieces of abandoned
machinery she encounters.  You can find more information about the game and how
to get ahold of a copy at https://mdsteele.games/annalog/.

This walkthrough is designed to help you complete the game with a full 100%
completion percentage, although any optional detours along the way are called
out, in case you want to skip them and just beat the game without collecting
every single thing.  Note that in general, this walkthrough will guide you
towards picking up the various collectables throughout the game at convenient
points to do so, not necessarily as soon as you possibly can.

++==========================================================================++
|| Section 01: Orc Attack!                                                  ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \__________________________________,           OO  OO  OO
 |                                                \          OOOOOOOOOOOO
 |  [] Wake up in your house                       |         ----------OOOO
 |  [] Talk to everyone else before meeting Alex   |         @@@@@@@@@@--OO
 |  [] Talk to Alex and get attacked by orcs       |         @@--@@--@@--OO
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #36                   |         @@@@@@@@@@@@--
 |  [] Move the lift machine and escape your cell  |     @@    @@@@@@@@--
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #2                     |       @@  --------
 |  [] Use the trolley machine to cross the gap    |       @@@@@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Blast the orc guards with a rocket          |         @@@@@@@@@@@@
 |_________________________________________________|           @@@@@@@@@@@@
                                                               @@@@@@@@  @@
The game begins as you wake up in your home in Bartik        @@@@@@@@@@@@
Town, unable to sleep.  Talk to your mother, Stela,          @@@@  @@@@@@
before leaving through the door.                             ----    ----
                                                             @@@@    @@@@
Your main task is to find and talk to your brother Alex.
He's all the way on the east (right-hand) end of town, and you can go straight
there and talk to him if you want.  However, once you talk to him, you'll be
leaving town and not returning, so right now is your only chance to talk to
everyone else in town first.

Start by heading to the left and entering the house west of yours.  Here you
can talk to Nora, who is stuck babysitting her baby sister Nina.  Leave and
head right to the house just east of yours, where you'll meet smith Dominic, to
whom your brother Alex is apprenticed.  Continuing to the right, you can visit
town hall, where your Uncle Martin and Aunt Laura are waiting for Elder Roman;
next you'll pass by Ivan and Sandra, who are tending to the fields, as well as
their house, where their kids Bruno and Marie are still awake; and finally
you'll reach the easternmost house, where Elder Roman is waiting for Uncle
Martin and Aunt Laura (oops!).

Once you've talked to everyone and you're ready to continue with the plot, head
further east and talk to your red-headed brother, Alex.  He'll show you
something strange he found, and talk about how he wishes you two could go out
and explore the wider world.  And then, you'll be interrupted by Orc Chief
Gronta and her band of grunts.

The orcs will attack.  You have no means of fighting back, and you won't be
able to dodge them forever, so go ahead and let them hit you.  Once they've hit
you twice in a row, you'll pass out, and they'll throw Alex and you into
separate jail cells.  Don't worry, you'll see Alex again, but it'll be a while.

                                  *    *    *

Once you wake up in your cell, the game begins in earnest.

Start by reading the notebook page on the wall to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #36.
Notice how once you've read it, the page will have a little checkmark on it;
this indicates that you've read this note already.  You can also pause the game
with Start and then press Down on the D-Pad to review all the notebook pages
you've collected so far.  None of these notes are required for beating the game
or seeing any particular story ending, but they reveal more of the game's
story, and your end-game completion percentage will depend partly on finding
them, so you if want to finish with 100% completion, you'll need to collect
them all.  This walkthrough will take you to collect all of them, but it will
also let you know whenever there's a major detour you can skip if you don't
care about collecting them and just want to get to the end of the game.

Once you've read the note, jump up the platforms to reach the computer terminal
in the top-left corner that has just flickered to life.  The orcs didn't seem
to notice that it was in here.  Walk up to it and press the B button to use it;
this is the first of many consoles throughout the game that you'll need to use
to program the various machines you'll find.  Enter the following program, then
press the B button to close the console:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 4:        |
        | 1:END     | 5:        |
        | 2: ----   | 6:        |
        | 3:        | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

The lift machine will rise up out of the way, allowing you to slip out the back
of the prison cell.

In the next room, head to the far left, then drop down and read the page on the
wall to collect FIELD MANUAL #2.  Don't be too hasty jumping down from there,
though; there are spikes below.  Fortunately, there's an easy trick to get past
them safely: jump onto the little 1-by-1 platform, then hold down Right on the
D-pad and don't let go.  You'll bonk into the ledge on your way down, but still
land safely on the right-hand side of the spike pit.

To the right of there, you'll see another spike pit; this time, you'll need to
use the machine to help you get across.  Hop down to the console, and use it to
enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE >  | 4: ----   |
        | 1:MOVE >  | 5:        |
        | 2:MOVE >  | 6:        |
        | 3:END     | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

This program will make the trolley move three blocks to the right and then
stop, putting the girder platform in the perfect place to help you jump across
the gap.  Proceed from there through the passage to the right to the next room.

Recognize the area above you?  You're now in a hidden tunnel underneath your
jail cell.  Keep going to the right, and you'll see two orc guards above you,
just outside your cell, as well as another console at the end of your tunnel.
Use it, and you'll see that it controls the rocket launcher machine in the
ceiling above the orcs.  It's time to give the orcs a taste of their own
medicine!  Enter the following program before closing the console:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 4:        |
        | 1:ACT     | 5:        |
        | 2:END     | 6:        |
        | 3: ----   | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

Awesome!  That's one guard knocked out, the other fled, and the rocket even
blew open a path for you to escape.  Just walk over so you can jump up, and...

...oh.  The floor gives way, and you'll fall down, down, down, until you land
in a small pond of water, deep underground.  Looks like you won't be escaping
back to the surface anytime soon.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 02: Helping the Mermaids                                         ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \______________________________,               @@
 |                                            \          @@  @@  @@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #13               |         @@@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Gain IF OPCODE upgrade                  |         OO@@OOOOOO
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #3                 |       OO------OOOOOO
 |  [] Squish a grub                           |       --@@@@@@--OOOOOO
 |  [] Meet Corra the mermaid                  |       @@--@@--@@--OOOO    OO
 |  [] Blow up a bug                           |       @@@@@@@@@@@@--OOOOOOOO
 |  [] Meet the mermaid queen                  |         @@----@@--OOOOOOOO
 |  [] Speak with Selene, the mermaid florist  |           @@@@----OOOOOO  OO
 |  [] Deliver the MERMAID VALE FLOWER         |           ----@@----OOOOOO
 |  [] Speak with Dion, the mermaid farmer     |           ||@@@@@@||@@
 |  [] Ascend to the garden tower              |         @@||||@@||||@@@@
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #6                 |         @@||||||||||||@@  ||
 |  [] Break open the tower entrance           |       @@--||||||||||--@@@@||||
 |  [] Defeat POLYCLOPS                        |       @@----||||||||----@@||||
 |  [] Gain 1st PROGRAM RAM upgrade            |       @@--||||||||||----@@||||
 |  [] Activate CIRCUIT #1                     |       @@@@----||||||--@@@@||||
 |  [] Report back to Queen Eirene             |           @@@@------@@@@--||||
 |_____________________________________________|         @@@@@@@@@@@@--@@--||||
                                                       @@@@--@@--------||--||||
After falling down from the Prison Caves, swim         @@--@@--||||||||----||||
to the right and jump up out of the water.             @@@@@@----------------||
Welcome to the Vine Garden!  Jump up to the              @@@@--||||||||||||||||
platform and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #13, then         @@@@@@@@@@------||||----||
leave through the eastern passage.                 @@@@@@--@@@@@@--||||||||||||
                                                   @@----------@@------------||
In the next room, you'll see what looks like a
computer chip with the word "IF" on it.  That's your first upgrade!  Walk up to
it and press the B button to gain the IF OPCODE.  This new instruction will
allow your machine programs to conditionally execute or skip single
instructions.  Now continue east to the next room.

This next room is crawling with grubs, and has passages out all four corners.
We'll call this room the "garden crossroads".  We'll be returning here later,
but for now our goal is to exit out the lower-right passage.  Unfortunately,
the grub that is crawling back and forth under the lift machine is blocking our
path.

Start by reading the page on the wall to collect FIELD MANUAL #6.  Then go to
the little chamber on the left with a console and a lever.  You can flip the
lever back and forth with the B button, but it doesn't seem to do anything,
does it?  That's because it's connected to the machine, but you haven't
programmed the machine yet!  Use the console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF L=1  | 4: ----   |
        | 1:MOVE v  | 5:        |
        | 2:IF Y=0  | 6:        |
        | 3:MOVE ^  | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

What does this program do?  For starters, there's no END instruction, so it
will loop forever, constantly checking if L=1 or if Y=0.  The L register refers
to the lever, which toggles between 0 and 1 when you flip it.  The Y register
refers to the lift machine's vertical position, which starts at 1.  So the way
this program works is that if you flip the lever to 1, it will move down; then
its vertical position will be 0, so it'll move back up and repeat.  When you
flip the lever back to 0, the machine will return to Y=1 and stop, waiting
until you to flip the lever again.

With this program in place, close the console with B, wait for the grub to
crawl right under the lift machine, and then flip the lever.  Squish!  Flip the
lever back off so the machine stops moving, and now the path is open for you to
walk under the machine and hop down to the bottom of the room.  There are
passages out the left and right here; for now, ignore the one to the left, and
head out the right-hand passage.

As you enter the next room you'll see...a mermaid!?  This is Corra.  Jump into
the water and speak with her.  She's as surprised to see a real human as you
are to see a real mermaid.  Answer her question either way you want, and she'll
advise you to go see the mermaid queen, and mark the location on your pause
screen minimap.  When the conversation is over, you can press the START button
to see the map; the new quest marker shows up as an X on the map.  Fortunately,
you don't have too far to go to get there.

Jump out of the water and head to the right; watch out for the bugs crawling on
vines, which will drop down towards you when you approach.  You'll see a
console for the bridge machine ahead.  You'll need to raise the bridge to reach
the level below, but if you raise it right now, it'll block your path.  Time to
make more use of your new IF instruction.  Enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF L=1  | 4:        |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 5:        |
        | 2: ----   | 6:        |
        | 3:        | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

Then walk past the bridge and flip the lever once.  The bridge will swing up,
and then...EHR-NHR-EHR.  The machine has hit an error.  That's because L is
still 1, so when the machine looped through the program it tried to move up
again, and couldn't.  Now the machine will halt until you return the console to
reset it (or leave the room and come back).  And that's fine!  Machine errors
are OK, and sometimes you can even use them to your advantage.  For now, the
path downward is open, so go ahead and hop down under the bridge.  Later in the
game, when you return to this room, you can always improve this bridge program
to make it a little more robust.

Down in the bottom half of the room, head to the left to see a cannon machine,
as well as another one of those vine crawling bugs blocking your way through.
Time to blow up a bug!  However, if you just fire the cannon machine blindly,
the bug will sense the danger and try to avoid your shots, so you might never
hit it; instead, you'll have to use the lever to time your shots.  Also, the
cannon barrel can move into either of two positions, and it's much easier to
hit the bug when aiming up than when aiming down.  Use the nearby console to
enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF Y=0  | 4: ----   |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 5:        |
        | 2:IF L=1  | 6:        |
        | 3:ACT     | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

How does this program work?  The cannon starts in the lower position (Y=0).
The MOVE ^ instruction moves the cannon into the upper position (Y=1), and the
IF Y=0 ensures that we only try to do that when the cannon is still in the
lower position.  (If we tried to move up twice, we'd get an error and the
cannon would no longer be able to fire until we reset it, just like the bridge
machine from before.)  Meanwhile, the second half of the program fires the
cannon when the lever is in the L=1 position.

With this program in place, close the console window.  Notice how the cannon
barrel lifts into the upper position and stays there.  Wait for the bug to
crawl to the top of the vine, then flip the lever to shoot it.  If you miss,
flip the lever back off, and wait to try again.  Also, be sure to flip the
lever to the off position before you try to jump through, or you might get shot
by the cannon yourself!  When you're ready, continue through the passage to the
left.

The next room is a narrow winding path past two grubs; just be patient, and
they should be easy enough to get past.  Continue leftwards to the next room.

Now you'll find yourself in a large room, dominated by a stone tower whose top
is choked with spiky, writhing vines.  We'll be returning to this tower soon,
but right now we've due for an audience with the queen.  So for now, just drop
down the treetops to the bottom of this room, and leave out the bottom-right
passage.

You'll find yourself in a sandy beach-like area, and the music will change.
Welcome to the Mermaid Vale!  To pass eastward through this room, you'll have
to get past Renaldo the Run-Wrecking Crab, a.k.a. "The Widowmaker".  He's not
too bad; he just scuttles back and forth, but will sometimes change directions
randomly and abruptly, perhaps knocking you off the ledge in the process.  By
the end of the game you will probably have a love/hate relationship with this
li'l guy.  For now, just do your best to jump past him, avoid the fish in the
water beyond, and continue east to the mermaid village in the next room.

The mermaid village is a large room with a handful of different huts across two
levels.  It will be your primary home base for the rest of the game, so you'll
want to take the time to look around and get familiar with it.  Your first stop
should be in the large hut in the top-center of the village, where you'll find
the throne room of Queen Eirene of the mermaids.  Speak with her.  After
hearing your tale, she will reluctantly grant you asylum in her village, but
asks in return that you help their farmers with a little "problem."  We'll get
to that soon.

To the right of the queen's hut, you'll find the guardhouse; there's a mermaid
just outside, and another inside, that you can talk to.  Directly below the
guardhouse is an empty hut, which will become occupied later in the game.  To
the left of the empty hut, in the bottom-center of the village, is the home of
Selene, the mermaid florist.  Enter and speak with her, and she will ask for a
favor.  There's no harm in answering however you like; if you refuse at first,
you can always talk to her again and change your mind.  She will ask you to
find and deliver a flower to her; this is the first step in a game-long (and
totally optional) side quest.  Eventually completing the quest will gain you
the BEEP opcode (which is mostly just a fun toy) and will increase your
end-game completion percentage, but doesn't affect the ending of the story, so
it really is optional.  If you do want to collect this flower now, the
"Optional Detour" box below will tell you how to get it.  If not, feel free to
skip the box below; you can always get the flower later (see Section 13).

          ,-,-,--------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,------/ / / Optional Detour: Deliver the Mermaid Vale Flower / / /------,
  |     '-'-'--------------------------------------------------'-'-'       |
  | Starting from the mermaid village, leave through the top-right         |
  | passage.  You'll find yourself at the hot springs; pass through here   |
  | and leave to the right.  This next room is the eastern end of the      |
  | vale.  Head right, avoiding the fish.  Feel free to peek inside the    |
  | hut here, but there's not much to do inside until later in the game.   |
  | From the outside of the hut, jump upwards, then around to the left,    |
  | avoiding the crabs and one more fish, and go through the upper-left    |
  | passage.  This next room has an elevator, but we don't need it right   |
  | now, so continue to the left.  Finally you'll reach the room with the  |
  | flower.  Climb up the left side of the room, jump over to the flower,  |
  | and press the B button to pick it up.                                  |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now that you're holding the flower, if you take damage the flower will |
  | break.  If that happens, you'll have to come back to this room and     |
  | pick it up again.  So your goal is to bring the flower back to Selene  |
  | in the bottom-center hut of the mermaid village without ever getting   |
  | hit along the way.                                                     |
  |                                                                        |
  | Don't jump straight down from where you picked up the flower, because  |
  | if you do the bird enemy will probably hit you and break the flower.   |
  | Instead, jump back to the left and drop down on that side of the room, |
  | then swim back out the right-hand passage.  Pass through the elevator  |
  | room back to the big eastern room.  After jumping over the first crab, |
  | you'll see there's a little hole here before the fish pool.  If you    |
  | want, you can try to drop through it and hold Left for a bit to land   |
  | on the narrow island between the two fish down below, but if you mess  |
  | up you'll probably get hit by the fish and have to go back for the     |
  | flower.  If you don't want to risk it, then instead of dropping        |
  | through the little hole, just hop over the fish pond to the right and  |
  | past the crab, drop down the right-hand side of the room, and then     |
  | proceed out the bottom-left passage, avoiding the two fish.  Once you  |
  | reach the hot springs, you're home free; just keep going left into the |
  | mermaid village, head to the bottom-center hut, and talk to Selene to  |
  | deliver the flower.                                                    |
  |                                                                        |
  | With each flower you deliver, Selene will tell you a few more tidbits  |
  | of lore.  Once you've delivered all twelve flowers (which won't be     |
  | until the game is almost over), she'll unlock her basement and you can |
  | claim the reward there.                                                |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

Whether or not you delivered the flower, you should be in the mermaid village
at this point.  Your last task here for now is to speak with Dion, the mermaid
farmer (and Corra's father) in the bottom left of the village (assuming you've
already spoken with the queen).  He'll tell you that there's a monster in that
garden tower just west of the mermaid vale, and that they'll open up the path
for you to get there.  You can also talk to Corra next to him, and to Corra's
mother Daphne and sister Phoebe in the hut to the left.  Once you're done
talking to everyone, leave the village out the upper-left passage.  It's time
to go monster hunting.

Proceed left past the fish and crab here, and re-enter the vine garden.  You
should see the large tower to your left.  Jump up the trees to reach the upper
of the two openings in the tower wall, then jump past the grubs until you're
standing on the ground on the left side of the tower.  You'll see a tree with a
cannon and some levers, but you can't jump high enough to get up there yet.
Instead, jump onto the two crates stacked on the ground, and then jump through
the narrow passage in the left-hand wall of the room.  (If you don't see two
crates on the ground, it means you didn't get the quest from Dion yet.  Go back
to the village and talk to the queen, and then to Dion the farmer.)

In the next room, you'll see a couple of consoles, and a raised bridge machine.
Use the bottom console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF L=1  | 4:        |
        | 1:MOVE v  | 5:        |
        | 2: ----   | 6:        |
        | 3:        | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

Then jump up to the lever and flip it to lower the bridge.  The machine will
error after the bridge lowers, but that's OK.  You can always reset the machine
if you need to.

Jump up to the next console; before using it, read the page to its left to
collect FIELD MANUAL #6.  This page gives a pretty big clue how to solve this
next puzzle.  Use the console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF Y>L  | 4: ----   |
        | 1:MOVE v  | 5:        |
        | 2:IF Y<L  | 6:        |
        | 3:MOVE ^  | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

Do you see how this works?  The lever position L can be 0 or 1, and the bridge
position Y can also be 0 or 1.  If Y is greater than L, that must mean that L=0
and Y=1, so the program moves the bridge down so that Y and L are both 0.
Similarly, if Y is less than L, that must mean that L=1 and Y=0, so the program
moves the bridge up so that Y and L are both 1.  Either way, the bridge will
always move to match the lever.

The lever starts at L=0 and the bridge starts at Y=1, so when you close the
console, the bridge will immediately move down to match the lever.  That lets
you jump up onto the bridge and over to the lever.  Flip the lever, and the
bridge will raise again; that lets you jump onto the tippy top of the raised
bridge, and from there onto the uppermost platform.  Finally, jump past the
vine bug and out through the upper-right passage, back into the tower room.

Our goal is to reach the door inside the tower, but there's a brick wall
blocking the way in, so we'll have to blow it up with the cannon.  Drop down to
the console here, and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF Y>L  | 4: IF R=1 |
        | 1:MOVE v  | 5: ACT    |
        | 2:IF Y<L  | 6:  ----  |
        | 3:MOVE ^  | 7:        |
        +===========+===========+

Notice how this is the same program as the last one, except that we've added
the "IF R=1 ACT" part.  This machine has two levers: L is the left lever, and R
is the right lever.  Just like before, the cannon barrel will always move to
match the left lever, and then you can fire with the right lever.  To destroy
the brick wall, you'll have to hit the top and bottom of it with two shots
each, so use the levers to fire the cannon twice in each of its two positions.
Once the wall is gone, you'll hear a little jingle melody to indicate that the
puzzle is solved.  Make sure to turn the cannon off with the right-hand lever
so it won't be shooting at you, then jump up from the levers to the cannon and
across into the tower.

That program we just wrote is about to come in handy again, because it's boss
time.  Press the B button to enter the doorway in the tower, and you'll be
faced with...

       ,-,--------------------,-,                     ,---------------------,
  ,----+ | Boss #1: Polyclops | +---------------------+ Difficulty:   1 / 5 |
  |    '-'--------------------'-'                     | Hits to defeat:   8 |
  | This giant flesh-eating plant has taken over the  | Reward: RAM upgrade |
  | garden tower, so it's time for you to do some     '--------------------+'
  | weeding.  Polyclops has two giant eyes that will                       |
  | open up one at a time to shoot fireballs at you.  The cannon machine   |
  | on the left can fire into the left eye when its barrel is raised, or   |
  | into the right eye when its barrel is lowered. You need to shoot each  |
  | eye four times while it's open.  The tricky part is, if you shoot an   |
  | eye while it's closed, then not only will you not do any damage, but   |
  | you'll knock loose some thorns which can fall and hurt you.  So you    |
  | can't just make the cannon shoot mindlessly; you'll need to control it |
  | with the levers.                                                       |
  |                                                                        |
  | There's two good strategies here, and you can pick either one:         |
  |                                                                        |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:IF L>Y  | 4:IF R=1  |            | 0:MOVE ^  | 4:IF R=1  |     |
  |     | 1:MOVE ^  | 5:ACT     |     OR     | 1:IF L=1  | 5:ACT     |     |
  |     | 2:IF L<Y  | 6: ----   |            | 2:ACT     | 6: ----   |     |
  |     | 3:MOVE v  | 7:        |            | 3:MOVE v  | 7:        |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | With the left-hand program, you control the angle of the cannon with   |
  | the left lever, and fire with the right lever, just like the cannon    |
  | outside the tower.  The disadvantage of this is that you have to run   |
  | back and forth between the two levers every time you want to attack,   |
  | but the advantage is that the cannon fires right away when you flip    |
  | the right-hand lever, and then you can turn it back off quickly before |
  | it fires again at a now-closed eye.                                    |
  |                                                                        |
  | With the right-hand program, the cannon will constantly move up and    |
  | down. When you flip the left lever, it'll fire at the left eye, and    |
  | when you flip the right lever, it'll fire at the right eye.  Depending |
  | on where the barrel is when you flip the lever, the cannon might not   |
  | fire right away, but this strategy does have the advantage that you    |
  | only need to run to one lever once you see which eye is opening.       |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you've scored at least four hits total, Polyclops will sometimes  |
  | shoot a big spray of fireballs; you'll hear a windup sound when it's   |
  | about to happen.  Either be ready to dodge it, or else shoot the open  |
  | eye right away to interrupt the attack.  Once you've scored a total of |
  | eight hits, this gardening nightmare will head to the compost bin.     |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

Once Polyclops has perished, pick up the PROGRAM RAM upgrade that appears.
Congratulations on downloading more RAM!  Your programs can now have up to ten
instructions each instead of only eight.  You'll be getting a few more of these
RAM upgrades as the game continues.

Once you pick up the upgrade, a giant circuit breaker lever will rise from the
ground.  Walk over and flip it, and a cutscene will play.  CIRCUIT #1 has now
been activated!  Time to head back to the mermaid village and report on your
success.  Exit through the unlocked door, drop down from the tower, and head
out the lower-right passage of the tower room.  Continue east until you reach
the mermaid village.

When you return to the mermaid village, you can talk to Dion the farmer in the
southwest corner if you want to get his thanks.  When you're ready, report to
Queen Eirene in the top-center hut.  She'll give you a new mission to ascend to
the top of the Lost Temple.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 03: Exploring the Temple                                         ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \___________________________,      @@      @@@@@@@@
 |                                         \     @@    @@------@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #12            |  @@@@@@  --@@@@@@--@@@@  @@
 |  [] Enter the Lost Temple                |  @@@@@@  @@--@@--@@--@@@@
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #1              |    @@    @@@@@@@@@@--@@--  @@
 |  [] Gain TIL OPCODE upgrade              |    @@    --@@----@@--@@@@@@
 |  [] Blast apart the cracked column       |    @@      --@@@@------@@
 |  [] Learn the true name of the temple    |    @@        ----@@--
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #9              |    @@      ||@@@@@@||@@@@o
 |  [] Ascend to the temple spire           |    @@    @@||||@@||||@@@@@@
 |  [] Defeat OUTBREAK                      |  @@@@  @@@@||||||||||||--@@@@
 |  [] Gain 2nd PROGRAM RAM upgrade         |  @@@@@@@@@@  ||||||||--    @@
 |  [] Activate CIRCUIT #2                  |    @@  @@    ||||||||--    @@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #28            |    @@        --||||||||--@@@@
 |  [] Deliver the LOST TEMPLE FLOWER       |    @@        ------------  @@
 |  [] Tell Phoebe about your latest        |    @@        @@@@@@@@@@@@
 |     adventure                            |    @@      @@--@@@@@@--@@
 |  [] Report back to Queen Eirene          |    @@    @@@@@@@@@@--@@
 |__________________________________________|    @@  @@@@@@@@@@@@@@
                                                 @@  @@--@@@@------        @@@@
Once Queen Eirene gives you the quest to         @@  @@@@--@@@@@@@@--@@@@@@@@
explore the temple, your first stop is the       @@    --@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@
guardhouse just to the right of the queen's      @@          @@@@      @@@@
hut, where the guard outside will have           @@                      @@
something new to say.  Then, leave the
mermaid village through the top-left passage, and continue west until you're
back in the Vine Garden.

Time to backtrack a bit.  In the huge garden room with the tower, jump up
through the trees and head out the top-right passage.  Crawl through this
winding tunnel, avoiding the grubs, and continue east.  Back in the big room
with the vine bug that blocks the way, jump onto the tree and then through the
opening above the bug, and head to the right-hand side of this room.  Climb up
the far right edge of the room to the very top, then jump up onto the uppermost
platform and head left past the bridge.  Leave this room through the top-left
passage.  You're now back at the "garden crossroads", where you earlier solved
a puzzle to squish a grub.  Walk left and exit out the bottom-left passage to
find yourself in a new room.

This room is a long hallway filled with little trees and platforms.  Your first
step is to jump up to the ledge on the right and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #12.
From there, head left by jumping along the highest platforms.  Watch out for
the vine bug, and jump over to the console on top of the tree.  You'll need to
use the lift machine to help you reach the top of the next tree, so enter the
following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF Y<5  | 5:        |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 6:        |
        | 2: ----   | 7:        |
        | 3:        | 8:        |
        | 4:        | 9:        |
        +===========+===========+

This will cause the lift machine to move up to a convenient height and then
stop there.  Once it does, jump on top of it, and then up onto the treetop.
Jump left from there to the next tree, and finally jump over the big wall with
spikes on top to land on the other side.  Avoid the grub and vine bug, and
leave through the left-hand passage.

You're now at the base of the temple, but the platforms that lead up to the
entrance are too high for you to reach.  However, there's a mermaid standing
(swimming?) guard here, so talk to her.  Since you have the queen's permission
to enter, the guard will raise up a column that will allow you to jump up.  (If
the guard says you don't have permission to enter, it means you didn't get the
quest from Queen Eirene yet.  Go back to the mermaid village and talk to the
queen, then come back here and talk to the guard again.)

Once the column is raised, jump onto it, then onto the ledge on the left, and
from there onto the platform with the giant toad.  Work your way past the
enemies, zig-zagging through the room as you jump upwards, until you can enter
the temple by passing through the upper-left passage and into the foyer.

Welcome to the Lost Temple!  It's full of abandoned machinery, for some reason;
perhaps we'll learn why later.  Before we worry about the console here, walk to
the left and collect FIELD MANUAL #1.  Then head back right, jump up to the
console, and activate it.  This carriage machine can move in all four
directions within the rectangular trellis, and we'll need to get it to navigate
through this little maze of walls.  But we don't have a very good way to do
that with our current set of commands; there aren't enough slots in our program
to type out all the MOVEs we'd need.  So the first thing we need to do is
program it to move straight up:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 5:        |
        | 1: ----   | 6:        |
        | 2:        | 7:        |
        | 3:        | 8:        |
        | 4:        | 9:        |
        +===========+===========+

Close the console and immediately jump onto the carriage as it trundles
upwards.  (If you miss your chance, just open the console to reset the machine,
and you can try again.)  The carriage will carry you upwards and error when it
reaches the top.  From up here, you should be able to see a new upgrade above
and to the right!  To reach it, first jump up onto the platform on the left,
avoiding the spiky beetles, then up to the right, and finally over to the
upgrade.  Walk up to it and press the B button to gain the TIL OPCODE.  This
new instruction will allow your machine programs to repeat a single instruction
unTIL a condition is met, and it's just the thing we need for programming this
carriage to get through the maze.

Jump off the ledge and fall all the way back down to the console.  Reprogram
the carriage, changing our first program into this new version:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 5:MOVE <  |
        | 1:MOVE <  | 6:TIL X=0 |
        | 2:TIL X=4 | 7:MOVE v  |
        | 3:MOVE ^  | 8:TIL Y=3 |
        | 4:TIL Y=8 | 9:END     |
        +===========+===========+

Close the console and immediately jump left onto the carriage as it starts
moving.  (Again, if you miss and the carriage leaves without you, just use the
console to reset the machine, and try again.)  Ride the carriage through the
maze until it stops in the middle of the left side.  Now you can lift off to
the left to land on the far ledge.  Climb up from there and leave this room
through the upper-left passage.

You'll find yourself in another huge room, this time with two consoles, which
operate two different carriage machines.  We'll call this room the "temple
nave".  There are passages leading off from all four corners of the nave, but
some of them will have to wait for later in the game.  For now, our goal is to
reach the upper-left passage.

Start by using the left-hand console to program the upper carriage machine:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 5:TIL Y=0 |
        | 1:TIL X=0 | 6:MOVE >  |
        | 2:MOVE ^  | 7:TIL X=9 |
        | 3:TIL Y=4 | 8: ----   |
        | 4:MOVE v  | 9:        |
        +===========+===========+

This will make the upper carriage loop along an L-shaped path, which will be
helpful to us once we get up there.  Next, use the right-hand console to
program the lower carriage machine:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE >  | 5:TIL X=2 |
        | 1:TIL X=9 | 6:MOVE ^  |
        | 2:MOVE ^  | 7:TIL Y=6 |
        | 3:TIL Y=3 | 8:END     |
        | 4:MOVE <  | 9: ----   |
        +===========+===========+

After closing the console, immediately jump up to the raised platform between
the consoles, then up onto the carriage as it moves to the left.  Once it
reaches the right side of the trellis, jump onto the ledge here, run left over
the little gap (it's narrow enough that you can just run straight over it
without jumping), and stand under the wide space between the spikes.  When the
carriage approaches you, jump straight up to land on it as it slides under you,
then carries you up.  Once the carriage stops, wait for the upper carriage,
which should still be moving in its L-shaped loop, to pass by, and jump onto
it.  Keep an eye on the spiked beetle enemy to your left, and when the coast is
clear, jump onto its ledge.  Pass to the left while avoiding the beetle, and
climb up through the upper-left passage.

You'll now be at the bottom of a tall room with several enemies.  Zig-zag
across the room as you climb up, avoiding the enemies.  For the toads, you can
either jump over them right after they land, or run under them right after they
jump.  Leave through the upper-right passage.

In this next room, you'll find another carriage-looking machine, but this one
has a minigun mounted in it.  Your path upwards is blocked, but one of the
columns is cracked.  Walk over to the console on the right, and enter the
following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 5:TIL Y=6 |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 6:ACT     |
        | 2:TIL Y=3 | 7:TIL 1=0 |
        | 3:MOVE >  | 8: ----   |
        | 4:MOVE ^  | 9:        |
        +===========+===========+

See the "ACT TIL 0=1" part in this program?  Since 1 will never equal 0, this
will loop forever, shooting the minigun the whole time.  Close the console
window and watch as the minigun machine moves upwards, and fires repeatedly
into the cracked column until it breaks.  With the column broken, you've got a
clear path to ascend; or rather, you *would* have a clear path, if there
weren't a minigun constantly shooting right at the spot where you need to walk!
So open the console again, and reprogram the machine as follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 5:MOVE v  |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 6:TIL Y=0 |
        | 2:TIL Y=3 | 7:MOVE >  |
        | 3:MOVE >  | 8: ----   |
        | 4:MOVE <  | 9:        |
        +===========+===========+

This will make the minigun move continuously in a C-shaped loop.  Close the
console window, and jump onto the minigun when it's at the bottom of its loop;
when it reaches the top of its loop, jump up to the platform above and go
through the gap where the cracked column was.  From there, jump up to the
plaque centered between the two levers, and press the B button to read it.

Hmmm.  "Temple of Peace" is an odd name for a temple full of guns and machines.
There's clearly more to the story here.  The A.D. 2250 date on the plaque is
also the first confirmation that this game is set far in the future, at a time
when the 23rd century is already forgotten history.

But enough about plot!  There's another minigun machine up here, but before we
deal with it, jump up to the ledge on the left and through the passage there.
You'll find yourself in a small room with a note on the left-hand wall.  To
reach it, jump into the water with the fish, jump over it to avoid it, swim to
the left side, and jump up onto the little ledge there.  This next jump into
the narrow gap below the spiky beetle's platform is a little tricky, and may
take you a few tries; try tapping the A button briefly to make a shorter hop.
Once you're through, jump up and read the note to collect FIELD MANUAL #9.
Once you do, you can either go back the way you game to leave the room, or if
you're careful, you can take a running leap over the spiked wall in the center
of the room.  Or, you know, you can just run into the beetle and die, and
you'll respawn at the entrance of the room.  In any case, leave this room to
return to the tall room with the plaque.

Back in the plaque room, jump over to the console in the upper-right, and enter
the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF L=1  | 5:ACT     |
        | 1:MOVE <  | 6: ----   |
        | 2:IF R=1  | 7:        |
        | 3:MOVE >  | 8:        |
        | 4:IF L≠R  | 9:        |
        +===========+===========+

Note the "NOT equals" sign in instruction #4.  This program will make the
minigun move left when the left lever is flipped, or right when the right lever
is flipped, and will shoot as it moves in either direction.  If neither lever
is flipped, it will stay still without shooting.  With this program in place,
operate one lever at a time, guiding the minigun towards the spiky beetles to
blast them.  Be careful not to let the machine go too far, or it will error
when it tries to move past the end of its track.  (If that happens, use the
console to reset the machine.)  Once all three beetles are gone, move the
minigun to the second position from the left (so that it's centered between the
left-hand column and the little platform to the left of that), then stop it
there.  Then you can jump up to the console ledge, then jump left across the
two columns, then jump up to the little platform, then carefully jump up onto
the minigun, and finally onto the uppermost platform.  Finally, jump up through
the passage in the center of the ceiling, holding Right on the D-pad as you do.

You'll find yourself jumping up through the floor of the room above; keep
holding Right and you'll land on the ledge here.  Jump up again to the right
and onto the little platform.  Keep an eye on the spike-dropping slime enemy
above; you'll need to jump across the platforms to the left here without
getting hit.  If necessary, you can bait out a spike from the enemy by jumping
towards it and then immediately turning around to land where you started; once
it drops a spike, there's a short cooldown before it can drop another one,
which gives you time to jump past.  Work your way over to the left side of the
room, then climb up top to stand in the doorway above.  You're now at the very
top of the temple spire.  Press the B button to enter the doorway, and get
ready to tangle with...

       ,-,-------------------,-,                      ,---------------------,
  ,----+ | Boss #2: Outbreak | +----------------------+ Difficulty:   2 / 5 |
  |    '-'-------------------'-'                      | Hits to defeat: ??? |
  | This massive mutant abomination hangs from the    | Reward: RAM upgrade |
  | top of the temple spire.  Even with the minigun   '--------------------+'
  | machine here, you have no means of hurting it directly.  Outbreak will |
  | periodically launch a slow-moving, bouncing ball of energy at you.  If |
  | the energy ball hits the floor, it will explode into two rapidly       |
  | moving tongues of fire that you'll have to dodge.  However, you can    |
  | move the minigun platform to intercept the energy ball and bounce it   |
  | back upwards; when it hits the boss, it will be briefly stunned, with  |
  | one eye open.  That's your chance to shoot the open eye with the       |
  | minigun, driving the boss upwards towards the spikes above.            |
  |                                                                        |
  | There are a number of possible program strategies you can use.  Here   |
  | are two good options:                                                  |
  |                                                                        |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:IF L=0  | 5:ACT     |            | 0:IF L=1  | 5:TIL R=0 |     |
  |     | 1:MOVE <  | 6:TIL R=0 |     OR     | 1:MOVE <  | 6:ACT     |     |
  |     | 2:IF L=1  | 7: ----   |            | 2:TIL L=0 | 7: ----   |     |
  |     | 3:MOVE >  | 8:        |            | 3:IF R=1  | 8:        |     |
  |     | 4:IF R=1  | 9:        |            | 4:MOVE >  | 9:        |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | With the left-hand program, the left lever controls which direction    |
  | the minigun moves, and flipping the right lever on makes the minigun   |
  | stop in place and fire until you turn the right lever back off.  With  |
  | the right-hand program, flipping the left lever on makes the minigun   |
  | move left until you turn the left lever back off, and similarly for    |
  | the right lever; whenever both levers are off, the minigun will fire   |
  | in place.                                                              |
  |                                                                        |
  | Regardless of which control scheme you prefer, the strategy is the     |
  | same: move the minigun to intercept the energy balls that Outbreak     |
  | shoots and reflect them back up.  Once the boss gets stunned, move the |
  | minigun under the open eye and fire at it until the boss shoots a new  |
  | energy ball.  One thing to keep in mind: sometimes, when Outbreak is   |
  | very near the ceiling, the reflected energy balls may miss it!  Don't  |
  | worry; just keep trying until you get a good angle.  Eventually, you   |
  | will drive the boss all the way up into the spikes, and victory will   |
  | be yours.                                                              |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

With Outbreak obliterated, pick up the PROGRAM RAM upgrade that appears.  Now
your programs can have up to twelve instructions each.  Once again, a circuit
breaker lever will appear; flip it, and another cutscene will play.  CIRCUIT #2
has now been activated!  It's time to head back to the mermaid village and
report on your success; however, there are a few (optional) things we can do on
the way back.

First up, leave the boss room and drop down from the spire until you're back at
the plaque in the room below.  From here, drop down through the narrow gap on
the right edge of the room (below the upper console), and walk through the
passage on the right (above the lower console).  You'll find yourself in a
small room, with a note in the upper-right corner.  Jump across the two lower
platforms to reach the ledge on the right, then jump up to the middle platform.
Watching out for the spiky beetles, jump up and to the left when the coast is
clear.  Run straight across the narrow gap to the left (no need to jump), jump
up onto the upper platforms, and then run straight across the narrow gap in the
middle before hopping down to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #28.  Then drop through the
gap and leave this room the way you came.

Next up, leave the plaque room through the lower-left passage, drop all the way
to the bottom of the next room, and leave it through the lower-right passage to
return to the "temple nave" (the huge room with two carriage machines).  Be
careful not to drop onto the spikes below; instead, walk along the spiky
beetle's platform before dropping off the right side to the bottom of this room
and walking over to the pair of consoles.

At this point, you have an opportunity to collect another flower before leaving
the temple.  If you don't want to do that now, feel free to skip the box below;
you can always come back and get the flower later (see Section 13).

          ,-,-,-------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,------/ / / Optional Detour: Pick up the Lost Temple Flower / / /-------,
  |     '-'-'-------------------------------------------------'-'-'        |
  | Starting from the two consoles at the bottom of the "temple nave",     |
  | enter these two programs for the two carriage machines:                |
  |                                                                        |
  |         Left-hand console                   Right-hand console         |
  |          (upper carriage)                    (lower carriage)          |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:MOVE <  | 6:MOVE <  |            | 0:MOVE >  | 6:MOVE ^  |     |
  |     | 1:TIL X=0 | 7:TIL X=0 |            | 1:TIL X=9 | 7:TIL Y=6 |     |
  |     | 2:MOVE ^  | 8:MOVE v  |            | 2:MOVE ^  | 8:END     |     |
  |     | 3:TIL Y=6 | 9:TIL Y=0 |            | 3:TIL Y=3 | 9: ----   |     |
  |     | 4:MOVE >  |10:MOVE >  |            | 4:MOVE <  |10:        |     |
  |     | 5:TIL X=7 |11:TIL X=9 |            | 5:TIL X=2 |11:        |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | The left-hand program will make the upper carriage move in a C-shaped  |
  | loop; the right-hand program is the same one we used before on our way |
  | to the temple boss.  With both these programs in place, use the        |
  | right-hand console to reset the lower carriage, then close the console |
  | window and quickly jump up onto the lower carriage as it starts moving |
  | to the right.  Once it reaches the right side of the trellis, jump     |
  | onto the ledge here, run left over the little gap (it's narrow enough  |
  | that you can just run straight over it without jumping), and stand     |
  | under the wide space between the spikes.  When the carriage approaches |
  | you, jump straight up to land on it as it slides under you, then       |
  | carries you up.  Once the carriage stops, wait for the upper carriage, |
  | which should still be moving in its C-shaped loop, to pass by, and     |
  | jump onto it.                                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | As soon as the upper carriage carries you to the left side of its      |
  | trellis and starts moving up, jump right onto the ledge, walk to the   |
  | right, and jump up through the little gap onto the floor above.  Once  |
  | the carriage slides through the gap with the spikes, jump onto the     |
  | carriage, then jump again onto the column on the right.                |
  |                                                                        |
  | Drop down from the column onto the ledge below, then jump over the     |
  | spikes onto the ledge on the right.  Now for the tricky part: you need |
  | to carefully fall off this ledge (without hitting the spikes next to   |
  | you), then immediately nudge right to land on the little platform      |
  | below.  Jump to the next little platform, then climb up and exit       |
  | through the passage in the upper-right.                                |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll find yourself on the left side of a long room, with spikes at   |
  | the bottom of a shallow pool of water.  If you fall in the water,      |
  | you'll take a hit from the spikes before bobbing to the surface, so be |
  | careful.  Jump rightwards across the various platforms, avoiding the   |
  | spikes dropped by the slime enemies above, until you see the flower    |
  | above and to the right.  At this point, jump left across two little    |
  | platforms, then up to the highest platform.  You may need to bait out  |
  | spikes from the slimes while doing this to avoid being hit.  Finally,  |
  | jump right from this highest platform to the next narrow platform, and |
  | then jump right again to reach the platform with the flower.           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you pick up the flower, don't jump down into the water!  You'll   |
  | hit the spikes in the shallow water, and the flower will break.        |
  | Instead, jump left across the platforms, basically unwinding the path  |
  | you took to get up here, and exit this room the way you came in.       |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the upper-right corner of the "temple nave", carefully jump    |
  | down the ledges and across the little hanging platforms so that you    |
  | don't fall on the spikes below.  Once you're standing on the leftmost  |
  | of the two hanging platforms, you can safely drop off the left side    |
  | and fall all the way to the floor of the nave.                         |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

At this point, with or without the flower, you should be at the bottom of the
"temple nave" room.  Exit through the bottom-right passage.  Drop down twice to
reach the bottom of this room, then exit out the bottom-right passage.  Drop
straight off the ledge here, and you'll land safely at the bottom of the temple
entrance without hitting any enemies.  Exit bottom-right, and you'll be back in
the Vine Garden.

At this point, you should return to the mermaid village.  In this long garden
room, stand on the little pedestal under the vine here, and let the vine bug
crawl down towards you.  Run right and jump right at the edge of the pedestal,
and you should be able to jump up onto the tree branch without hitting the vine
bug.  Now stand on the left edge of the branch; this should be close enough
that the vine bug will crawl down to the bottom of the vine again, at which
point you can easily jump over it to the left-hand platform, and then continue
climbing up until you reach the very highest platform above the tree.  Get a
running start and run off the right edge of this platform without jumping, and
you should land on top of the tree to the right.  From here you should be able
to work your way through the rest of this room, while avoiding the spikes.

From here, follow your minimap on the pause screen to return to the mermaid
village.  See Section 02 for details if you need to re-solve any of the machine
puzzles on the way.  When you enter the Mermaid Vale, be very careful of the
crab here, as his movements are unpredictable, and he loves to eat flowers.

Once you reach the mermaid village, start by visiting Selene in the bottom
center hut to deliver the flower, if you carried it with you.  Next, visit
Phoebe, the little mermaid girl in the bottom-left hut, to tell her about your
adventure in the temple.  Finally, visit the queen's hut in the top center of
the village, and report your success to Queen Eirene.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 04: Rescue Mission                                               ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \___________________________,        @@@@@@@@@@
 |                                         \     @@------@@@@@@
 |  [] Program the lower elevator           |    --@@@@@@--@@@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #29            |    @@--@@--@@--@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #07            |    @@@@@@@@@@@@--@@
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #5              |    --@@----@@--@@@@
 |  [] Get a little help from Corra         |    @@--@@@@------@@@@
 |  [] Return to the prison caves           |      ------@@------@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #15            |      ||@@@@@@||@@--@@
 |  [] Trap an orc in a jail cell           |    @@||||@@||||@@@@
 |  [] Check on the tunnel to the surface   |    @@||||||||||||@@@@
 |  [] Rescue your friends!                 |  @@----||||||||----@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #18            |  @@----||||||||------@@
 |  [] Deliver the PRISON CAVES FLOWER      |  @@@@||||||||||----@@--
 |  [] Report back to Queen Eirene          |    @@----||||||@@@@--
 |  [] Hatch a plan with Alex               |      @@@@--------@@--  @@@@
 |  [] Speak with your other rescued        |      @@--@@@@@@@@--      @@  @@@@
 |     friends                              |      @@@@--@@@@--        @@@@
 |__________________________________________|        @@@@@@@@--      @@@@
                                                     @@@@@@--@@--@@@@@@
After you have defeated the boss in the Lost         @@@@@@@@--@@@@@@
Temple and activated the circuit there, Queen          @@@@@@@@@@
Eirene will tell you that her scouts have
located some of your fellow imprisoned humans.  She advises you to head
straight up from the mermaid village, so it's time to do just that.

Start by leaving the mermaid village through the upper-right passage.  If you
delivered the Mermaid Vale flower earlier, you'll have already been this way;
if not, right now is a good opportunity to do so if you want to (see Section 02
for details).  In any event, head right past the hot spring, and into the next
room.  Head right, avoiding the fish.  Feel free to peek inside the hut here
and play around for a bit: now that you've activated the first two circuits,
the drum machine and trombone machines here are usable (the organ machine
requires circuit #4, which you won't be activating for a while).  There's no
puzzle to solve here, but you can use the machines to make music just for fun.

From outside of the music hut, jump upwards, then around to the left, avoiding
the crabs and one more fish, and go through the upper-left passage to reach the
elevator room.  With circuit #2 activated, we're finally able to use the jet
machine here.  Use the console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 6:        |
        | 1:TIL Y=8 | 7:        |
        | 2:MOVE v  | 8:        |
        | 3:TIL Y=0 | 9:        |
        | 4: ----   |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

This program is a bit crude, but it'll do for now; we'll be able to improve it
later in the game.  Close the console, and the jet machine will start moving
very quickly.  Wait for it to come back down, and leap aboard, but be ready to
jump off to the right: as the jet approaches the top of the room, you'll see a
page in a small alcove on the right side of the elevator shaft.  Jump over to
it and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #29.  Wait for the elevator to come back and jump
back on, and this time ride it through the passage in the ceiling, then jump
off when it reaches the landing in the room above.

Welcome to the Rust Factory!  Here you'll find metal structures and large,
abandoned machinery.  What was being manufactured in this place?  It'll be a
while before we find out.

The factory elevator room branches off in many directions, but most of them are
dead ends for now.  Climb up and exit through the upper-left passage, and
you'll find yourself in a small room with various enemies and another page at
the bottom.  Hop down, avoiding the enemies as best you can, and collect
JOURNAL ENTRY #7.  Climbing back up from here is a bit tricky; you can bait the
birds into dashing, then climb up while they're still flapping their wings and
settling back down.  Or, you can just run into them and die, and you'll respawn
back at the passage above.  Either way, back at the top of the room, jump past
the grub and exit through the left-hand passage.

In this next room, don't bother with the console: it'll be unusable until
you've activated circuit #3.  Instead, jump down, then climb up the left side
and exit through the upper-left passage.

You'll find yourself at the bottom of a tall room, with a page in the middle of
the floor.  Walk over to it and collect FIELD MANUAL #5.  Jump up to the
platform with the grub (from the right side), then jump past the grub and jump
up along the left side, and finally hop over to the console and enter the
following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 6:        |
        | 1: ----   | 7:        |
        | 2:        | 8:        |
        | 3:        | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

I know, I know, it's a very complicated program, but it does the job.  Jump
onto the little platform attached to the lower wheel and let it carry you until
you can jump to the little platform attached to the upper wheel, and finally up
to either of the two upper platforms with grubs.  Head right, avoiding the
grubs, and exit through the upper-right passage.

You'll find yourself at the bottom of a new area: the Power Core.  Swim right
across the little pond, hopping over the fish and the floating crate as needed,
climb up the right-hand side of the room, and jump into the upper pool of
water.  You should see Corra the mermaid; if she's not here, then you didn't
talk to Queen Eirene after clearing the Lost Temple, and you need to go back to
the mermaid village and talk to her before returning here.  (If you need to do
this, then unfortunately you won't be able to go back exactly the way you came.
Instead, return to the previous room with the two big wheels, drop to the
bottom, then take the bottom-left passage.  Drop to the bottom of this new room
and take the bottom-left passage again.  You'll find yourself in the "garden
crossroads" room; from here, follow your minimap or see Section 02 to find your
way back to the mermaid village.  Once you talk to Queen Eirene, start back at
the beginning of this section to find your way back here to meet Corra.)

Talk to Corra.  She'll marvel at the technology here, then release the anchored
crates so you can jump onto them and reach the upper platforms.  Climb up
through here, avoiding the grubs, and jump through the ceiling passage to the
room above.

You'll find yourself in a small room with passages leading left and right.
Climb up and take the left-hand passage to a new room, where you'll come face
to face with two of the orcs' war rhinos.  If you get too near them, they'll
charge, so be ready to jump when they do!  Cross over the floor to the left
side of this room, and jump up to the middle platform (not the very top one,
because the upper rhino is much harder to avoid).  Jump over the rhino when it
charges, then climb up the right side of the room.  Ignore the passage to the
right; instead, head left, and jump atop the column of rocks, but don't jump
off the other side of it!  There's an orc grunt ahead, and he'll give swift
chase if he spots you.  Instead, jump up and right from the rocks onto the
metal platform, climb up to the uppermost platform, then hop across to the
left, slipping through the left-hand passage without the orc seeing you.

New music, old area: you've finally returned to the Prison Caves, but this time
you're no prisoner.  It's time for a little jailbreak operation.  Start by
jumping up to the platform on your left, then jumping right from there to the
lever.  Flip it to open up the prison gate below.  Jump down and pass through
the now-open gate, then drop down.  You'll see another orc, guarding a
powered-down console.  You have no way to fight him, so you'll have to trick
him instead.

Drop down to the very bottom of the room, and head left until you see another
lever.  Jump up to it and flip it to open the prison gate above.  Now head back
right and climb up until you're ready to jump to where the orc is.  Approach
close enough to let him see you, then run away and jump back down to the bottom
of this room.  When the orc loses sight of you, he'll start patrolling back and
forth at a run.  Immediately run left and climb back up to the lever.  When the
orc runs into the open cell, flip the lever to close the gate, trapping him
inside.  If you did it right, the orc will pound fruitlessly on the gate, and
the console will power on.  Climb back up to the console, and enter the
following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 6:        |
        | 1:TIL Y=0 | 7:        |
        | 2:END     | 8:        |
        | 3: ----   | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

The lift machine will slide down and stop.  Hop onto the little rock lip at the
right side of this ledge, and from there leap onto the lift machine.  Jump left
from there and climb up to the lever, then flip it to open the upper-left gate.
That's your ticket through this room.  Jump down, take a moment to taunt the
orc if you like, then head right and drop to the bottom of the room again.
Head left, and jump onto the little platform above the spike pit.

Before you climb up, take a moment to enter the passage just left of the spike
pit.  You'll find yourself in a small room with a page up top.  If you want to
collect it, head left, avoiding the grub, and climb up the left side of the
room.  Watch out for the slime up top that will try to drop spikes on you; you
may need to bait out spikes by jumping towards it and then back again, before
jumping across while it reloads.  Once you reach the top, leap right to the
little ledge with the page and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #15.  Then drop down and
leave this room the way you came in.

Back at the bottom-left of the big room where you trapped the orc, climb up the
left-hand side of the room by jumping back and forth across the spike pit.
Pass through the open prison gate, and leave through the upper-left passage.

You'll find yourself in a tall room with a pool of water at the bottom.  We'll
call this room the "prison crossroads".  Jump across and head out the
lower-left passage.  Ta-da!  You're back at (almost) the start of the game,
where you blasted those orcs with a rocket after escaping your cell.  *DON'T*
jump down the pit here!  If you do, you'll land back in the Vine Garden, and
have to take a long trip around to get back up here.  Instead, jump over the
pit and up to the lever.  Flip it to open up the gate to your former cell.  If
you missed collecting either of the first two papers (in your cell and the room
just west of it), you can go into your cell and go get them now.  Otherwise,
jump up from the lever to the small tunnel above the rocket launcher, and go
through the narrow passage to the right.

You'll be back in the "prison crossroads", but higher up now.  Carefully drop
down to the small ledge just below, then hop across to the small platform to
your right.  Climb up from here, avoiding the grubs, and go through the
upper-right passage.  Read the sign here; finally, you've found a passage back
to the surface!  Except...the tunnel has clearly collapsed.  No getting home
from here, then.  Pick up the flower here if you want to try delivering it,
then exit this room the way you came in.

With or without the flower in hand (or rather, in hair), you're back in the
"prison crossroads", this time at the top.  Carefully jump to the upper grub's
platform when it's on the left side, hop over it as it crawls right, then jump
from there to the passage in the upper left and go through.  Run to the left,
and you'll find the other imprisoned children!  Talk to Bruno and Marie here
(you can't reach Nora and Nina above quite yet), then keep heading left until
you find Alex.  Talk to him through the gate, and he'll ask you to let him out.
There's a lever visible above, but how to reach it?

Walk back to the right and talk to Marie again.  She'll jump up and push out a
loose brick, creating a tiny foothold for you.  Jump up onto the white brick,
then leap from there to the upper prison cell.  Talk to Nora here, then jump up
onto the little lip on the left side of this ledge.  Take a running leap from
the very edge of this lip, and you should be able to make it onto the ledge
with the lever.  Flip it, and Alex will be freed.  Jump down and talk to him,
and he'll go pick the locks on the other doors to let everyone out.

After scouting ahead, Alex returns to report on the same collapsed tunnel that
you've already seen.  Without an immediate way back the surface, he'll propose
that you all return to the mermaid village to seek shelter for now.  He'll lead
the other kids down, leaving you to return there on your own.  After the
cutscene is over, run to the right and exit this room.

You're back in the "prison crossroads".  Work your way to the bottom slowly and
carefully; if you just jump down, there's a good chance you'll run into the
fish and break your flower (if you're carrying it).  Once you reach the bottom,
exit out the bottom-right passage.  You'll be back in the big room where you
trapped the orc (although the orc is gone now).  There's a shortcut back out of
this room: climb up the upper-left side to the top of the room, then head
right.  You'll end up back in the upper-right part of the room, and can head
out through the upper-right passage to return to the Power Core.

The orc here is gone too, so head right, then drop down past the war rhinos and
take the lower-right passage.  In the small room here, drop down through the
bottom passage to return to the room where Corra helped you.  Drop straight
down the left side of the room, and you should land safely without hitting any
enemies.  Exit through the bottom-left passage to return to the Rust Factory.

Head left to the upper-left corner of this room, then drop straight down the
left side to safely avoid the spikes and the grub at the bottom.  Hop down from
here, and go through the lower-left passage.  You'll be at the top of a new
room, with a page to your left.  Leap over to it from the little platform, and
collect JOURNAL ENTRY #18.  You need to get to the bottom of this room, but
there's a grub at the bottom that you don't want to risk hitting if you're
still carrying the flower from earlier.  Jump down to the platform that you can
see next to the crane.  From there, jump onto the crane itself, then jump to
the right and hug the right-hand wall as you fall.  You'll land in front of a
console on a small rise, safe from the grub.  Before we leave this room, we may
as well program the crane to make coming through here easier in the future:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 6:        |
        | 1:TIL Z=9 | 7:        |
        | 2:MOVE ^  | 8:        |
        | 3:TIL Z=2 | 9:        |
        | 4: ----   |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Note that although you can use ACT to open and close the crane's jaws, it can't
pick up you or the grub, so there's not much use for the crane in this room
other than as an elevator.  Later in the game, you'll find other cranes that
will prove useful in other ways.  Leave this crane running, and leave through
the bottom-left passage to return to the Vine Garden.

You'll be back in the "garden crossroads".  Now that we have the TIL opcode, we
could make the lift machine in this room move all the way up and down as an
elevator, but we don't need to do that right now; instead we'll wait until a
bit later in the game, when we can make the program even more convenient.  For
now, just drop down the lift shaft, then down the right side of the room to the
bottom, and take the bottom-right passage.

You'll be back in the huge room where you first met Corra.  At this point, just
follow your minimap back to the mermaid village; if you need help getting
through, see Section 02 for details.  If you're still carrying the flower from
the Prison Caves, be sure to deliver it to Selene at the bottom center of the
village once you arrive.

When you return to the mermaid village, visit the queen's hut at the top.  A
cutscene will play in which Alex argues with Queen Eirene, then leaves.  Talk
to the queen, then leave her hut.  Alex will be treading water outside, so talk
to him next.  He'll ask you to meet up with him back in the temple.  We'll be
doing that soon, but first, go visit the hut in the bottom-right corner of the
village that used to be empty.  The other human kids are there now, so talk to
each of them.  Throughout the rest of the game, Bruno (standing outside the
hut) will be able to tell you where Alex is at any given time, if you ever need
help finding him.  Marie and Nora, inside the hut, will have various things to
say as the game progresses, so be sure to talk to them whenever you return to
the village.  Finally, you can go visit Corra in the bottom-left of the
village, who will congratulate you on rescuing your friends.  We'll be seeing
more of her soon enough.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 05: The Hidden Crypt                                             ||
++===============================================================++=========++
 ,___________,                                                   ||
 | Checklist  \_______________________________,                  ||
 |                                             \                 ||
 |  [] Meet with Alex in the temple nave        |                ||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #34                |                ||
 |  [] Delve into the Hidden Crypt              |                ||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #11                |                ||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #08                |                ||
 |  [] Gain GOTO OPCODE upgrade                 |                ||
 |  [] Locate the tomb of Dr. Zoe Alda          |                ||
 |  [] Defeat DOOMGAZE                          |                ||
 |  [] Gain REST OPCODE upgrade                 |                ||
 |  [] Activate CIRCUIT #3                      |                ||
 |  [] Pick up the HIDDEN CRYPT FLOWER          |                ||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #21                |                ||
 |  [] Escape from the crypt!                   |                ||
 |  [] Meet with Corra outside the Lost Temple  |      @@    @@  ||  @@    @@
 |  [] Deliver the HIDDEN CRYPT FLOWER          |      @@  @@    ||    @@  @@
 |  [] Tell Phoebe about your latest adventure  |    @@    @@  @@||@@  @@    @@
 |  [] Speak with your other rescued friends    |    @@    @@  @@@@@@  @@    @@
 |  [] Gain SKIP OPCODE upgrade                 |    @@    @@@@@@@@@@@@@@    @@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #14                |      @@    @@@@@@@@@@    @@
 |  [] Deliver the VINE GARDEN FLOWER           |      @@    @@--@@--@@    @@
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #7                  |      @@  @@@@--@@--@@@@  @@
 |  [] Meet with Alex outside the Ancient City  |      @@@@  @@@@@@@@@@  @@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #31                |              @@@@@@
 |  [] Deliver the POWER CORE FLOWER            |              @@  @@
 |  [] Hatch a plan at the hot springs          |
 |______________________________________________|

There's a lot to do in this chapter, so buckle up.  Starting from the mermaid
village, talk to Bruno outside the hut in the lower right, and make sure he
says that Alex is waiting for you in the temple.  If Alex is still in the
mermaid village, go talk to him until he says he'll meet you at the temple.

Follow your minimap to return to the "temple nave" (the huge room with two
carriage machines).  It will be marked with an X on your minimap.  If you need
help getting there, see Section 03 for details.

When you find Alex, talk to him.  He'll marvel at all the machinery in the
temple, and suggest that you explore further underneath.  He'll walk over to
the column you couldn't jump over before and crouch down to give you a boost.
Jump on top of him, then over the column and through the passage beyond.

You'll find yourself in a small chamber with a deep pit in the middle.  Before
you jump down, be sure to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #34.  Then take a deep breath,
and jump down into the pit.  Try not to worry too much about how you'll ever
climb back up.  I'm sure it will be fine.

You'll land on a small platform surrounded by spikes.  Leave through the
passage to the right.  In the next room, you'll find a new kind of machine: a
winch.  These can move their loads slowly up and down, or drop them quickly
with an ACT instruction.  Use the console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
