        | 0:ACT     | 6:        |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 7:        |
        | 2:TIL Z=0 | 8:        |
        | 3: ----   | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

This will drop the girder platform to the bottom, then slowly crank it back up
to the top.  (The "Z" register indicates how far extended the chain is, so Z=0
means the chain is retracted as much as possible.)  Ride the platform to the
bottom, then jump out to the right.  There's a lift machine here, controlled by
a second console, but it's unpowered; you won't be able to use it until you've
activated circuit #3.  Leave through the lower-left passage.

The next room is full of spiders, so hopefully that doesn't bother you.
They'll crawl along the ceiling, and drop down on a thread when you get near,
so watch out.  Navigate through the room, watching out for the spiders and
spikes, and leave through the passage in the floor at the bottom-right.

You'll drop down into a tall room with another winch machine, this time with
many spikeballs hanging from it.  We'll need to clear a path down, so for now,
just program it like this:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:ACT     | 6:        |
        | 1:END     | 7:        |
        | 2: ----   | 8:        |
        | 3:        | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Drop your way down to the floor of this room, avoiding the bats and spikeballs,
then leave through the bottom-right passage.

In the next room, you'll see a console and another winch machine, this time
with a big crusher weight attached.  We'll call this the "crusher room".  This
winch can move sideways as well as cranking its load up and down.  You'll need
to use the winch to ride to the passage in the upper-right.  Jump down to the
console, and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 6:TIL X=0 |
        | 1:TIL Z=2 | 7: ----   |
        | 2:MOVE >  | 8:        |
        | 3:TIL X=9 | 9:        |
        | 4:MOVE v  |10:        |
        | 5:MOVE <  |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Close the console and jump up onto the winch's crusher from the left side of
the room, then ride over to the right side and jump through the passage when
you reach it.  You'll find yourself in a small room with a winding trio of
passages, filled with bats and spikes.  We'll call this the "trefoil room".
Watch out for the spikes in the floor right where you enter, and jump over them
carefully, then hop down to the center of the room.  From here there are two
exits to take: the bottom-right leads to a dead end with another page to
collect, while the top right continues onwards.

First, if you want to, you can go get that page.  Work your way to the bottom
right passage, avoiding the bats and spikes, and head through.  You'll find
yourself in a small, spiral-shaped room with more bats and spikes, and a page
at the center of the spiral.  Work your way to the page to collect JOURNAL
ENTRY #11.  Then you can either work your way back to the passage you came in
through, or if you're in a hurry, you can just jump into the spikes and die,
and you'll respawn back at the passage.  Either way, head back through the
passage to the "trefoil room", and work your way back to the center of that
room.

Back at the center of the "trefoil room", now work your way to the top-right
passage in the ceiling, and jump up through it.  You'll be at the bottom of a
tall room with several passages branching off.  If you want to collect another
page, head through the left-hand passage, and you'll be in a small room with a
big spike pit, and another page to collect.  You'll have to jump across the
pit, climb up, and then jump back across to this page, avoiding the bats.  The
easiest way to make the second jump safely is to jump while the upper bat is
flying to the right, just before it reaches the right edge of the gap.  Collect
JOURNAL ENTRY #08, then jump down and go back through the passage to the
previous room.

This time, head through the right-hand passage, watching out for the two bats
along the way.  You'll find yourself at the left-hand side of a wide room
filled with spikes, with a spider on the ceiling and another winch machine up
ahead.  Hop down to the console and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:ACT     | 6:MOVE ^  |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 7:TIL Z=0 |
        | 2:TIL Z=0 | 8:MOVE <  |
        | 3:MOVE >  | 9:TIL X=1 |
        | 4:TIL X=7 |10: ----   |
        | 5:ACT     |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Close the console and jump onto the winch's crusher when it slams into the
ground.  Ride it up and across the top of the wall, then hang on tight as it
slams back into the ground on the other side.  Jump over to the other console
(this is just another console for the same winch), then from there jump over to
the chip you see and press the B button to gain the GOTO OPCODE.  This new
instruction can jump execution to any other instruction in your program;
combined with IF, this allows you to write more complicated logic, such as a
loops or conditionals that work over multiple instructions.  With GOTO in hand,
wait for the winch to slam back down on your side of the wall, then jump onto
the crusher, and ride it back to the other side before jumping over to the
first console.  Watching out for the spider, exit back out the passage on the
left side.

We're back in the tall room from before.  If you were to head up from here, you
could find another flower to collect, but now is a bad time to pick it up,
because we've still got a boss to fight before we can leave the crypt.  Don't
worry, you'll have an opportunity to get it before we leave.  For now, drop
through the passage in the bottom-right of the floor here and return to the
"trefoil room".  Work your way back to the upper-left passage, and head
through.

You're back in the "crusher room".  There wasn't much we could do here before
other than pass through, but now that we're armed with GOTO, we're ready for a
breakthrough.  Use the console and change the winch machine's program as
follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 6:ACT     |
        | 1:TIL Z=2 | 7:GOTO 4  |
        | 2:MOVE >  | 8: ----   |
        | 3:TIL X=8 | 9:        |
        | 4:MOVE ^  |10:        |
        | 5:TIL Z=0 |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Close the console and watch the winch crusher do its work.  It will slam into
the thin section of floor from full height, damaging it.  Thanks to the GOTO,
it will do this again and again, until it breaks through the floor after three
hits.  Jump down the hole onto the crusher after it breaks through, then jump
over to the other console (a second console for the same winch machine), and
from there through the passage to the left.

You'll be greeted in the next room with another winch machine and another
console, this time with two nearby levers.  If you look carefully, you'll see
two thin floors that you'll have to break through, one on each side.  You'll
have to destroy both floors to reach the bottom of the tomb; however, if you
reset the machine before both floors are destroyed, they'll reset as well.
Fortunately, we can use the levers to help control the machine.  Use the
console and enter the following program (although note that you won't be able
to enter the destinations for the GOTO instructions until those destinations
exist, so you may need to leave them as "GOTO 0" at first, and go back and
change them after entering the rest of the program):

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 6:GOTO 9  |
        | 1:TIL Z=0 | 7:IF X<9  |
        | 2:IF L=1  | 8:MOVE >  |
        | 3:GOTO 7  | 9:IF R=0  |
        | 4:IF X>0  |10:GOTO 2  |
        | 5:MOVE <  |11:ACT     |
        +===========+===========+

With this program, the left lever controls whether the winch moves left or
right, and flipping the right lever will make it drop the spikeball; then the
spikeball will be cranked back up before the winch continues moving sideways.
The various IFs and GOTOs ensure that the winch won't try to move past the
edges of its track, avoiding any errors that would halt the machine.

Use the levers to move the winch to the right-hand side, and drop the spikeball
on the floor three times to break it.  Then move the winch to the left-hand
side, and give the same treatment to the other floor.  Once you hear the
puzzle-solved jingle, the floors will stay gone.  Retract the spikeball to the
top so it won't be in your way, then drop through the new hole in each of the
two floors, down to the bottom of this room, where you'll find another console
for the same winch machine, as well as a plaque.  Press the B button to read
the plaque, and learn of Dr. Zoe Alda.  We'll learn more about her in time.

When you're feeling ready, go through the door at the bottom of this tomb, and
prepare to face...

       ,-,-------------------,-,                      ,---------------------,
  ,----+ | Boss #3: Doomgaze | +----------------------+ Difficulty:   3 / 5 |
  |    '-'-------------------'-'                      | Hits to defeat:   6 |
  | This tentacled horror has its eye on you, and it  | Reward: REST opcode |
  | will dodge about the room and spray streams of    '--------------------+'
  | deadly fireballs.  Sometimes, it will slide rapidly from one edge of   |
  | the room to the other, dropping fiery embers on the way.  And all you  |
  | have to defend yourself from this onslaught is your agility, your      |
  | wits, and a machine with a giant spiky ball that you can program to do |
  | your bidding.  We just need to crank that spikeball to the top, and    |
  | then drop it right on top of this Lovecraftian nightmare's face.  Just |
  | remember that you have to drop the spikeball with ACT; simply MOVE-ing |
  | it slowly up and down won't damage the boss.                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | As usual, there are many approaches you could take.  Here are two      |
  | reasonable options:                                                    |
  |                                                                        |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:MOVE ^  | 6:GOTO 9  |            | 0:MOVE ^  | 6:TIL L=0 |     |
  |     | 1:TIL Z=0 | 7:IF X<9  |     OR     | 1:TIL Z=0 | 7:IF R=1  |     |
  |     | 2:IF L=1  | 8:MOVE >  |            | 2:IF L=R  | 8:MOVE <  |     |
  |     | 3:GOTO 7  | 9:IF R=0  |            | 3:GOTO 2  | 9:TIL R=0 |     |
  |     | 4:IF X>0  |10:GOTO 2  |            | 4:IF L=1  |10:ACT     |     |
  |     | 5:MOVE <  |11:ACT     |            | 5:MOVE >  |11: ----   |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | The left-hand program above is exactly the same as the one we used in  |
  | the previous room.  The left-hand lever controls whether the winch     |
  | moves left or right, and flipping the right lever will make it drop    |
  | the spikeball; then the spikeball will be cranked back up before the   |
  | winch continues moving sideways.  This is fairly intuitive, but has    |
  | the disadvantage that you'll need to jump quickly between levers to be |
  | able to drop the spikeball at the right moment.                        |
  |                                                                        |
  | The right-hand program works a bit differently.  The spikeball will be |
  | lifted to the top, and then the winch machine will wait in place until |
  | you flip one of the two levers.  Whichever lever you flip, the winch   |
  | will start moving *away* from that lever, and as soon as you flip that |
  | lever back to off, the spikeball will drop.  This way, you can wait    |
  | for the boss to finish moving, stand at lever at the other end of the  |
  | room, and control the winch with only a single lever.  The downside is |
  | that this program has no protections against errors if the winch tries |
  | to move past the edge of its track, so you'll need to reset the        |
  | machine if that happens.                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | Whatever strategy you choose, be sure to take your time.  You're a     |
  | sitting duck just standing still at a lever, so don't try to control   |
  | the winch until the boss stops shooting fireballs.  If you don't have  |
  | a good opening to hit the boss safely, just wait for the next movement |
  | cycle and try again.  It only takes six solid hits to send this fiend  |
  | back where it belongs.                                                 |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

Once Doomgaze has met its doom, pick up the REST OPCODE upgrade that appears.
This lets your programs pause for a short time, for example to make a platform
wait so you have a chance to jump onto it.  A circuit breaker lever will appear
on the left side of the room; flip it, and another cutscene will play.  CIRCUIT
#3 has now been activated!  That's the key we need to escape from this crypt,
so let's start working on that.

Leave the boss room, and climb back up, then exit through the upper-right
passage.  (If for some reason the spikeball above is blocking your way up, you
can use the lower console to reset it and alter or clear its program.)  You'll
be back at the bottom of the "crusher room".  If your previous program is still
in place, then just be patient; soon enough the crusher will drop down, and
then you can ride it back up.  But if you need to, you can use the lower
console here to re-enter the program from before:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 6:ACT     |
        | 1:TIL Z=2 | 7:GOTO 4  |
        | 2:MOVE >  | 8: ----   |
        | 3:TIL X=8 | 9:        |
        | 4:MOVE ^  |10:        |
        | 5:TIL Z=0 |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Wait for the winch to drop its crusher next to you, then hop on and ride it
back up to the top of the room.  At this point, you have an opportunity to
collect another flower before escaping from the crypt.  If you don't want to do
that now, feel free to skip the box below; you can always come back and get the
flower later (see Section 13).

          ,-,-,--------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,------/ / / Optional Detour: Pick up the Hidden Crypt Flower / / /------,
  |     '-'-'--------------------------------------------------'-'-'       |
  | At the top of the "crusher room", ride the crusher and leave through   |
  | the upper-right passage to return to the "trefoil room".  Work your    |
  | way to the top-right of this room and leave through the upward         |
  | passage.  Climb up past the two bats to the upper half of this next    |
  | room, jump over the little spiked walls, and avoid the spider on the   |
  | ceiling as you head up and through the upper-right passage.            |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll find yourself in a small room with another winch, and the       |
  | flower visible at the bottom-left.  Use the console to program the     |
  | winch as follows:                                                      |
  |                                                                        |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |     | 0:ACT     | 6:        |                                          |
  |     | 1:REST 1  | 7:        |                                          |
  |     | 2:MOVE ^  | 8:        |                                          |
  |     | 3:TIL Z=0 | 9:        |                                          |
  |     | 4:REST 2  |10:        |                                          |
  |     | 5: ----   |11:        |                                          |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | This will make the winch move its two platforms up and down, with some |
  | extra pauses to give you more time to make your jumps.  Jump onto the  |
  | upper platform; then, when the winch drops to the bottom, walk off the |
  | right side of the upper platform and then immediately hold Left on the |
  | D-pad to slip onto the lower platform.  Wait for the platforms to rise |
  | and fall again, then jump over to the flower and pick it up.           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Getting back up is a little trickier.  When the winch drops, jump onto |
  | its lower platform.  Wait for the platform to rise and fall, then make |
  | a short hop over to the floor to the right, and immediately walk over  |
  | to hide under the block on the right side, where the spider can't      |
  | reach you.  Wait for the spider to wander to the left, then quickly    |
  | run out and hop onto the block, then climb up the right side of the    |
  | room.  Wait for the bat to start moving up, then jump onto the floor   |
  | below it, and immediately jump onto the top platform of the winch.     |
  | Wait for it to rise to the top, then jump over to the console, and     |
  | leave the room.                                                        |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now at the top of the previous room, getting down safely from here     |
  | takes some care.  Slip past the spider and drop down to the left of    |
  | the three little spiked walls.  Trying to jump and land exactly        |
  | between the leftmost two walls is risky, but there's an easier way.    |
  | Hold Right on the D-pad to press up against the left side of the       |
  | leftmost wall, then keeping holding Right as you press and hold A for  |
  | a long jump.  You'll sail right over the first two walls and land      |
  | safely to the left of the third.  Then you can easily jump safely over |
  | the third wall.  From there, just avoid the bats as you drop down the  |
  | right side of the room and down through the passage on the right side  |
  | of the floor.                                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the "trefoil room", which is your next challenge.    |
  | Carefully avoid the rightmost bat as you drop down the right side.     |
  | When the center bat starts moving up, hop over the patch of spikes in  |
  | the middle, and hide under the little ledge here.  Wait again for the  |
  | bat to move up, then quickly slip out and jump onto the little ledge   |
  | you were hiding under.  Wait for the bat to move down, then jump over  |
  | it and climb up the center.  Finally, you'll have to make a tricky     |
  | jump over a wide patch of spikes.  If you tap the A button lightly     |
  | instead of pressing it down and holding it, you'll make a short hop    |
  | that avoids bouncing your head off the ceiling, which can make the     |
  | jump a little easier.  If you do fail the jump and break the flower,   |
  | stay here to practice a bit before heading back to get the flower      |
  | again.  Once you make the jump with the flower intact, leave through   |
  | the passage to return to the "crusher room".                           |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

With or without the flower, you should at this point be at the top of the
"crusher room".  Leave through the upper-left passage to return to the room
with the long chain of spikeballs hanging from a winch.  Use the console here
to reprogram the winch as follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 6: ----   |
        | 1:TIL Z=0 | 7:        |
        | 2:REST 5  | 8:        |
        | 3:MOVE v  | 9:        |
        | 4:TIL Z=4 |10:        |
        | 5:REST 5  |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

With the spikeballs moving up and down, you can now zigzag your way up the
room, waiting each time for the spikeballs to be in a favorable position before
crossing the center of the room.  Once you reach the top, jump up through the
passage on the right side of the ceiling.  You'll be back in the small room
with three spiders; maneuver your way through, avoiding the spiders and the
spikes, and leave through the right-hand passage.

You'll be back in the room with the lift machine that didn't work, but now with
circuit #3 active, we can finally use it to escape.  Jump over to the console
and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 6: ----   |
        | 1:TIL Y=9 | 7:        |
        | 2:REST 2  | 8:        |
        | 3:MOVE v  | 9:        |
        | 4:TIL Y=0 |10:        |
        | 5:REST 4  |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

The lift will move up and down, pausing at each end to give you a chance to
jump on and off.  Ride it to the top, and leave through the upper-right
passage.

We're almost out, but this next room is chock full of enemies.  Swim through
the pool of water to the right, avoiding the fish, and jump onto the wider
ledge and move right to hide from the bat under the short ledge.  When the bat
moves up, jump up onto the short ledge, then jump over the bat when it moves
down.  Head left and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #21 to learn something very
interesting about the mermaids and Dr. Zoe Alda, then jump up onto the two
little platforms on the columns.

Above you are two bats.  The easiest way past them is to wait until they're
synced up close to each other and moving together to the right, and then as
they are moving past the edge of the right-hand ledge, jump to that ledge and
then immediately leap up and left to the other ledge, and then up to the rocks
on the far left.  There you can catch your breath and take a look at the two
spiders on the ceiling.  Jump right and quickly past them, watching out for the
spikes, and head through the upper-right passage.

You'll be back in the Lost Temple.  Climb up and over the column blocking your
way, and leave through the passage to the right to return to the temple
entrance.  (This is a good time to mention, by the way, that if you ever need
to return to the crypt, go back to the "temple nave" where you met up with
Alex.  He won't be there anymore, but he'll have left behind a crate you can
jump onto to get over the column blocking your way back to the crypt entrance.)

Drop down the left edge of this tall room to safely avoid the enemies.  Ahead
you'll see Corra the mermaid.  Talk to her (it doesn't matter how you answer
her question), and she'll tell you that Alex is waiting for you elsewhere, and
put a mark on your minimap.  We'll need to head there to meet him, but we have
a few things to do along the way.

Leave through the bottom-right passage to return to the Vine Garden.  Pass
through the long room here (see Section 03 if you need help doing this safely
while carrying a flower) to return to the bottom of the "garden crossroads"
room.  If you want to, this is a convenient time to reprogram this lift machine
to act as an elevator, while still letting you use it to squish the grub when
needed:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 6:IF L=0  |
        | 1:TIL Y=9 | 7:GOTO 0  |
        | 2:REST 1  | 8:REST 0  |
        | 3:MOVE v  | 9:TIL L=0 |
        | 4:TIL Y=1 |10:MOVE v  |
        | 5:REST 1  |11: ----   |
        +===========+===========+

With this program, the lift machine will move up and down like an elevator,
allowing you to reach the upper-right passage.  However, if you flip the lever,
the lift will stop at the bottom of its route; then when you flip the lever
back, the lift will immediately move down to squish the grub before resuming
its route.

From here, follow your minimap back to the mermaid village; if you're still
carrying the flower from the Hidden Crypt, deliver it to Selene in the
bottom-center hut.  While you're here, be sure to also talk to Phoebe, the
little mermaid girl in the bottom-left hut, to tell her about your latest
adventure.  You can also get new dialog at this point by talking to Queen
Eirene in the top-center hut, and to Marie and Nora in the bottom-right hut.

When you're ready, leave the village through the upper-right passage, and
follow your minimap around to the bottom of the elevator shaft.  It's time we
made this elevator a bit more convenient to use, so use the console to update
its program as follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF U=L  | 6:GOTO 0  |
        | 1:GOTO 7  | 7:REST 2  |
        | 2:REST 2  | 8:IF Y>0  |
        | 3:IF Y<8  | 9:MOVE v  |
        | 4:MOVE ^  |10:TIL Y=0 |
        | 5:TIL Y=8 |11: ----   |
        +===========+===========+

With this program, the jet machine will stay put until you flip one of the two
levers (either the *U*pper lever at the top of the shaft, or the *L*ower lever
at the bottom), at which point it will pause briefly (to give you a chance to
board) before zooming to the other station.  Flip either lever again to bring
the elevator platform back.

Take the elevator up to the factory.  Then climb up to the top of this room.
With circuit #3 activated, the upper jet machine is usable now, so use the
console up here and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF U=L  | 6:GOTO 0  |
        | 1:GOTO 7  | 7:REST 2  |
        | 2:REST 2  | 8:IF Y<9  |
        | 3:IF Y>0  | 9:MOVE ^  |
        | 4:MOVE v  |10:TIL Y=9 |
        | 5:TIL Y=0 |11: ----   |
        +===========+===========+

This program works similarly to above, but is tailored for this other elevator.
We'll be riding up this elevator soon, but we have a couple other things to
collect first, so head left through the passage to the left of this upper
console.  You'll be in a small room you've visited before; hopefully you
already collected the page below, so you can just jump across and leave through
the left-hand passage.  *Don't* jump down in the next room; first, jump over to
the console, which is usable now that we've activated circuit #3.  (If you do
jump down by accident, follow your minimap back around to the mermaid village,
and then back up to the elevator room so you can get back here.)  Use the
console to enter the following very complicated program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 6:        |
        | 1: ----   | 7:        |
        | 2:        | 8:        |
        | 3:        | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Hey, if it works, it works.  With this program in place, not only will you
finally have a way to traverse this room from left to right in the future, but
the way down below will also be unblocked.  Jump down from the console and down
into the gap.  From here you can go left or right, but if you go left you won't
be able to climb back up from here, so let's go right first.  Drop down,
avoiding the grub enemies, and exit through the bottom-right passage.

You'll be in a small room with a new upgrade!  Climb up to it and press the B
button to gain the SKIP OPCODE.  This instruction is similar to GOTO, but
instead of jumping to a specific address, it skips forward by a certain number
of instructions.  The real power of SKIP is that the number of instructions can
be a register instead of a constant, which allows for some clever uses.  That
said, it can be tricky to use well, and we won't be using it much in this
walkthrough.

From here, you have a choice.  If you want to collect another flower, you can
return through the left-hand passage and take the bottom-left passage from the
previous room.  If you don't want to do that right now, then jump down the
right side of the SKIP room and take the passage there to return to the
elevators, in which case you can skip past the box below; you can always get
the flower later on (see Section 13).

          ,-,-,-------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,------/ / / Optional Detour: Deliver the Vine Garden Flower / / /-------,
  |     '-'-'-------------------------------------------------'-'-'        |
  | Having just picked up the SKIP opcode, take the left-hand passage.     |
  | (Or, if you're in the Rust Factory elevator room, take the upper-left  |
  | passage, pass through the next room, make sure the rotor machine is    |
  | moving, then drop down.)  Then take the bottom-left passage in the     |
  | tall room with the rotor machine in the middle.                        |
  |                                                                        |
  | You find yourself back in the Vine Garden, in a small room with a      |
  | flower in the top-right and a page in the bottom-left.  Being careful  |
  | not to fall into the narrow pit in the middle of the room, hop over to |
  | the page and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #14.  Then head back right and jump |
  | onto the bottom-right tree branch.  Watching out for the enemies, jump |
  | leftwards until you reach the little ledge at the top-left.  Wait for  |
  | the uppermost grub to be on the right side of its treetop, then jump   |
  | up there with it.  Hop over it carefully as it moves left, then stand  |
  | on the rightmost edge of the treetop; this will goad the vine bug into |
  | sliding down, allowing you to jump over it to the other treetop.  From |
  | here it's a simple matter to walk over and jump up to the flower.      |
  |                                                                        |
  | Unfortunately, we can't go back the way we came, so you'll have to     |
  | jump down through the narrow pit at the bottom of the room.  You'll    |
  | land back in the large room where you first met Corra, way back near   |
  | the start of the game.  From here, you can just follow your minimap    |
  | back to the mermaid village; see Section 02 if you need help getting   |
  | back.  Deliver the flower to Selene in the bottom-center hut.          |
  |                                                                        |
  | With the flower safely delivered, leave the mermaid village through    |
  | the upper-right passage, follow your minimap back to the Mermaid Vale  |
  | elevator room, and take the elevator back up to the Rust Factory to    |
  | continue the walkthrough.                                              |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

Whether or not you delivered the Vine Garden flower, at this point you should
be back in the Rust Factory elevator room.  It's finally time to take the upper
elevator up.  Go to the top of this room, and flip the lever to call the
platform down if necessary; then flip the lever and board the elevator to ride
it up to the Power Core.  On the way up, you'll see a page in a small alcove on
the left side of the shaft, so you can jump off and grab it to collect FIELD
MANUAL #7.  (If you miss it, just ride the elevator down and up and try again.)
Once you've collected it, jump down the shaft and use the lever again to call
the platform and ride it back to the very top.

It's finally time to go meet up with Alex.  At the top of the elevator in the
Power Core, head through the right-hand passage.  You'll be at the bottom of a
tall room filled with tall, narrow platforms, and bird enemies waiting to knock
you off.  The best way up, for the most part, is to jump up quickly and
confidently.  If you do it right, the first two birds will rush at you just
before you reach them, and you can keep jumping past them while they're still
flapping their wings and settling back down.  When you're almost at the top,
the third bird will fly to the right as you jump onto the upper-left-most
platform.  You may need to jump at it to bait it to fly to the left, so that
you can jump past and through the upper-right passage.

You'll find Alex in the next room.  Jump up and speak to him.  You'll share
with him what you learned in the crypt, and he'll show you the view of the
ruined city ahead, which unfortunately you can't reach just yet.  But don't
worry, Alex has a plan!  He'll ask you to meet him back in the village, and
then leave.  Follow him out the passage, drop down the right-hand side of the
tall room past the birds, and return to the top of the Power Core elevator.

Before we return to the mermaid village, there's one more flower we can grab on
the way, if you're interested.  If not, just skip the box below; you can always
get the flower later (see Section 13).

           ,-,-,------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,-------/ / / Optional Detour: Pick up the Power Core Flower / / /-------,
  |      '-'-'------------------------------------------------'-'-'        |
  | Take the left-hand passage from the top of the Power Core elevator     |
  | room, then drop down the right-hand side of the tall room you find     |
  | yourself in.  At the bottom, you'll find a flower and a page.  Pick up |
  | the flower, then walk over and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #31.  Jump up     |
  | onto the platform with the two spiky beetles, and carefully jump over  |
  | one before jumping onto the ledge on the right.  From here, jump up    |
  | onto the first of two platforms with a giant toad on it.  Wait for it  |
  | to jump, then immediately run underneath, and wait for the last moment |
  | to jump from the leftmost edge of the platform, so you don't hit the   |
  | toad as you jump up to the ledge on the left.  Next, jump onto the     |
  | second toad platform.  This time, it is probably easier to jump over   |
  | the toad to the next platform, rather than trying to run underneath    |
  | it; just make sure you jump immediately after it lands, so you don't   |
  | risk it jumping again.  From here, climb the rest of the way up,       |
  | avoiding the last two spiky beetles, and leave through the top-right   |
  | passage to return to the elevator room.                                |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

With or without the flower, you should be at the top of the Power Core
elevator.  From here, it's easy; just ride the elevator down, then ride the
lower elevator back down to the Mermaid Vale.  Follow your minimap back around
to the mermaid village, being careful of the enemies along the way.

You'll run into Alex at the hot springs just east of the mermaid village.  If
you're still carrying the flower, be sure to go deliver it to Selene in the
bottom-center hut of the village first, then return and talk to Alex.  He's got
an idea of how you can reach the blocked off city ruins, but as usual, you're
going to have to do all the dirty work.  He'll repair the console here, and ask
you to descend below the hot springs and find a passage up from below the city.
It's a solid plan, but it's going to have to wait for the next section of this
walkthrough.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 06: Into the Pits                                                ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \______________________,
 |                                    \
 |  [] Drain the hot springs           |
 |  [] Gain COPY OPCODE upgrade        |                  OO
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #4         |
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #10       |                OO      OO
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #27       |        OOOO    OOOOOO  OO    OO
 |  [] Break some chains               |          OOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO  OO
 |  [] Blow off some steam             |          OOOO@@OOOOOO@@OOOOOOOO
 |  [] Defeat PYROFUGE                 |        OOOO@@@@@@@@@@@@@@OO@@OOOO
 |  [] Gain 3rd PROGRAM RAM upgrade    |      OOOO@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@OO
 |  [] Activate CIRCUIT #4             |        OO@@@@@@----@@----@@@@@@OO
 |  [] Ignore the flower here for now  |        OO@@@@@@@@--@@@@--@@@@@@OO
 |  [] Enter the Salt Mines            |        OOOO@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@OOOO
 |_____________________________________|            OO@@@@@@@@@@@@@@OOOO

If you're in the mermaid village, leave through the upper-right passage to find
Alex waiting for you at the hot springs.  (If he's not there, you probably
didn't meet him at the city outskirts yet; check for an X on your minimap.)
Talk with him if you haven't already, and he will repair the console.  Use it
and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 6:        |
        | 1:TIL Y=0 | 7:        |
        | 2:MOVE ^  | 8:        |
        | 3: ----   | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

This will pump out most of the water from the spring, and then move the water
level up and down at the bottom, so you can reach the lever that's down there.
A word of warning, however: once you descend below the spring, you'll be stuck
down there for a while.  If there's anything you want to do first, like collect
any flowers or pages that you missed earlier, feel free to do that now;
otherwise, you can do those things later on, when you eventually return to the
village.

With the pump program in place, drop down to the bottom of the spring.  You'll
see a lever there, mounted to the underside of a small ledge.  Swim in the
water just below the lever, and when the water carries you up, press the B
button to flip the lever.  The bottom of the spring will detonate, and you'll
fall down the shaft to land in...

...the Volcanic Pit.  Here you'll find fiery enemies, and at the bottom, lava
that will kill you instantly on contact.  So, you know, watch out for that!

Feel free to peek inside the passage to the left here, but there's nothing we
can do in there yet.  Instead, drop down the left edge of this big room.
You'll see big fireballs jumping out of the lava, and blobs of fire
("hotheads") crawling around on the ledges above.  Hotheads will drop fiery
embers down when you're below them, so keep your wits about you as you head to
the right, jumping over the gaps and avoiding all the threats around you.
Leave through the passage in the bottom-right.

You'll be in a small room with a new kind of machine, as well as a page in
plain sight.  Unfortunately, you can't reach the page without the help of the
machine, and you can't make productive use of the machine until you earn a new
kind of instruction.  We'll be doing that soon, but for now, just pass straight
through this room.

Now you'll be at the bottom of a tall room with more enemies.  Hop rightwards
across the gaps, watching out above and below you, then jump up along the
various platforms until you reach the top of the room.  There you'll find
another new kind of machine, but we still need that new instruction to be able
to do very much with it.  For now, leave through the upper-left passage; don't
worry, we'll be back soon.

Now you'll be at the bottom of another tall room, with spikes at the bottom and
four hotheads crawling above.  Climb up through this room, avoiding the enemies
and the embers they'll rain down on you, and leave through the upper-right
passage.  In the next room, you'll see a spider on the floor below that will
rush towards you.  Jump over it and past it, and then run right and climb up
before it catches you.  Jump down on the right-hand side to see another
machine, but first, grab that upgrade to gain the COPY OPCODE.  This new
instruction is the key to using the machines we've been passing by so far: it
allows us to change the value of certain machine registers, such as the
position of a valve (V) or of a lever (L).  We'll put it to use immediately, by
using the nearby console to enter the following program (note that that first
instruction is using your new COPY opcode):

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:V=2     | 6:        |
        | 1:ACT     | 7:        |
        | 2:REST 2  | 8:        |
        | 3: ----   | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Valves can be a little confusing, so here's how they work: pipes lead into and
out of the valve, and the center line of the valve wheel acts as a barrier.  So
if steam enters the valve from one pipe, it can exit through any other pipes
that are on the same side as the center line, but not through pipes on the
opposite side.  The V register controls the position of the valve, with 0
meaning the center line is straight up and down, and increasing V by 1 rotates
the center line clockwise by 45 degrees (one eighth of a full turn).  So,
COPYing 2 into V in the above program will turn the valve clockwise 90 degrees
(one quarter of a full turn) from its starting position, making the center line
horizontal.  Then, when the boiler machine ACTs, the steam can flow through the
right-hand pipe, emitting a vertical jet from the floor above.

With the steam puffing away, climb up from the console, and stand on the pipe.
The steam won't hurt you, but it'll boost you up really high (note there's no
need to press the A button to jump; just stand there and let the steam blast
you into the air).  Wait until just after it sends you flying, then hold Right
on the D-pad, and you should land on the platform with the page so you can
collect FIELD MANUAL #4.  As this page explains, the COPY opcode also makes
available a new "A" register on every machine.  This register always starts out
as zero, and COPYing to it doesn't do anything; however, you can use it in your
program as a spare variable to save a value for later.  We'll be using the A
register later on in the game.

Jump back to the pipe, and let the steam throw you upwards again; this time,
hold Left to get back up on the high cliff.  Head left, jump over the spider,
and leave through the left-hand passage.  Back in the previous, tall room, drop
down either edge of the room, then leave through the bottom-left passage.

You'll be in the upper-right pocket of the huge room that you fell into when
you first entered the Volcanic Pit.  There's another boiler machine you'll need
to make use of here, so use the console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF L=1  | 6:GOTO 0  |
        | 1:GOTO 7  | 7:V=1     |
        | 2:IF D≠1  | 8:ACT     |
        | 3:GOTO 0  | 9:L=0     |
        | 4:V=2     |10: ----   |
        | 5:ACT     |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Note the "NOT equals" sign in instruction #3, and remember that instructions
like "V=2" are using your new COPY opcode.  Beyond that, there's a couple other
things going on here.  First, we can assign to the L register to flip the
lever!  This can be handy to automatically reset a lever after detecting that
you've flipped it.  Second, the D register we're using in this program is
connected to the distance sensor that you can see to the left of the console.
Note the little double-line icon on it; a handful of other machines in the game
(like the crane machine we saw in the Rust Factory earlier) have such sensors
with a D register, so keep an eye out for them.  They detect how far away is
the nearest object in their line of sight, whether that's you, or an enemy, or
a boulder, or something else.

For this program, we've set it up so that the distance sensor will notice when
you step on the bottom pipe, and boost you up; and flipping the lever atop the
upper pipe will boost you from there.  Just watch out for the hothead enemy as
you climb up with the help of the steam pipes.  Once you're at the top, you can
take several careful hops to the right to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #10.  Then hop
back to the left to the edge of the cliff.  Drop down and hold Right on the
D-pad to hug the side of the cliff.  Keep holding Right, and you should land
safely, avoiding the lava below.  Then leave this room through the bottom-right
passage.

You'll be back in the small room with the note that we couldn't get earlier.
Now that you have the COPY opcode, we can make use of the boiler machine here.
Use the console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:V=1     | 6:        |
        | 1:TIL L=1 | 7:        |
        | 2:ACT     | 8:        |
        | 3:REST 4  | 9:        |
        | 4: ----   |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Unfortunately, V=1 is the only valve position that will allow steam to reach
the bottom pipe, and it also allows steam to exit the upper pipe, which will
tend to throw you into the spikes above.  So this program is set up so that the
boiler will only fire when the lever is flipped to 1, so that the upper pipe
won't always be blowing steam.  With this program in place, walk past the upper
pipe and flip the lever on to start the boiler firing.  Carefully drop down
onto the pipe sticking out of the lava between steam blasts, and wait patiently
as the steam repeatedly blows you straight up.  Get the hang of the rhythm, and
wait for an opportunity to boost up to the ledge with the note when the big
fireball isn't in your way; you'll need to press Right on the D-pad just as the
steam is blowing you upwards.  Once you make it on the the ledge, go ahead and
collect JOURNAL ENTRY #27.  Jump back onto the pipe head, and let it boost you
up onto the little rock platform, then jump up the the lever and flip it to
turn the steam back off, and leave through the right-hand passage.

You'll be back at the bottom of the tall room with the crates hanging from
chains.  Jump across to the right, and carefully climb up to the top of the
room.  Now that we have the COPY opcode, we can make proper use of this
machine.  Use the console, and start with the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 6:        |
        | 1:TIL X=1 | 7:        |
        | 2:ACT     | 8:        |
        | 3:END     | 9:        |
        | 4: ----   |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

The blaster machine will move to just above the little mirror on a tripod and
fire; the blast will bounce off the mirror and break the chain, allowing the
crate on the right to drop.  (If you want, you can jump onto the crate and peek
through the passage above it, but you'll run into a dead end in the next room
for now.)  Next, use the console to alter the program a bit:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 6:        |
        | 1:TIL X=1 | 7:        |
        | 2:M=3     | 8:        |
        | 3:ACT     | 9:        |
        | 4:END     |10:        |
        | 5: ----   |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Setting the M register to 3 will rotate the mirror to a different position, so
this time the blast will bounce to the left and break the chain, allowing the
crate on the left to drop.  In general, increasing M by 1 will rotate the
mirror 12.5 degrees clockwise (1/16 of a full turn).  If you want, you can
experiment by changing the value of M in the above program to see all the
different directions in which the mirror can bounce the blaster shot.

There's one more crate to drop, but this one's going to be a bit more
complicated to reach.  You may have noticed earlier that there are two more
mirrors at the bottom of this room.  The M register controls all three mirrors,
and we'll have to bounce the blast all around the room to hit the last chain.
We'll want to use the lever to help us, but that hothead enemy is going to
cause us a lot of trouble, so let's get rid of it first.  Use the console to
enter a new program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 6:        |
        | 1:ACT     | 7:        |
        | 2:GOTO 1  | 8:        |
        | 3: ----   | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

Close the console and wait.  You may have to wait for a bit for the hothead to
be in the right position, but eventually it should get hit by one of the blasts
and go poof.  That should make things easier.  Use the console one last time,
and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE >  | 6:TIL L=0 |
        | 1:REST 0  | 7:M=8     |
        | 2:TIL L=1 | 8:GOTO 1  |
        | 3:M=3     | 9: ----   |
        | 4:ACT     |10:        |
        | 5:REST 0  |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

The blaster machine will move into position and await your command; the "REST 0
TIL L=1" part will make it wait for you to flip the lever.  Flip the lever, and
the blaster will shoot a blast downward towards the lower-right mirror.  Wait
for the blast to bounce once, then flip the lever again; that will turn the
mirrors so that the lower-left mirror will bounce the blast up diagonally
towards the chain.  Now jump of the way quick, because the blast is coming
right towards you!  If you did all this right, the blast should break the
chain, and the last crate should fall.  If you mess up, just flip the lever
again to try again.  Once the crate has dropped, jump onto it, then up through
the passage on the right.

You'll be in a new, huge room.  There's a lot going on in here.  For starters,
jump across the lava pits, avoiding the giant fireballs, and head all the way
to the bottom-right side of the room.  You'll see a number of consoles and
pipes and machines along the way, and at the far right side, a wide passage
that we can't quite reach.  That's our goal, but we'll have to do some work to
get up there.  First step: head back to the left, and then jump up and left to
the console perched by itself on the tiny rock platform, and enter the
following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF L=0  | 6: ----   |
        | 1:V=2     | 7:        |
        | 2:IF L=1  | 8:        |
        | 3:V=0     | 9:        |
        | 4:ACT     |10:        |
        | 5:REST 2  |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

This will make the boiler machine above shoot steam out of the sideways pipe to
your left, but will also give you the option of redirecting the steam using the
lever in the top-left corner, so that you can get past that otherwise dead end
in the future.  For now, jump up to the ledge on the left.  You'll need to
stand right in front of the pipe, and the moment it blasts steam at you, hold
Right on the D-pad and jump as you reach the edge of the ledge.  If you do it
right, the speed boost from the steam will let you jump super far and reach the
ledge on the right.

From here, jump up to the next console.  Notice the pipes and boiler tanks
nearby that have no boiler unit attached.  Instead, you'll need to use the
blaster machine controlled by this console to blast those tanks, creating the
heat necessary to shoot steam out of the pipes.  Let's start by getting a
better look around.  Enter the following program in the console:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 6:L=0     |
        | 1:TIL X=2 | 7:GOTO 2  |
        | 2:M=8     | 8: ----   |
        | 3:IF L=0  | 9:        |
        | 4:GOTO 2  |10:        |
        | 5:ACT     |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

This program will make the blaster move into position above the mirror, then
wait for you to flip the lever.  When you do, it will fire a blast, and the
mirror will direct it towards the boiler tank.  You'll need to step out of the
way to the left, then step back over the pipe just before the blast hits the
tank, so that you'll get boosted up to the ledge with the other lever.  From
here, head right a bit, and you'll see two more mirrors, as well as a boiler
tank with a pipe leading downwards.  That's the tank we'll have to hit with the
blaster in order to help us reach the bottom-right passage we saw earlier.
You'll also see a passage at the top-right; feel free to peek in there, but the
lift machine in there won't work until we activate the next circuit.

Now that we've seen the mirror layout, we can solve this puzzle.  Jump back to
the left, hop back down to the blaster machine console, and modify the program
as follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 6:M=0     |
        | 1:TIL X=2 | 7:REST 2  |
        | 2:M=8     | 8:M=6     |
        | 3:IF L=U  | 9:REST 2  |
        | 4:M=7     |10:GOTO 2  |
        | 5:ACT     |11: ----   |
        +===========+===========+

This program will cause the blaster to shoot at the mirror, and the mirrors
will automatically bounce the blast around to hit the boiler tank on the other
side.  If you want to see all that in action, you can flip the bottom lever to
redirect the blasts at the boiler tank below the lever, letting you boost
yourself up, and then you can flip the upper lever to return the blasts to
their previous three-mirror ricochet path so you can watch it happen.  Every
few seconds, another blast should hit the tank, sending steam out of the
sideways pipe below; if it's not working, go back to the console and
double-check your program.

Once everything is running smoothly, drop down to the rock mesa below the
console, and head right to the console in the bottom-right corner of the room.
You should see the sideways pipe, now above you, periodically emitting steam
thanks to the blaster machine's work.  That will give us the sideways speed we
need to enter the passage on the right, but we still need vertical speed.  Use
the console here to enter one more program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:REST 0  | 6:        |
        | 1:TIL L=1 | 7:        |
        | 2:ACT     | 8:        |
        | 3:L=0     | 9:        |
        | 4: ----   |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

With this program in place, the boiler machine to the left will boost you
upwards whenever you flip the lever.  Stand at the lever, and watch the mirror
above you.  It will face up-and-to-the-right, then turn way to the left, then
turn back again.  When it turns back, flip the lever and fly upwards.  If you
timed it right, you'll be in front of the sideways pipe just as it blasts
steam; when this happens, hold Right on the D-pad.  If all goes well, you'll go
flying through the passage into the next room.  If not, try again; it will
probably take a few tries before you get it.

In the next room, you'll see a spider running back and forth up ahead.  Instead
of tangling with it, jump to the floor above it, and up to the console.  How
can we get rid of the spider?  Notice the distance sensor down there?  Use the
console and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:V=0     | 6:        |
        | 1:IF D=2  | 7:        |
        | 2:ACT     | 8:        |
        | 3: ----   | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

This program will wait until the spider is just over the left-hand pipe, the
the steam will blast it into the spiked ceiling, killing it.  Now we just need
to change the program to help you get through there safely.  Use the console
again, and change the program as follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:V=2     | 6:        |
        | 1:IF D=8  | 7:        |
        | 2:ACT     | 8:        |
        | 3: ----   | 9:        |
        | 4:        |10:        |
        | 5:        |11:        |
        +===========+===========+

With this program in place, head left from the console, hop down to the chamber
where the spider was, and walk over to stand on the right-hand pipe.  When the
distance sensor detects you standing there, the steam will blast you up into
the air, allowing you to land on top of the right-hand wall.  From there, hop
down to the doorway on the right.  You probably know what's next.  It's time
for...

       ,-,-------------------,-,                      ,---------------------,
  ,----+ | Boss #4: Pyrofuge | +----------------------+ Difficulty:   4 / 5 |
  |    '-'-------------------'-'                      | Hits to defeat:   6 |
  | This armored arachnid is a significant step up in | Reward: RAM upgrade |
  | difficulty from the previous bosses: its attacks  '--------------------+'
  | are much harder to dodge, and there are now two machines you'll have   |
  | to use to help you win this fight.                                     |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pyrofuge will scuttle around the back wall of the room, and then use   |
  | one of three attacks.  First, it will crawl down close to the floor,   |
  | and breathe out a wall of flame.  After a short delay, this wall will  |
  | split in two, sweeping sideways in either direction.  The flames are   |
  | too tall to jump over, and the bottom will still hit you even if you   |
  | stand at one of the two consoles and duck, so the only way to avoid    |
  | them without getting hurt is to use the steam jets to leap high over   |
  | them.  The second attack is that Pyrofuge will charge up and then fire |
  | a large arc of fireballs; these are a bit easier to dodge, but if you  |
  | got hit with the flame wall then these may well finish you off.        |
  | Finally, at certain points throughout the fight, the boss will climb   |
  | up high and drop an egg that hatches into a smaller spider.  Just like |
  | in the previous room, you can use the steam jets to blast this spider  |
  | into the ceiling.                                                      |
  |                                                                        |
  | As for actually fighting back, there's a blaster machine on the        |
  | ceiling.  The boss's only weak point is the red blob on its tail,      |
  | which is exposed when it opens its jaws.  So you need to wait for that |
  | moment, then fire the blaster straight at the tail.                    |
  |                                                                        |
  | With two levers, a distance sensor, and two machines to control, there |
  | are many strategies you could use for programming the machines.  Here  |
  | is one possibility:                                                    |
  |                                                                        |
  |         Left-hand console                   Right-hand console         |
  |             (blaster)                            (boiler)              |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:A=D     | 6:MOVE >  |            | 0:IF R=1  | 6:V=1     |     |
  |     | 1:IF A=9  | 7:GOTO 0  |            | 1:GOTO 6  | 7:ACT     |     |
  |     | 2:GOTO 8  | 8:IF L=R  |            | 2:IF L=0  | 8:REST 3  |     |
  |     | 3:IF X>A  | 9:ACT     |            | 3:GOTO 0  | 9:L=0     |     |
  |     | 4:MOVE <  |10: ----   |            | 4:V=0     |10:R=0     |     |
  |     | 5:IF X<A  |11:        |            | 5:GOTO 7  |11: ----   |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | Take a look at the boiler program on the right first.  It will wait    |
  | for you to flip one of the two levers, and when you do, it will send   |
  | steam through the pipe on that side.  So when the boss breathes a      |
  | flame wall, you can run to the nearest lever, wait until just before   |
  | the flames start sweeping toward you, then flip the lever and step     |
  | onto the pipe to be thrown upwards, over the flames.                   |
  |                                                                        |
  | Next, take a look at the program on the left.  It takes a reading from |
  | the distance sensor (storing it in A), then moves the blaster towards  |
  | that position, so that the blaster will always try to stay right above |
  | you.  If the distance is 9, i.e. you are not in view of the sensor at  |
  | all, then the blaster will fire.  Thus, you can walk back and forth    |
  | under the boss, and when it opens its tail, you can jump, causing the  |
  | blaster to fire.  On the other hand, this program also prevents the    |
  | blaster from firing if one of the levers is flipped; this prevents the |
  | blaster from shooting you when you're using one of the steam jets to   |
  | jump over the flames.                                                  |
  |                                                                        |
  | So with these two programs in place, you just need to (1) keep         |
  | yourself under the boss, and jump when it opens its tail, (2) when the |
  | boss starts shaking to shoot flames, move to one of the levers on the  |
  | side, and get ready to use the steam jets to jump over, (3) when the   |
  | boss drops an egg, move to one of the levers on the side, and get      |
  | ready to flip the lever the moment the smaller spider is over the      |
  | steam pipe.  After six blaster hits to the tail, this giant spider     |
  | will be squished.                                                      |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

With Pyrofuge pulverized, pick up the PROGRAM RAM upgrade that appears.  This
brings the your maximum program size up to fourteen instructions.  Once again,
a circuit breaker lever will appear; flip it, and a new cutscene will play.
CIRCUIT #4 has now been activated!  Unfortunately, you're still stuck at the
bottom of a lava pit, with no way back yet.  So we'd better get working on
that.

Leave the boss room through the door.  In the outer room, climb up and jump up
and left to the upper path, then head left and out the left-hand passage.
You'll be back in the huge room with all the steam pipes.  Head left back to
where the giant fireballs jump out of the lava, and jump up to the console here
and then up to the sideways pipe.  If your old program is still in place, it
should still be blowing steam; if not, see above for how to program the console
here.  Use the steam to make the long jump to the ledge on the right.

Up at the second console, if your prior program is still in place, you should
be able to just flip the lever to redirect the blasts to the boiler tank here,
allowing you to boost up to the upper lever; if not, see above for how to
program the console here.  Boost yourself up, then head to the right and jump
across the gap to leave through the upper-right passage.

With circuit #4 activated, the lift in this room is now operational.  If you
want, you can climb up the left side of the room and peek through the
upper-left passage, but all that's through there is another flower, and we have
no way to get it back to the village yet, so there's no need to pick it up just
now.  So, back at the bottom of the lift room, head to the console, and enter
the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 7:        |
        | 1:TIL Y=0 | 8:        |
        | 2:REST 5  | 9:        |
        | 3:MOVE ^  |10:        |
        | 4:TIL Y=9 |11:        |
        | 5:REST 5  |12:        |
        | 6: ----   |13:        |
        +===========+===========+

This will get the left moving up and down.  Close the console and head back
left, under the hothead's platform, then jump up and right to where the lift
stops at the bottom of its route.  Jump up onto the lift when it stops at the
bottom, and be sure you're centered on it so your head doesn't get crushed when
it starts moving up.  When it stops at the top, jump up and right to the top of
the column of stone.  From there you can head through the passage on the upper
right, finally escaping from the Volcanic Pit and finding yourself at the
bottom of the Salt Mines.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 07: Through the Mines                                            ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \______________________,                      @@@@@@
 |                                    \                   @@@@--@@
 |  [] Gain SYNC OPCODE upgrade        |                  @@--@@@@
 |  [] Get some treadmill exercise     |      @@@@@@      @@@@--@@    @@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #26       |    @@--@@@@@@      --@@--  @@--@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #25       |    @@--@@@@--@@@@@@@@--@@@@--@@--@@
 |  [] Defeat SALTWYRM                 |    @@------@@--@@@@@@@@----@@--@@@@
 |  [] Gain 4th PROGRAM RAM upgrade    |      @@@@@@--@@@@@@@@--@@@@--@@@@
 |  [] Activate CIRCUIT #5             |      @@              @@@@@@--@@
 |  [] Escape from the Salt Mines      |    @@                @@@@@@--
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #19       |                        @@--@@
 |  [] Gain ADD/SUB OPCODES upgrade    |          @@            --@@@@@@
 |  [] Feed the ducks                  |            @@      @@--@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Open up the factory pass        |            @@@@--@@@@@@--@@@@
 |  [] Meet with Bruno                 |              @@--@@@@@@@@
 |_____________________________________|                  @@@@@@

Welcome to the Salt Mines!  You should have just entered here from the Volcanic
Pit, and found yourself in a small room with two wasp-looking bugs flying in
circles.  Head to the right side of this room, then zig-zag your way up and
leave through the upper-right passage.

You'll find yourself at the bottom-left of a huge room.  Climb up over the wall
in front of you, then drop down its right side.  Read the sign ahead to learn
that there's some kind of equipment to collect below, then drop down through
the passage in the floor.  At the bottom of this small room, you'll find the
SYNC OPCODE.  This instruction makes it much easier to get multiple machines to
work together, which we'll be doing quite a bit of from this point in the game
forward.

Now we just have to figure out how to get back out of this room.  There's two
hoists to help carry you back up to the passage in the ceiling, but one doesn't
go down far enough to jump onto, and the other doesn't go high enough to reach
the passage in the ceiling.  So let's use the two nearby consoles and program
them to line up:

            Left-hand console                    Right-hand console
            (left-hand hoist)                    (right-hand hoist)
        +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 7: ----   |            | 0:MOVE ^  | 7: ----   |
        | 1:TIL Z=3 | 8:        |            | 1:TIL Z=0 | 8:        |
        | 2:SYNC    | 9:        |            | 2:SYNC    | 9:        |
        | 3:REST 4  |10:        |            | 3:REST 4  |10:        |
        | 4:MOVE ^  |11:        |            | 4:MOVE v  |11:        |
        | 5:TIL Z=0 |12:        |            | 5:TIL Z=4 |12:        |
        | 6:REST 3  |13:        |            | 6:REST 2  |13:        |
        +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+

With these two programs in place, the left-hand hoist will move down as far as
it can go, and the right-hand hoist will move as far up as it can go.  Then
they will SYNC, forcing each to wait until the other has reached its position,
and then REST for a bit before moving again.  This gives you a chance to jump
from one to the other.  When they're not syncing up, the right-hand hoist will
move down so you can jump on, and the left-hand hoist will move up so you can
jump out, and then they'll sync up again and repeat.  This should give you an
easy means of escape, so go ahead and climb up and out of this room.

Back in the huge room from before, head right to the console.  We need to get
up this shaft, so we'll start with a simple program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 7:        |
        | 1:TIL Z=9 | 8:        |
        | 2:REST 3  | 9:        |
        | 3:MOVE ^  |10:        |
        | 4:TIL Z=0 |11:        |
        | 5:REST 3  |12:        |
        | 6: ----   |13:        |
        +===========+===========+

Ride the hoist to the top, then jump left and head down to the second console.
This controls the trolley machine above.  We'll need to ride its platform
across, but it's too high up for us to reach easily.  Remember how we used SYNC
to get the two machines in the room below to line up so we could jump from one
to the other?  Time to do that again.  Use the trolley console to enter the
following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE >  | 7: ----   |
        | 1:TIL X=9 | 8:        |
        | 2:SYNC    | 9:        |
        | 3:REST 4  |10:        |
        | 4:MOVE <  |11:        |
        | 5:TIL X=0 |12:        |
        | 6:REST 4  |13:        |
        +===========+===========+

Console the console, and watch as the trolley moves all the way to the right
and stops.  Then wait a bit more, and watch as it...does absolutely nothing.
It just sits there.  It's waiting for the other machine in this room to SYNC
with it, but the hoist machine doesn't even have a SYNC instruction anywhere in
its program!  So let's go fix that.  Climb up from the trolley console, and
drop back down the right-hand shaft to get back to the hoist console, and
update its program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 7: ----   |
        | 1:TIL Z=9 | 8:        |
        | 2:REST 3  | 9:        |
        | 3:MOVE ^  |10:        |
        | 4:TIL Z=0 |11:        |
        | 5:SYNC    |12:        |
        | 6:REST 4  |13:        |
        +===========+===========+

Now, when the hoist reaches the top, it and the trolley will sync up and pause
together before each continuing their route.  Ride the hoist to the top, and
jump onto the trolley platform once both machines stop.  Let the trolley carry
you to the left, wait a second for the wasp to give you a clear path to jump,
and then jump left onto the rock platform.  Feel free to peek through the
ceiling passage above, but it'll be a dead-end for now; when you're ready,
leave instead through the upper-left passage.

You'll be at the right side of a wide tunnel.  There are several more wasps in
this room, as well as a pair of new enemies up ahead: bugs that fly up and
down, spitting fireballs at you as they go.  You can't reach the upper path
from where you're standing, so instead head left along the lower path.  Jump
past the wasp and over the spikes, and up onto the little ledge behind the
rocks.  Wait for the fire bugs to be out of your way, then jump up onto the
middle path, jump across the gap, and run to the right.  Wait for the wasp to
move out of your way (but not too long, or the fire bugs will blast you from
behind), then jump up, and then jump left onto the upper path.  Run to the
left, dodge fireballs as necessary, leap over the gap, and drop down the left
side of the room, avoiding the last wasp.  Whew!  Leave through the left-hand
passage.

In the next room, you'll find another fire bug, as well as some kind of metal
cage resting on the ground.  Use the console nearby, and you'll find that
there's a crane machine up above; we'll need to use it to lift the cage so we
can ride up.  Enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 7: ACT    |
        | 1:TIL D=0 | 8: ----   |
        | 2:ACT     | 9:        |
        | 3:REST 3  |10:        |
        | 4:MOVE ^  |11:        |
        | 5:TIL Z=0 |12:        |
        | 6:REST 3  |13:        |
        +===========+===========+

All crane machines, including this one, have a built-in distance sensor
connected to the D register.  In this case, we've programmed the crane to move
down until the distance to the metal cage is zero, then ACT to close its jaws
around the top.  After a short pause, it will lift the cage up to the top of
the room, then ACT again to drop it before repeating.

Jump in the cage and ride it up to the top; you may need to wait a bit for the
cycle to line up well such that the rocks will be between you and the fire bug
as you're riding upwards.  When you reach the top of the room, jump out of the
cage to the right to land on the ledge there.  Ignore the passage at the
upper-left for now; there's another flower and page to collect there, but we'll
get them later on, since we still don't have a way to take the flower back to
the mermaid village yet.  Instead, leave through the upper-right passage.

In this small room, hold Right on the D-pad to walk straight off the edge, and
keep holding Right until you land on the little ledge above the fish enemy.
Now you need to get past the fire bug.  The trick here is that fire bugs will
only turn to face you at the top or bottom of their flight pattern; they won't
turn around in the middle.  So jump up onto the top of the rocks as the fire
bug is moving up, wait for it to start moving down, and then quickly jump under
and behind it onto the first little ledge.  As soon as it moves down past you,
keep climbing up, and then jump down into the passage on the right.

The next room is a huge room filled with spikes and conveyor belts.  The
conveyor belts are currently still, but that console up ahead will let us
change that.  Head over to the console and press the B button to use it.  Once
the camera pans, you'll be able to see that this machine controls conveyors at
both the top and bottom of the room, and there are two levers (a lower one for
register L, and an upper one for register U) to help control them.  There is
also a G register; this controls the gearing (speed and direction) of the
conveyors.  Take a close look at the machine diagram in the console window;
setting G to a value on the left side of the numpad (1, 4, or 7) moves the
conveyors to the left, while a number on the right side of the numpad (3, 6, or
9) moves them to the right.  Higher numbers move the conveyors faster.  7 and 9
are the most useful gearings to use, since we'll need the highest speed to help
us jump over the large gaps.  With all that in mind, enter the following
program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF U=L  | 7:        |
        | 1:G=9     | 8:        |
        | 2:IF U≠L  | 9:        |
        | 3:G=7     |10:        |
        | 4: ----   |11:        |
        | 5:        |12:        |
        | 6:        |13:        |
        +===========+===========+

Note the "NOT equals" sign in instruction #3.  This program will make the
conveyors move quickly to the right at first, but toggling either lever will
toggle the direction of the conveyors.  This will be important later.  For now,
close the console and climb up to the bottom-left conveyor belt.  It will be
moving quite quickly, so practice intermittently holding and releasing Left on
the D-Pad so as to run in place (and get a little exercise, why not).  Once
you've got the hang of it, hold Right on the D-Pad and jump to make a long jump
to the next conveyor, then go back to running in place while you get your
bearings.  Wait for the wasp to be out of the way, then make another long jump
rightwards to the next conveyor, and then hop off to the lever.

There's another console here, controlling a hoist machine that's blocking off a
narrow downward passage.  That passage leads back to a room we've already been
to, so there's no need to go down there, but later in the game we'll be able to
use that passage as a shortcut if we program the hoist now, so let's do that.
Use the console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 7:        |
        | 1:TIL Z=5 | 8:        |
        | 2:REST 4  | 9:        |
        | 3:MOVE v  |10:        |
        | 4:TIL Z=8 |11:        |
        | 5:REST 4  |12:        |
        | 6: ----   |13:        |
        +===========+===========+

With that program in place for later, leave through the passage to the right.
You'll be at the bottom of a tall room, with two consoles nearby and a fire bug
up above.  One console controls a hoist machine that can lift you up to the top
of the room, but you'll be a sitting duck for that fire bug.  However, the
other console controls a lift machine; it can't get you to the top of the room,
but it's right in between the hoist platform and the fire bug, so you can use
it as a shield.  The two machines tend to move at different speeds, however, so
you'll need to use SYNC to help them to line up.  Program them as follows:

            Left-hand console                   Right-hand console
                 (hoist)                              (lift)
        +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 7:IF Z>0  |            | 0:MOVE v  | 7: ----   |
        | 1:TIL Z=9 | 8:GOTO 5  |            | 1:TIL Y=0 | 8:        |
        | 2:REST 6  | 9:REST 4  |            | 2:SYNC    | 9:        |
        | 3:MOVE ^  |10:SYNC    |            | 3:MOVE ^  |10:        |
        | 4:MOVE ^  |11: ----   |            | 4:IF Y<7  |11:        |
        | 5:SYNC    |12:        |            | 5:GOTO 2  |12:        |
        | 6:MOVE ^  |13:        |            | 6:SYNC    |13:        |
        +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+

The idea here is that both machines will move to the bottom, and then use a
series of SYNCs and MOVEs to move up together in lockstep, so that the lift is
always in front of the hoist platform.  Since these programs involve multiple
SYNCs, you may need to reset both machines after programming them to make sure
they're not out of phase.  You can do that by pressing the START button in
either console window to bring up the debugger menu, selecting "DEBUG" to reset
both machines and start the debugger, and then pressing the START button one
more time to exit the debugger.  Or, you can just leave the room and reenter.
Either way, jump on to the hoist platform when it's paused at the bottom, and
start riding it upwards.  You'll see a page on a small ledge behind he fire
bug; if you want, when the fire bug is out of the way, you can jump from the
hoist onto the top of the lift, then jump from there to the ledge to collect
JOURNAL ENTRY #26.  Afterwards, jump back to the lift and slip back down onto
the hoist platform (or just jump down to the bottom of the room, and ride the
hoist up again).  When the hoist reaches the top and pauses, hop onto the rock
platform to your left, then climb up to the upper-left passage and go through.

You'll be back in the conveyor belt room, this time at the top path.  If your
program from before is still in place, the conveyors should be moving to the
right, and you should be able to start them moving left by flipping the lever
in front of you.  (If that doesn't work, there must be a problem with the
conveyor program, so drop down, work your way to the console in the bottom-left
of this room, and re-enter the program from earlier in this section.  Then
follow the walkthrough from there to return here.)  With the conveyors moving
to the left, run in place on the first conveyor while you wait for the wasp to
get out of your way, then hold Left on the D-pad and jump to leap to the next
conveyor, then quickly leap leftwards again.  Hop off the conveyor a take a
look around.  There are two passages here, one to your left and one in the
ceiling.  The ceiling passage is a dead end until you activate circuit #5, but
