you may want o jump up below it anyway, because there's a page to collect to
the right.  Hop carefully across two gaps to the right, and then collect
JOURNAL ENTRY #25.  Finally, hop back to the left, and leave through the
upper-left-hand passage.

You'll find yourself in a small room with a crane, and two boulders blocking
your way.  You'll need to program the crane to move them out of the way, but
this crane is actually *two* machines; one for the crane unit, which can move
up and down, and one for the trolley, which can move left and right.  You'll
need to SYNC up the machines so they can work together.  Use the two consoles
to enter the following programs:

            Left-hand console                    Right-hand console
                (trolley)                             (crane)
        +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE <  | 7:MOVE >  |            | 0:SYNC    | 7:SYNC    |
        | 1:TIL X=1 | 8:IF A=1  |            | 1:MOVE v  | 8:ACT     |
        | 2:SYNC    | 9:END     |            | 2:TIL D=0 | 9:SYNC    |
        | 3:SYNC    |10:A=1     |            | 3:ACT     |10: ----   |
        | 4:MOVE <  |11:GOTO 2  |            | 4:MOVE ^  |11:        |
        | 5:SYNC    |12: ----   |            | 5:TIL Z=0 |12:        |
        | 6:SYNC    |13:        |            | 6:SYNC    |13:        |
        +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+

In these programs, we're using SYNCs to allow the machines to play tag team, so
that only one of them is moving at once.  Notice how the SYNCs always come in
pairs: the first SYNC effectively hands control over to the other machine, and
the second SYNC waits for it to hand control back.  When it's the crane's turn,
it alternates between picking up a boulder and dropping it.  The trolley, for
its part, moves over the first boulder, signals the crane to pick it up, moves
over, signals the crane to drop it again, and then goes back for the second
boulder.  It also uses the A register to keep track of how many boulders have
been moved, so it can END after the second boulder.

This room also has two levers in case you wanted to use them in your programs,
but we don't really need them.  Just close the consoles and watch as the crane
moves the boulders out of your way, then head left through the narrow gap the
boulders were blocking, and leave through the left-hand passage.

You'll be at the right end of a winding tunnel; carefully work your way to the
left.  You'll see lots of grubs, and burrow marks on the walls, and you'll hear
loud noises and shaking as you progress.  At the end of the tunnel, you'll find
a doorway.  Pass through to find the source of all that noise...

       ,-,-------------------,-,                      ,---------------------,
  ,----+ | Boss #5: Saltwyrm | +----------------------+ Difficulty:   3 / 5 |
  |    '-'-------------------'-'                      | Hits to defeat:   6 |
  | This enormous burrowing worm will poke its head   | Reward: RAM upgrade |
  | out of the wall soon after you enter the room,    '--------------------+'
  | and start blasting you with fireballs.  After the barrage is over, it  |
  | will retreat back into the walls before popping out at another         |
  | location to shoot at you again.  There's a crane overhead, and a       |
  | conveyor in the upper-left that delivers boulders, so it's time to see |
  | how this overgrown grub likes having rocks dropped on it.              |
  |                                                                        |
  | The crane above is the same two-machine configuration as you saw in    |
  | the boulder room earlier, but this time, we'll have to use the levers  |
  | to control it so that we can drop the boulders on the boss.  There's   |
  | more than one way to set that up, but here's one that works well:      |
  |                                                                        |
  |         Left-hand console                    Right-hand console        |
  |             (trolley)                             (crane)              |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:MOVE <  | 7:GOTO 10 |            | 0:SYNC    | 7:MOVE ^  |     |
  |     | 1:TIL X=0 | 8:IF X<9  |            | 1:MOVE v  | 8:SYNC    |     |
  |     | 2:SYNC    | 9:MOVE >  |            | 2:MOVE v  | 9:SYNC    |     |
  |     | 3:SYNC    |10:IF L=0  |            | 3:REST 0  |10:ACT     |     |
  |     | 4:MOVE >  |11:GOTO 6  |            | 4:TIL D=0 |11:L=0     |     |
  |     | 5:TIL X=3 |12:SYNC    |            | 5:ACT     |12:R=0     |     |
  |     | 6:IF R=0  |13:SYNC    |            | 6:MOVE ^  |13:SYNC    |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | With these programs in place, the trolley will move to the far left    |
  | and signal the crane to pick up a boulder; if necessary, the crane     |
  | will wait for a boulder to appear on the conveyor, then pick it up and |
  | signal the trolley to proceed.  The trolley will then move right to    |
  | the leftmost position that the boss can appear at, and stop there.  At |
  | this point, if necessary, you can flip right-hand lever to move the    |
  | crane farther to the right so as to be over the boss, then flip the    |
  | lever back to stop the trolley in place.  When you're ready, flip the  |
  | left-hand lever to drop the boulder, at which point the levers will    |
  | reset and the whole cycle will repeat.  Note that this program doesn't |
  | provide a way to move the trolley back to the left while it's holding  |
  | a boulder, so if you go too far to the right, just drop the boulder    |
  | and try again with a new one.  Just don't drop a boulder on your own   |
  | head!                                                                  |
  |                                                                        |
  | At various points in the battle, the boss will drop a grub onto the    |
  | floor to get in your way.  You can either ignore it, and just avoid it |
  | while continuing to operate the levers, or you can drop a boulder onto |
  | it to get rid of it.  Keep dropping more boulders onto Saltwyrm's      |
  | head, and after six solid hits, you'll be the early bird and this worm |
  | will be toast.                                                         |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

With Saltwyrm smashed, pick up the final PROGRAM RAM upgrade.  This brings the
your maximum program size up to sixteen instructions, where it will stay for
the rest of the game.  Once again, a circuit breaker lever will appear; flip
it, and a new cutscene will play.  CIRCUIT #5 has now been activated!  However,
we still need to find a way out of here.

Take the door back out of the boss room, and head to the right, back out of
this room.  With the boss dead, you'll no longer hear any shaking as you move
through the room.  Take the passage back to the small room with the crane, then
leave that room through the right-hand passage.

Back in the conveyor room, jump up to the ceiling passage we ignored before,
and jump up through it.  Say goodbye to the Salt Mines for now; welcome instead
to the Old Waterway!  This first grub is a bit tricky to get past; try standing
right it front of it, and jumping over as soon as it moves toward you.  Next,
climb up and past the fish enemy to the console.  This console was inoperable
until we activated circuit #5, but now we can enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 8:        |
        | 1:TIL Y=7 | 9:        |
        | 2:REST 3  |10:        |
        | 3:MOVE v  |11:        |
        | 4:TIL Y=1 |12:        |
        | 5:REST 3  |13:        |
        | 6: ----   |14:        |
        | 7:        |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

In the past we've mostly used lift machines to lift us upwards, but in this
case, we're using it to push us downwards into the water.  Fortunately, you can
hold your breath indefinitely in this game, so you don't need to worry about
drowning.  Get under the lift machine when it stops at the top of its cycle,
then let it push you down.  This program is designed to make the lift stop
before it crushes you against the floor; when it stops at the bottom, hold Left
on the D-pad to slide out from under it and end up on the left side of the
water.  Watch out for the jellyfish enemy as you surface, then climb up to the
top of the room.  The left-hand passage leads to a puzzle we can't solve quite
yet, so leave through the right-hand passage instead.

This next room is full of enemies.  Watch the movement pattern of the jellyfish
for a little bit before you try to navigate through.  Once you've got the hang
of their movement and where you can stand safely, work your way all the way to
the right end of the room, where you'll see an upgrade in a small chamber
below.  Once again, you'll need to use a nearby machine to help push you under
the water to reach that chamber, but it's a little more complicated this time.
Use the nearby console to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:J=0     | 8:TIL Y=6 |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 9:MOVE v  |
        | 2:TIL Y=8 |10:TIL Y=1 |
        | 3:REST 3  |11:J=2     |
        | 4:MOVE v  |12:MOVE ^  |
        | 5:TIL Y=1 |13:TIL Y=4 |
        | 6:J=1     |14:MOVE v  |
        | 7:MOVE ^  |15:TIL Y=1 |
        +===========+===========+

This is our first multiplexer machine; instead of controlling a single moving
platform, it controls several smaller ones.  The J register selects which
platform to move (0 for the leftmost platform, then 1, then 2 for the
rightmost), and the MOVE command moves whichever platform is currently selected
by the J register.  This program will first move the leftmost platform to the
top of its track, pause for you to get under it, then slide it down to push you
under the water.  Next it will move the middle platform up, allowing you to
slide under it, then back down to push you down again.  Finally, the rightmost
platform will do the same; slide under it, and when it moves to the bottom,
slide right again to reach the chamber with the upgrade.  Jump out and gain the
ADD/SUB OPCODES.  These new instructions allow you to add or subtract two
values, and store the result in a register (just like the COPY instruction).
If the result would be less than 0 or greater than 9, it just stores 0 or 9.
These instructions will make future multiplexer machines much easier to work
with.

With the new upgrade in hand, jump back down into the water, and bob up to the
surface on the left-hand side.  Swim over so that you're above the left hand
platform of the multiplexer machine, and wait for it to lift you to the top of
its track, then jump up and left to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #19.  Once you've
done that, head to the left, back out of this room.  Back in the room with the
lift machine, take the top-left passage that we ignored earlier.

In this room you'll find another multiplexer machine, this time controlling
five different platforms.  That's too many to control the way we did before, by
COPYing each value to J and writing different code for each one.  Instead,
we're going to use the new ADD instruction to iterate through the platforms in
a loop.  Use the console in the bottom-right to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:J=0     | 8:J=0     |
        | 1:MOVE v  | 9:MOVE ^  |
        | 2:TIL Y=1 |10:TIL Y=4 |
        | 3:IF J=0  |11:IF J=0  |
        | 4:MOVE v  |12:MOVE ^  |
        | 5:J=J+1   |13:J=J+1   |
        | 6:IF J<5  |14:IF J<5  |
        | 7:GOTO 1  |15:GOTO 9  |
        +===========+===========+

This program will lower all the platforms one by one (lowering platform #0 a
little extra, so you can jump up onto it), and then raise all the platforms one
by one (raising platform #0 a little extra, so you can jump from it up on top
of the wall on the left.  You can't fit through the narrow gaps in the floor
that the platforms move through without getting crushed, so instead, jump
across the platforms from left to right when they're in the down position, then
ride the rightmost platform up, and finally head left and wait for the leftmost
platform to raise you up so you can jump up onto the wall.  From there, drop
down into the water below (watching out for the fish enemy), and then exit this
room through the left-hand passage.

Read the sign in the next room.  You can feed the ducks by standing in front of
the bag on the ground, and pressing the B button to throw a piece of food into
the pound.  Quack!  When you're all done, head left and out through the
left-hand passage.

Pass through this next room (with an unreachable lever under the water), and
leave through the left-hand passage.  We'll be back here soon enough.  In the
following room, jump onto the rocks and flip the lever on the ceiling to open
up the pass.  Work your way past the giant toad enemies, and leave through the
left-hand passage to return to the factory elevator room.  With the pass
opened, you now have an easy route into and out of the Old Waterway.

You'll find Bruno waiting for you in the elevator room.  Talk to him, and he'll
fill you in on what's been going on while you were gone.  In particular, he'll
tell you that Alex wants to show you something, and he'll mark the location on
your map.  Sounds intriguing, but it'll have to wait for the next section of
this walkthrough.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 08: Ascending to the City                                        ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \_______________________________,              @@--@@--@@
 |                                             \           @@--@@--@@--@@
 |  [] Deliver the RUST FACTORY FLOWER          |            @@@@@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #09                |            @@--@@--@@@@
 |  [] Deliver the SALT MINES FLOWER            |            @@@@@@@@@@--@@
 |  [] Deliver the VOLCANIC PIT FLOWER          |            @@------@@@@
 |  [] Tell Phoebe about your latest adventure  |              @@@@@@@@
 |  [] Learn about Alex's discovery             |              --------
 |  [] Meet Corra one last time                 |              @@@@@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #03                |            @@@@@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #05                |          @@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #22                |          @@  @@@@@@@@  @@
 |  [] Deliver the OLD WATERWAY FLOWER          |            @@@@@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Ascend to the Ancient City               |            @@@@  @@@@@@
 |______________________________________________|            ----    ----
                                                             @@@@    @@@@
Before you go and find out what Alex has discovered,
you may want to return to the mermaid village and talk with everyone there.
Also, there are two flowers we passed by in the Salt Mines and the Volcanic Pit
that we can now go back and retrieve, now that we have a way back to the
village from those areas, as well as one in the Rust Factory that is a bit
easier to get now that we have the SYNC instruction.  If you don't want to
deliver those flowers right now, feel free to skip any or all of the boxes
below; you can always get them later on (see Section 13).

          ,-,-,--------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,------/ / / Optional Detour: Deliver the Rust Factory Flower / / /------,
  |     '-'-'--------------------------------------------------'-'-'       |
  | From the Rust Factory elevator room, take the upper-right passage.     |
  | You'll be at the bottom of a tall room with two consoles and two rotor |
  | machines.  Use the consoles to enter the following two programs:       |
  |                                                                        |
  |         Left-hand console                    Right-hand console        |
  |           (lower rotor)                        (upper rotor)           |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:MOVE >  | 8:        |            | 0:MOVE >  | 8:        |     |
  |     | 1:TIL T=3 | 9:        |            | 1:TIL T=2 | 9:        |     |
  |     | 2:REST 4  |10:        |            | 2:SYNC    |10:        |     |
  |     | 3:MOVE <  |11:        |            | 3:REST 3  |11:        |     |
  |     | 4:TIL T=1 |12:        |            | 4:MOVE <  |12:        |     |
  |     | 5:SYNC    |13:        |            | 5:TIL T=6 |13:        |     |
  |     | 6:REST 3  |14:        |            | 6: ----   |14:        |     |
  |     | 7: ----   |15:        |            | 7:        |15:        |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | With these programs in place, the lower rotor platform will move down  |
  | and pause for you to jump on, then carry you up and wait to sync up    |
  | with the other rotor.  Meanwhile, the other rotor will move down and   |
  | wait to sync up so you can jump onto its platform.  The upper rotor    |
  | will then carry you upwards.                                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll need to jump onto the ledge with the grub.  This can be little  |
  | tricky.  Unfortunately, if we made the upper rotor stop at position    |
  | T=7, then the platform would be just a little too low for you to jump  |
  | up to the ledge.  Instead, we have to move it all the way to T=6,      |
  | which means the platform slides under the ceiling, pushing you off.    |
  | So you have to jump off while the rotor is still turning, just as the  |
  | platform is sliding under the ceiling.  But the grub might be in your  |
  | way at that moment, so it might just take you a few tries.             |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you're up there, head left, jump over the spikes and grab the     |
  | flower, then jump back over the spikes.  This is another tricky jump   |
  | that may take a few tries to make without breaking the flower.  Once   |
  | you're back over the spikes with the flower intact, drop down while    |
  | holding Right on the D-pad.  You should land safely in one of the      |
  | alcoves in the bottom right.  From there, jump to the left-hand        |
  | console, and then down to the left.  Be careful leaving this room;     |
  | it's all too easy to whack your head on the side of the ledge just     |
  | above the exit passage and fall into the spikes.  To avoid that, make  |
  | a short jump over the spikes by tapping the A button briefly.          |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you're back in the factory elevator room, take the elevator down  |
  | and return to the mermaid village to deliver the flower.  Then return  |
  | to the factory elevator room for the next part of the walkthrough.     |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

           ,-,-,------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,-------/ / / Optional Detour: Deliver the Salt Mines Flower / / /-------,
  |      '-'-'------------------------------------------------'-'-'        |
  | From the Rust Factory elevator room, take the lower-right passage.     |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pass straight through this room and the next two rooms from left to    |
  | right; feed the ducks along the way if you want.  Quack!  After the    |
  | duck room, you'll need to climb up past the grubs (remember the trick  |
  | to get past them: standing right in front of them, and jump as soon as |
  | they inch towards you), then keep heading right and exit the room      |
  | through the right-hand passage.  This brings you back to the room with |
  | the lift machine that can push you underwater.  Jump up and to the     |
  | right to get to the top of the room, then drop down the right hand     |
  | side, and continue downwards to leave this room through the bottom     |
  | passage.                                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the conveyor belt room in the Salt Mines.  Drop down |
  | the left side of this room.  If you've been following the walkthrough  |
  | up to this point, you should already have all the programs in this     |
  | room and in the room to the east set up.  If not, refer to Section 07  |
  | at this point to program the conveyors, as well as the hoist and lift  |
  | machines in the room to the east; you'll want all those programs in    |
  | place before you try to carry the flower back through here.  When      |
  | you're ready, leave the conveyor room through the bottom-left passage. |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pass straight through this next room, avoiding the enemies, after      |
  | which you'll be back at the top of the room with the crane that picks  |
  | up and drops the metal cage.  If you've been following the walkthrough |
  | up to this point, the crane should still be programmed to move up and  |
  | down; if not, drop down and refer to Section 07 to program the crane,  |
  | then return to the top of the room.  When you're ready, jump from the  |
  | top-right ledge of the room onto the top of the crane machine itself   |
  | (not the metal cage!) as it's moving up, then quickly jump onto the    |
  | upper-left platform before you get crushed against the ceiling.  From  |
  | here, go through the upper-left passage.                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be a new, small room, with two hoist machines, a flower, and a  |
  | page on the left-hand side.  Each hoist has a limited range of motion, |
  | and you'll have to program them carefully to allow you to move from    |
  | one to the other without getting crushed.  Use the two consoles to     |
  | enter the following programs:                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  |         Left-hand console                    Right-hand console        |
  |         (left-hand hoist)                    (right-hand hoist)        |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:MOVE v  | 8:        |            | 0:MOVE v  | 8:        |     |
  |     | 1:TIL Z=5 | 9:        |            | 1:TIL Z=3 | 9:        |     |
  |     | 2:SYNC    |10:        |            | 2:REST 4  |10:        |     |
  |     | 3:REST 4  |11:        |            | 3:MOVE ^  |11:        |     |
  |     | 4:MOVE ^  |12:        |            | 4:TIL Z=0 |12:        |     |
  |     | 5:TIL Z=0 |13:        |            | 5:SYNC    |13:        |     |
  |     | 6:REST 4  |14:        |            | 6:REST 4  |14:        |     |
  |     | 7: ----   |15:        |            | 7: ----   |15:        |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | With these programs running, jump into the right-hand hoist cage when  |
  | it stops at its lowest position, then wait until the two cages sync    |
  | up.  Quickly step into the left-hand cage, and make sure you're        |
  | completely inside of it so that you don't get crushed when the two     |
  | cages start moving apart.  Let the left-hand cage carry you to the     |
  | top, then step out and grab the flower.  Before dropping down, wait    |
  | for the left-hand cage to move back down out of the way, then jump     |
  | left to the ledge with the left-hand hoist machine, and from there     |
  | drop down the left side so you can collect JOURNAL ENTRY #09.          |
  |                                                                        |
  | With the flower and the page collected, leave this room to return to   |
  | the crane room.  At this point, you just need to backtrack.  Hop       |
  | across the top of the crane to leave through the upper-right passage;  |
  | pass through the next room (remembering that the fire bug will only    |
  | turn around at the top and bottom of its route, so you can jump behind |
  | it as it moves down to avoid getting shot at); use the conveyors to    |
  | leap over the spikes; ride the hoist up the eastern room with the lift |
  | guarding you; use the conveyors at the top to reach the ceiling        |
  | passage to the Old Waterway.                                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the Old Waterway, climb past the grub and fish, let the lift   |
  | machine push you down into the water, and leave through the upper-left |
  | passage, avoiding the jellyfish.  Use the multiplexer machine to get   |
  | through the next room, and leave through the left-hand passage.  Feed  |
  | the ducks if you like (quack!), then keep heading left through the     |
  | next couple of rooms until you return to the factory elevator room.    |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you're back in the factory elevator room, take the elevator down  |
  | and return to the mermaid village to deliver the flower.  Then return  |
  | to the factory elevator room for the next part of the walkthrough.     |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

          ,-,-,--------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,------/ / / Optional Detour: Deliver the Volcanic Pit Flower / / /------,
  |     '-'-'--------------------------------------------------'-'-'       |
  | From the Rust Factory elevator room, take the lower-right passage.     |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pass straight through this room and the next two rooms from left to    |
  | right; feed the ducks along the way if you want.  Quack!  After the    |
  | duck room, you'll need to climb up past the grubs (remember the trick  |
  | to get past them: standing right in front of them, and jump as soon as |
  | they inch towards you), then keep heading right and exit the room      |
  | through the right-hand passage.  This brings you back to the room with |
  | the lift machine that can push you underwater.  Jump up and to the     |
  | right to get to the top of the room, then drop down the right hand     |
  | side, and continue downwards to leave this room through the bottom     |
  | passage.                                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the conveyor belt room in the Salt Mines.  Jump      |
  | across the upper platforms to the right, and drop down the right side  |
  | of this room.  If you've been following the walkthrough up to this     |
  | point, you should already have all the programs in this room and in    |
  | the room to the east set up.  If not, refer to Section 07 at this      |
  | point to program the conveyors and hoist here, as well as the hoist    |
  | and lift machines in the room to the east; you'll want all those       |
  | programs in place before you try to carry the flower back through      |
  | here.  When you're ready, leave the conveyor room through the vertical |
  | passage in the floor below the hoist machine.                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | In this large room, drop carefully down the left side, avoiding all    |
  | the wasp enemies, and leave through the bottom-left passage.  In this  |
  | small room, drop down and leave through the left-hand passage to       |
  | return to the Volcanic Pit.                                            |
  |                                                                        |
  | Jump up and left, past the lift machine here, and take the upper-left  |
  | passage, watching out for the hothead above.  You'll find yourself in  |
  | a small room bristling with spikes, with a flower on the left side.    |
  | Pick your way past the spikes to the flower, and pick it up.           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now we just have to get this flower back without breaking it, starting |
  | by getting out of this room.  Jump right twice to reach the right-hand |
  | ledge.  Next, you need to jump left onto the little platform; you'll   |
  | probably find you need to release the A button slightly early to avoid |
  | hitting your head and falling into the spikes below.  From here, jump  |
  | up and left, then up onto the top of the L-shaped platform.  From      |
  | here, make another low jump (with a short A button press) to the small |
  | platform to the right.  Next you need to jump right again, at first    |
  | holding Right on the D-pad to make sure you don't hit the ceiling      |
  | spikes on your way up and that you continue past the upwards spikes,   |
  | then reversing and tapping Left on the D-pad to make sure you don't    |
  | sail past the little platform below.  Finally, make a medium jump      |
  | (again releasing A slightly early) over the final spikes, and exit     |
  | this room.                                                             |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now, we backtrack.  Use the lift to reach the upper-right passage and  |
  | return to the Salt Mines.  Climb past the two wasps here to return to  |
  | the large room.  Climb over the wall here, then drop down and head     |
  | right, and use the hoist and trolley to travel up and left to the      |
  | ceiling passage in this room (see Section 07 if you need to reprogram  |
  | these).  Take the ceiling passage upwards.                             |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the conveyor room, take the hoist up and leave through the     |
  | passage to the right.  In the next room, ride the hoist up while the   |
  | lift machine protects you, and leave through the upper-left passage to |
  | return to the top of the conveyor room.  Use the lever to reverse the  |
  | conveyor belts, and leap across to the left side of the room before    |
  | taking the ceiling passage back to the Old Waterway.                   |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the Old Waterway, climb past the grub and fish, let the lift   |
  | machine push you down into the water, and leave through the upper-left |
  | passage, avoiding the jellyfish.  Use the multiplexer machine to get   |
  | through the next room, and leave through the left-hand passage.  Feed  |
  | the ducks if you like (quack!), then keep heading left through the     |
  | next couple rooms until you return to the factory elevator room.       |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you're back in the factory elevator room, take the elevator down  |
  | and return to the mermaid village to deliver the flower.               |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

If you haven't already talked to everyone in the village since returning from
the Salt Mines, you should do that now; a number of characters have new things
to say, including Queen Eirene, Phoebe (the little mermaid girl in the
lower-left hut), Daphne (the adult mermaid in the same hut), Marie (in the
lower-right hut), and Nora (also in the lower-right hut).  If you want, you can
also go visit the music hut that's two rooms to the east of the mermaid
village, where you will now be able to use all three instrument machines.  When
you're ready, return to the factory elevator room where Bruno is still waiting.

We're finally ready to go see what Alex has to show us.  Bruno has already
marked the location on the map, so it's time to go there.  From the factory
elevator room, take the upper-left passage.  Pass straight through this next
small room.  In the room with the rotor machine, make sure the rotor platform
is still rotating counter-clockwise (so you'll have an easier time getting
back); if not, you can program it with a single "MOVE <" instruction.  Then
pass through here and take the upper-left passage.  Climb up this next room (if
necessary, you can again program the rotor here with a single "MOVE <"
instruction), and take the upper-left passage.

You'll find yourself in a small room with a lift machine blocking the way.
This machine was previously unpowered, but since you activated circuit #4 in
the Volcanic Pit, it's now possible to get in.  Use the console on the right to
enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE ^  | 8:        |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 9:        |
        | 2:END     |10:        |
        | 3: ----   |11:        |
        | 4:        |12:        |
        | 5:        |13:        |
        | 6:        |14:        |
        | 7:        |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

The lift will slide out of the way, allowing you to proceed.  (The left-hand
console here controls the same lift, just in case you ever manage to lock
yourself inside; otherwise, you can just ignore it.)  Take the left-hand
passage.

You'll be in a wide room, with a red computer terminal ahead.  Read the text on
the terminal to get a slightly ominous status update on the factory, then keep
heading left to find Alex.  Talk to him, and learn what he has discovered about
what was being created here.  He'll conclude that you still need to reach the
city; the orcs are looking for something there, and whatever it is, you need to
find it before they do.

It's time to continue climbing through the Old Waterway, so backtrack to the
factory elevator room, and take the lower-right passage.  Pass straight through
the small room with the two giant toads to return to the room with the
underwater lever.  Only now, Corra is here waiting for you.  Talk to her.
Answer her question however you want; either way, she will decide to help you.
She'll swim underwater and flip the lever for you, which will fill the pool
above with water.  After saying farewell to Corra, climb to the top of the
room; with the water pool filled to the top, you'll be able to jump up to the
right-hand ledge, and from there climb up through the passage in the ceiling.

You'll need to move quickly in this next room to avoid the enemies.  Climb up
the left side, avoiding the two jellyfish.  Once you're standing on the
upper-left-most ledge, you can take a moment to rest.  Bait out a spike from
the slime enemy above, and jump to the top-center platform.  To get to the
passage on the right from here, wait until the right-hand slime is near you,
and no jellyfish is in the way, bait out a spike from the slime, and then walk
right off the right edge of the platform while continuing to hold Right on the
D-Pad.  You'll land safely on the ledge, and can walk through the passage.

This next room is a bit maze-like, and also full of enemies.  Pick your way
through to the right, watching out for spikes from above and crabs from below.
When you get to the center shaft, drop all the way to the bottom and head right
to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #03.  Then go back to the shaft, climb halfway up, and
head right out the right-hand passage.

You'll be at the top of a tall room.  Your goal here is the upper-right
passage, but you can't reach it yet, so start by dropping down the left side of
the room.  The passage in the floor just leads back to a room you've been to
before, so no need to go down there.  Instead, jump across to the console on
the right, and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:A=J+4   | 8:A=A+1   |
        | 1:MOVE >  | 9:MOVE >  |
        | 2:TIL X=A |10:TIL X=A |
        | 3:J=J+1   |11:IF J=9  |
        | 4:IF J<5  |12:END     |
        | 5:GOTO 0  |13:J=J+1   |
        | 6:A=J-5   |14:GOTO 6  |
        | 7:A=A+A   |15: ----   |
        +===========+===========+

Close the console, and the multiplexer machine will arrange the platforms into
two staircase patterns.  Climb the lower staircase up to the page on the wall,
and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #05.  Then climb up the upper staircase, and leave
through the passage in the upper-right.

You'll find yourself in a wide room with a number of valves along the ceiling.
At first, the falling water fills the pool on the left side of the room.  If
you explore to the right, you'll find an empty pool at the right side of the
room; to proceed, you'll have to redirect the water to fill that pool instead.
There's lots of ways to do this, including using the two levers to help control
the valves manually, but to make things simple we'll just automate it.  Use the
console in the center of the room to enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:V=2     | 8:V=1     |
        | 1:IF J<4  | 9:IF J<9  |
        | 2:V=J     |10:GOTO 13 |
        | 3:IF J=0  |11:V=3     |
        | 4:V=3     |12:END     |
        | 5:IF J=4  |13:J=J+1   |
        | 6:V=1     |14: ----   |
        | 7:IF J=6  |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

This will quickly set all valves to the correct position, and the pool of water
on the right side will fill up, allowing you to reach and exit through the
right-hand passage.

In the next room, drop down and follow the little waterfall down to the bottom
of the room, avoiding the grub, but do not exit through the bottom passage (it
just leads back to a previous room).  At the bottom, jump across to the
bottom-left corner of the room.  Climb up, watching out for spikes from the
slime enemy above, and after baiting out a spike, leap to the right to land on
top of the center wall.  This next jump is tricky; you need to jump up and
right, past the waterfall, to land on the ledge on the right, but without
bonking your head and falling down.  Get a running start and try to jump as
late as you can; it make take a few tries.  Once you make it, take the passage
in the upper-right.

You'll be in a small room with a flower, as well as a page in the top left.
Whether or not you want to deliver the flower right now, we may as well grab
the page.  Climb up to the console and enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 8:        |
        | 1:TIL Y=0 | 9:        |
        | 2:MOVE ^  |10:        |
        | 3:TIL Y=5 |11:        |
        | 4: ----   |12:        |
        | 5:        |13:        |
        | 6:        |14:        |
        | 7:        |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

The trick here is that you need the water level lowered in order to get down
and make it around the bend, but if you just lower the water level to its most
shallow and leave it there, then you'll hit the spikes when you jump in.  So
instead, we've programmed the pump to keep moving the water up and down; you
can safely float yourself down while staying at the surface of the water, then
climb up the other side.  Once up top, grab the flower if you want, then go
left to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #22.  Don't jump down from here, or you'll hit
the spikes and break your flower.  Instead, go back to the right, ride the
moving water carefully down, then climb back up to the console, and exit the
room from there.  At this point, you can either deliver the flower now, or skip
the box below and come back to get the flower later on (see Section 13).

          ,-,-,--------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,------/ / / Optional Detour: Deliver the Old Waterway Flower / / /------,
  |     '-'-'--------------------------------------------------'-'-'       |
  | This flower is fairly straightforward to deliver.  Starting from just  |
  | outside the flower room, drop down carefully along the right side of   |
  | this tall room, and take the bottom passage in the floor.  From here,  |
  | drop down the side, avoiding the jellyfish, and take the left-hand     |
  | passage.  You'll be in the room with the lift machine that can push    |
  | you underwater; just jump across the top of the room and take the      |
  | left-hand passage.  In the next room, the multiplexer machine should   |
  | still be programmed to help you get through, but if not, refer back to |
  | Section 07 to program it; then take the left-hand passage.  Feed the   |
  | ducks in the next room if you want (quack!), then keep heading left    |
  | through the next several rooms until you reach the factory elevator    |
  | room.  From here, take the elevator down and follow the hopefully      |
  | familiar by now route to the mermaid village to deliver the flower.    |
  |                                                                        |
  | To return to where you left of in the Old Waterway, return to the      |
  | factory elevator room, and take the bottom-right passage.  Pass        |
  | through this next room to return to the room with the underwater       |
  | lever.  Climb up this room and exit through the passage in the         |
  | ceiling.  In this small room, jump up the left side, climb to the top, |
  | and walk off the right side of the top-center platform, holding Right  |
  | on the D-Pad as you do, to land on the right-hand ledge and exit       |
  | through that passage.  Work you way to the right through the next      |
  | room, avoiding the many enemies, and take the right-hand passage.      |
  |                                                                        |
  | The multiplexer machine in this next room should already be programmed |
  | to give you a stairway to the upper-right passage; if not, see earlier |
  | in this section to program it.  After taking that passage, the next    |
  | room with the valves should also already be programmed; see earlier in |
  | this section if not.  With the right-hand pool filled with water, take |
  | the right-hand passage.  Finally, as before, drop down to the bottom   |
  | of this room, climb up the left side, jump across past the little      |
  | waterfall, and climb up.                                               |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

At this point, whether or not you delivered the flower, you should be standing
in the Old Waterway, just outside the room that had the flower in it.  From
here, climb up to the very top of this room, and jump up through the passage in
the ceiling.

You'll be at the bottom of a tall room filled with jellyfish enemies.  Climb up
to the the top of this room, zig-zagging your way up past all the jellyfish and
flowing water, and exit through the upper-right passage to leave the Old
Waterway.  Welcome to the Ancient City!  You'll see one of the orc's war rhinos
up above you, and if you enter the building in front of you, you'll see an orc
grunt standing guard.  Clearly, this is occupied territory: just like Alex
said, the orcs are searching for something here.  In the next section, we're
going to find it before they do.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 09: Claiming the Remote                                          ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \_________________________,                        ||
 |                                       \                     ||||||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #16          |                    ----||||
 |  [] Collect FIELD MANUAL #8            |                @@@@@@@@--||
 |  [] Connect the key generator          |              @@@@@@@@@@@@--
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #17          |            --@@@@@@@@@@@@--
 |  [] Unlock the eastern door            |            @@||@@@@@@||@@@@--
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #30          |            ----@@------@@----
 |  [] Defeat BOMBSHELL                   |            --@@@@@@--@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Gain the B-REMOTE                  |            @@@@@@@@@@@@@@||@@
 |  [] Activate CIRCUIT #6                |            @@------@@@@@@@@--
 |  [] Share your discovery with Alex     |            @@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@--
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #24          |            --@@@@@@@@@@@@----@@
 |  [] Clear a path through the city      |        @@------------------@@@@@@
 |     outskirts                          |      @@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #35          |    @@@@@@--@@||@@@@@@||@@--@@@@@@@@
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #33          |    @@@@@@--@@@@@@@@@@@@@@----@@@@@@
 |  [] Deliver the ANCIENT CITY FLOWER    |    @@@@----@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@--@@@@@@
 |  [] Tell Phoebe about your latest      |    @@@@@@----@@@@@@@@@@@@----@@@@@@
 |     adventure                          |    ||||||----@@@@@@@@----||--||||||
 |________________________________________|    --||||||----------||||----||||||
                                               --@@@@@@--||||||||||------@@@@@@
At this point, you should have just left the     --@@----||||||||----@@--@@@@--
Old Waterway and entered the Ancient City.         ------||||||||--@@@@------
Enter the doorway at the bottom of the             --@@@@--||||--@@@@----
building in front of you.  To get past the         --@@@@@@------@@@@@@--
orc guard in here, climb up on the left side         --@@@@--------@@@@--
of the room, just long enough to get the             --------    --------
orc's attention, so that he starts running           --@@@@--    --@@@@--
back and forth trying to find you.  Then
drop down, run over to the right side of the room, wait for the orc to run to
the left, and quickly climb all the way to the top of the room.  Once you're
out of reach of the orc, exit the building through the upper doorway.

You'll be back outside, but now on the upper level of the building.  From here,
you can jump up along the rooftops to climb up to the top of the room; just
watch out for the war rhinos along the way.  Exit this room through the
upper-left passage.  You'll now be in the main city center, and you'll see
another orc just ahead.  Walk up and enter the door in front of you quickly,
before the orc can give chase.  Climb up the inside of the building, watching
out for the rats; be sure to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #16 along the way.  At the
top, you'll see a locked door, an open door, and a computer terminal.  Read the
terminal, then exit through the open door.

Back outside atop the building, you'll see a new kind of machine: a semaphore
station.  There are three more of them along the buildings to the west, and
later we'll need to get them to communicate with each other.  For now, though,
just ignore it and head left along the walkways.  At the third building (where
the walkway ends), drop down one level, then look down and to the left to see
another door on top of a lower roof.  There's another war rhino on that roof,
so keep an eye out before jumping down to where that door is.  From here, you
should be low enough to see the ground, including another orc standing guard
below.  To avoid alerting the orc, jump over the war rhino and drop down to the
ground on the left side of the building, then walk over and enter the same
building at the ground level.

You'll find yourself inside a small building with three consoles, three
machines, a stack of crates, and a page on the wall above.  The next boss fight
will feature a very similar trio of machines, so this is our chance to get some
practice.  The left-hand machine is an ammo rack; it can ACT to refill its
three slots with rockets, but it's an error for it to ACT unless all three
slots are empty.  The right-hand machine is a rocket launcher; it can MOVE up
and down, and ACT to fire a rocket, but only if there's one loaded.  Finally,
the center machine is a reloader; it can MOVE left and right, and can ACT to
take a rocket from the ammo rack or put one into the launcher, as long as it's
lined up.  You'll need all three machines to work together to keep reloading
the launcher, so that you can fire rockets to break all the crates.  Use the
three consoles to enter the following programs:

            Left-hand console                    Right-hand console
               (ammo rack)                           (launcher)
        +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+
        | 0:ACT     | 8:        |            | 0:MOVE ^  | 8:GOTO 11 |
        | 1:SYNC    | 9:        |            | 1:TIL Y=6 | 9:IF Y<4  |
        | 2:SYNC    |10:        |            | 2:SYNC    |10:MOVE ^  |
        | 3:SYNC    |11:        |            | 3:SYNC    |11:IF R=0  |
        | 4:SYNC    |12:        |            | 4:IF L=1  |12:GOTO 4  |
        | 5:SYNC    |13:        |            | 5:GOTO 9  |13:ACT     |
        | 6:SYNC    |14:        |            | 6:IF Y>0  |14:L=0     |
        | 7: ----   |15:        |            | 7:MOVE v  |15:R=0     |
        +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+

                                Center console
                                  (reloader)
                           +===========+===========+
                           | 0:MOVE <  | 8:A=A+1   |
                           | 1:TIL X=A | 9:IF A=3  |
                           | 2:ACT     |10:A=0     |
                           | 3:MOVE >  |11: ----   |
                           | 4:TIL X=8 |12:        |
                           | 5:SYNC    |13:        |
                           | 6:ACT     |14:        |
                           | 7:SYNC    |15:        |
                           +===========+===========+

Notice how the reloader program uses the A register to keep track of which ammo
rack slot to take a rocket from next; meanwhile, the ammo rack program counts
off six SYNC instructions to know when its slots are all empty (since the other
two machines use exactly two SYNCs for each rocket fired).  If the machines get
out of sync with each other as you're entering their programs, you can just
leave and re-enter the building to reset them all.

With all three programs in place, the machines will automatically coordinate to
get a rocket loaded into the launcher, at which point the launcher will become
controlled by the two levers: the lever lever toggles whether the launcher
should move down or up, and the right lever fires a rocket.  Once you fire a
rocket, the machines will automatically load up another one before returning
control back to the levers.  Use the launcher to destroy four crates: the
uppermost one, as well as the three on the top of the stack (the bottom two
crates of the stack can't be hit).  With those four crates destroyed, you'll
have a clear path to climb up and collect FIELD MANUAL #8.  From there, climb
up further and exit the building out the upper door.

You'll find yourself outside on the roof of the building; watch out for the war
rhino to your left.  Jump over it, then jump twice to your left to reach a rock
ledge, and from there jump up to the top of the taller building, where you see
another console.  Ignore the console for now, and instead enter the doorway
next to it.  Inside, you'll see a whole bunch of screens with numbers on them,
as well as a red computer terminal to the right.  Press the B button to read
the terminal screen, and when it asks you whether to connect the key generator
to the western semaphore, answer "YES".  Those ten screens above show the
randomly generated key, which gets re-randomized every time you enter a
building (or leave the main city center).  Since the key keeps changing, it's
pointless to write it down; instead, you'll have to program the semaphore
machines outside to transmit the key over to where it can be used to unlock the
door on the east end of the city.

Take the doorway back outside, and open up the console there to program the
first of four semaphore machines.  Each semaphore machine has a sliding part
that can MOVE up or down between two positions, as well as two flags.  An ACT
instruction will toggle whichever flag the sliding part is lined up with, while
the F flag indicates the current state of the flag, 0 or 1.  This western-most
semaphore can read the 10-digit key directly from the key generator, using J to
select the digit, and K to read the digit, with each digit being a number from
1 to 4.  The other semaphore machines instead have an S register that senses
the state of a flag from the previous machine.  Finally, the eastern-most
semaphore machine has registers to input the key into the lock.  Therefore, to
solve this puzzle, we need this western-most machine to use its flags to signal
the key digits to the next machine, which must pass the digits along, until
they reach the last machine, which can put them into the lock.  With all that
in mind, program this first semaphore machine as follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:A=K-1   | 8:SYNC    |
        | 1:IF A<2  | 9:SYNC    |
        | 2:GOTO 5  |10:IF F=1  |
        | 3:ACT     |11:ACT     |
        | 4:A=A-2   |12:MOVE v  |
        | 5:MOVE ^  |13:IF F=1  |
        | 6:IF A>0  |14:ACT     |
        | 7:ACT     |15:J=J+1   |
        +===========+===========+

Since each digit is a number from 1 to 4, we start by subtracting 1 to get a
number from 0 to 3, which we can then encode in binary using the two flags: the
lower flag stores the 2's place, and the upper flag stores the 1's place.  Then
we SYNC twice, first to signal the next machine that the digit is ready to
read, and then a second time for it to signal this one back that it has
finished reading the digit.  Finally, we reset the flags, increment J to the
next digit, and repeat.  (Note that there are other ways to transmit the digits
besides this binary-coding scheme.  For example, you could use a single flag,
relying on timing to indicate the digit, e.g. by toggling the flag on for N
seconds to indicate a digit of N.  But the binary scheme is what we'll use for
this walkthrough.)

Close the console and this semaphore will start running, but it won't get very
far yet, since it will be waiting for the other three machines to SYNC with it.
To get to the next machine, first jump down and to the right to the rooftop
just below the next console.  Since we can't jump straight up from here, we'll
have to climb up the inside of the building; drop straight down the right side
of the building you're on, and then quickly enter the doorway before the orc
below can catch you.  Climb up the inside of this building, avoiding the rats.
About halfway up, you should see a paper tucked into a little alcove on the
right.  This paper requires a bit of a precise jump to reach; get a running
start on the ledge just below and to the left, and don't jump until one foot is
already over the ledge.  Once you make it into the alcove, collect JOURNAL
ENTRY #17, then continue upwards and take the doorway out to the rooftop.
There, use the console to program the second semaphore machine as follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:SYNC    | 8: ----   |
        | 1:IF F≠S  | 9:        |
        | 2:ACT     |10:        |
        | 3:MOVE ^  |11:        |
        | 4:IF F≠S  |12:        |
        | 5:ACT     |13:        |
        | 6:MOVE v  |14:        |
        | 7:SYNC    |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

Note the "NOT equals" signs in instructions #1 and #4.  This program will SYNC
once to wait for the first semaphore to get its two flags ready to be read,
then change this machine's two flags to match before SYNC-ing again.  This
second SYNC will signal the previous machine that it can move on to the next
digit, while also signaling the following machine that this machine's flags are
ready to be read.

Of course, this machine won't do anything at first when you close the console,
because its first instruction is a SYNC, and there are still two more machines
in the room that aren't SYNC-ing at all yet.  So head to the right across the
bridge to the next console, and enter the following program for the third
semaphore machine:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:SYNC    | 8: ----   |
        | 1:SYNC    | 9:        |
        | 2:IF F≠S  |10:        |
        | 3:ACT     |11:        |
        | 4:MOVE ^  |12:        |
        | 5:IF F≠S  |13:        |
        | 6:ACT     |14:        |
        | 7:MOVE v  |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

This is exactly the same as the previous program, except that it does both
SYNCs before it starts updating its flags: the first SYNC waits for the first
semaphore machine to set its flags, and the second SYNC waits for the second
semaphore machine to read those and set up its own flags, after which this
third semaphore machine can do the same before repeating the loop.

Close the console and head to the right across the next bridge, and open up the
console for the fourth and final semaphore machine.  This one doesn't have any
flags (nor F register), since there are no more semaphore machines to signal to
after this one.  Instead, it has a J and K register just like the first
semaphore machine, except that instead of using those to read out the ten-digit
key, we are using them to write the key into the lock.  Enter the following
program for the fourth and final semaphore machine:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:SYNC    | 8:K=K+1   |
        | 1:SYNC    | 9:MOVE v  |
        | 2:SYNC    |10:IF J=9  |
        | 3:K=1     |11:END     |
        | 4:IF S=1  |12:J=J+1   |
        | 5:K=K+2   |13:GOTO 1  |
        | 6:MOVE ^  |14: ----   |
        | 7:IF S=1  |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

This program needs three SYNCs at the start, since it needs to wait for the
first digit of the key to pass through the first three semaphore machines
before it can read it, but thereafter it only needs two SYNCs per digit, hence
the GOTO 1 at the end of the program to skip the first SYNC.  Other than that,
this program just decodes the binary signal from the two flags to store a digit
from 1 to 4 in K, then increments J to move on to the next digit, ENDing after
the last digit.

Close the console and let it run for a while.  You can watch the register HUD
in the upper-left corner of the screen to see the program's progress; soon
after J reaches 9, you should hear the puzzle-solved jingle, and the door below
should unlock.  If it doesn't work, the machines may have gotten out of sync
somehow; in that case, open the console for the last semaphore machine, press
the START button to open the debugging menu, and select DEBUG to reset all
machines in the room, then press the START button again to exit the debugger,
and finally close the console to let the programs run again.  If it still
doesn't work, you may have made a typo in one of the four programs; in that
case, go back to each of the four consoles and carefully check each program
against the listings above.

Once the door below the eastern-most console has unlocked, enter the door next
to the console, drop down to the door just below, and exit the building again
through the now-unlocked door.  You'll now be able to head left along the lower
bridge, and enter the top of the building on the other side.  Work your way to
the bottom, avoiding the rats, and be sure to collect JOURNAL ENTRY #30 in the
bottom-left corner before heading through the lower door.  Back outside, you'll
be atop a mound of rubble, with a chasm leading down through a passage in the
ground below; carefully drop down through this passage.

Drop down to the bottom of this new room.  There's a console here to control
the lift that can bring you back up when you're ready, but for now, pass
through the door to face:

       ,-,--------------------,-,                     ,---------------------,
  ,----+ | Boss #6: Bombshell | +---------------------+ Difficulty:   3 / 5 |
  |    '-'--------------------'-'                     | Hits to defeat:   8 |
  | This huge, floating, clam-like monster will weave | Reward:    B-Remote |
  | back and forth in the air, occasionally opening   '--------------------+'
  | and closing its shell to either shoot a spray of spikes at you, or to  |
  | drop a pair of bombs that explode into rapidly moving tongues of fire  |
  | that you'll have to dodge.  Its shell is impervious, so your job is to |
  | fire rockets at its soft, inner core whenever the shell opens up.  But |
  | since the launcher machine only holds one rocket at a time, you'll     |
  | have to coordinate all three machines in this room to keep it stocked  |
  | with ammo.                                                             |
  |                                                                        |
  | Thankfully, this trio of machines           Upper-right console        |
  | should be familiar if you already               (reloader)             |
  | solved the rocket-and-crates puzzle      +===========+===========+     |
  | earlier in the city area.  Start by      | 0:MOVE <  | 8:A=A+1   |     |
  | entering the following three programs    | 1:TIL X=A | 9:IF A=3  |     |
  | into the three consoles:                 | 2:ACT     |10:A=0     |     |
  |                                          | 3:MOVE >  |11: ----   |     |
  |                                          | 4:TIL X=9 |12:        |     |
  |                                          | 5:SYNC    |13:        |     |
  |                                          | 6:ACT     |14:        |     |
  |                                          | 7:SYNC    |15:        |     |
  |                                          +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  |         Left-hand console                   Lower-right console        |
  |            (ammo rack)                           (launcher)            |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:ACT     | 8:        |            | 0:MOVE ^  | 8:GOTO 11 |     |
  |     | 1:SYNC    | 9:        |            | 1:TIL Y=5 | 9:IF Y<4  |     |
  |     | 2:SYNC    |10:        |            | 2:SYNC    |10:MOVE ^  |     |
  |     | 3:SYNC    |11:        |            | 3:SYNC    |11:IF R=0  |     |
  |     | 4:SYNC    |12:        |            | 4:IF L=1  |12:GOTO 4  |     |
  |     | 5:SYNC    |13:        |            | 5:GOTO 9  |13:ACT     |     |
  |     | 6:SYNC    |14:        |            | 6:IF Y>0  |14:L=0     |     |
  |     | 7: ----   |15:        |            | 7:MOVE v  |15:R=0     |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | Refer to earlier in this section if you want a refresher on how these  |
  | three programs work; they are very similar to the puzzle before, with  |
  | only slight tweaks to account for the dimensions of this room.  To use |
  | them, use the left lever to control whether the rocket launcher moves  |
  | up or down, and the right lever to fire; after firing, the launcher    |
  | will automatically move back up to be reloaded.                        |
  |                                                                        |
  | Stand at the levers and wait for your opportunity to fire a rocket     |
  | into Bombshell's core.  If it drops fire bombs where you're standing,  |
  | jump elsewhere and just wait for them to dissipate.  This boss isn't   |
  | too difficult if you're patient, but if you try to rush things you     |
  | might get caught and die and have to start over, so take your time.    |
  | The boss's shell will start to crack at the edges with each successful |
  | rocket strike, and after eight hits, this giant clam's flying days     |
  | will be over.                                                          |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

With Bombshell blasted, pick up the strange upgrade that appears.  It's the
B-Remote!  This is what the orcs have been searching for all this time.  The
B-Remote gives you more direct control over the machines, by giving each
machine a new B register whose value is directly controlled by the B button on
your controller: if you're not holding the B button, the B register has a value
of zero, but when you hold down the B button, it has a value from 1 to 9,
determined by which direction(s) you're holding on the D-pad:

                            1       2       3
                                  ,___,
                                  | ^ |
                               +--+   +--+
                            4  | <  5  > |  6
                               +--+   +--+
                                  | v |
                                  '---'
                            7       8       9

Notice how the pattern is similar to a phone keypad (or to the digit menu when
programming one of the machines in this game), if that helps you remember it.
By using the B register with IF or SKIP, you can effectively program machines
to operate by remote control.  You'll get a chance to try it out soon enough.
In the meantime, once you've picked up the B-Remote, a circuit breaker lever
will once again appear; flip it, and another cutscene will play.  CIRCUIT #6
has now been activated!  When you're ready, leave this building through the
door.

You'll be back outside in the sinkhole pit.  Use the nearby console to program
the lift, and let's try out this new B-Remote upgrade:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF B=8  | 8:MOVE v  |
        | 1:GOTO 7  | 9: ----   |
        | 2:IF B≠2  |10:        |
        | 3:GOTO 0  |11:        |
        | 4:IF Y<9  |12:        |
        | 5:MOVE ^  |13:        |
        | 6:GOTO 0  |14:        |
        | 7:IF Y>0  |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

Note the "NOT equals" sign in instruction #2.  With this program in place, the
lift will move down when you hold the B button and press Down on the D-pad, and
it will move up when you hold the B button and press Up on the D-pad.  Close
the console and use the B button and D-pad to bring the lift down to the
ground, then hop aboard and use the B button and D-pad to make the lift carry
you to the top.  Once you're at the top of the room, jump up through the
passage in the ceiling.

Back in the main city cavern, you'll find Alex waiting for you.  Speak with
him, and he'll congratulate you on finding the B-Remote, and mark on your map
the next place you need to go.  Before we head there, however, we have some
other errands to take care of.

Jump up the rest of the way out of the pit, and head to the left.  Jump over
the orc when he gives chase, and keep running until you exit through the
left-hand passage.  You'll be a the top of a tall room, with a broken bridge
and many enemies crawling below.  For now, leap over the gap in the bridge, and
exit through the top-left passage.

In the next room, jump down to the floor, watching out for the war rhino below,
and enter the building through the bottom door.  Climb up the inside of this
building, avoiding the rats inside, and collect JOURNAL ENTRY #24 at the top.
Then exit through the upper door.  Back outside, use the console to enter the
following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:MOVE v  | 8:        |
        | 1:MOVE v  | 9:        |
        | 2:ACT     |10:        |
        | 3:END     |11:        |
        | 4: ----   |12:        |
        | 5:        |13:        |
        | 6:        |14:        |
        | 7:        |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

The rocket launcher will fire at the thin wall to the left, blasting it open.
This will give you an easier path between the Ancient City and the Power Core
if you need to pass through here again later.

Our next major destination is the mermaid village, but there are two more
papers and one more flower that can be collected along the way.  If you want to
do that, see the "Optional Detour" box below.  If not, you can skip the box
below, and instead head westward from here until you find the Power Core
elevator, and take the elevators all the way to the Mermaid Vale; you can
always get the flower and papers later (see Sections 13 and 14).

          ,-,-,--------------------------------------------------,-,-,
  ,------/ / / Optional Detour: Deliver the Ancient City Flower / / /------,
  |     '-'-'--------------------------------------------------'-'-'       |
  | Starting from the outskirts of the Ancient City, at the top of the     |
  | building with a launcher machine outside, jump to the right from the   |
  | top of the building, and head through the passage to the right.        |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the room with the broken bridge and the enemies below,         |
  | carefully drop down, avoiding the enemies, the head through the        |
  | passage in the bottom-right.  You'll see another broken bridge, with   |
  | a bird enemy lurking underneath, and a deep pit below.  Get a running  |
  | start and leap over the gap in the bridge.  (If you miss the jump and  |
  | fall below, don't continue down the pit; instead, get the bird to fly  |
  | to the left side, then jump up on the little ledge on the right, just  |
  | below the bridge, then jump up and to the left and immediately turn    |
  | around in midair so you can land back on the bridge.  If you do fall   |
  | down the pit, you'll land back in the Old Waterway, and you'll have to |
  | work your way back up to the city; see Section 08 for help with that.) |
  | Once you're across the bridge, exit through the right-hand passage.    |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be in a small room with a squat building.  Before dropping to   |
  | the floor, jump from the passage ledge to the top of the building      |
  | where the giant toad is, landing on the very edge of the roof, just    |
  | short of the toad; there's just barely enough room there for you to    |
  | stand.  This may take you a few tries.  Once you make it onto the edge |
  | of the roof, wait for the toad to jump, then slip underneath it and go |
  | collect JOURNAL ENTRY #35.  Then drop to the bottom of the room, and   |
  | enter the building.                                                    |
  |                                                                        |
  | There's an orc grunt just inside, but he won't attack you; in fact,    |
  | you can talk to him.  Just beyond the orc is a flower on a crate.      |
  | Grab the flower, and the orc will attack.  As soon as you grab the     |
  | flower, jump up to the ledge on the right to escape the orc, then jump |
  | left to the platform, and when the orc is clear of the door, drop down |
  | and exit the building.                                                 |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now you need to climb back up to the upper-left passage without        |
  | breaking the flower.  This requires a tricky jump over the grub that's |
  | halfway up the building; as usual, stand right in front of it, and     |
  | jump as soon as it inches towards you.  Then exit through the passage. |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the room with the bird enemy, we'll need to drop below the     |
  | bird without getting hit (but don't drop down the pit yet!).  The      |
  | easiest way to do this is to first leap over the gap in the bridge;    |
  | this will most likely get the bird to fly across, and then while it's  |
  | still settling down and flapping its wings, you can safely hop down to |
  | the floor below it.  Once you're below the bird, get ready to drop     |
  | into the pit, holding Left on the D-pad as you fall.  You'll land on a |
  | small ledge where you can collect JOURNAL ENTRY #33.  Then drop the    |
  | rest of the down the pit.                                              |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll fall back into the Old Waterway, into the room with the         |
  | vertical multiplexer machine.  Drop again through the passage in the   |
  | floor here to land at the top of the next room, with two grubs and a   |
  | fish enemy below you.  Drop onto the little ledge on the left, wait    |
  | for the fish to be out of your way, then drop down into the passage in |
  | the bottom-left.  This will take you back to the duck pond room.  Head |
  | left, feeding the ducks along the way if you want (quack!), and exit   |
  | through the left-hand passage.  Head left through the next two rooms,  |
  | avoiding the two giant toads, and you'll return to the factory         |
  | elevator room.  From here, you can take the elevator down to the       |
  | mermaid vale, and return to the village.  Deliver the flower to Selene |
  | in the bottom center hut.                                              |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

Whether or not you delivered the flower, you should be in the mermaid village
at this point.  Before you head to the destination Alex told you about, talk to
everyone in the village; several characters will have new things to say,
including Phoebe (the little mermaid girl in the bottom-left hut), Dion (the
mermaid farmer outside), Marie and Nora (in the bottom-right hut), the mermaid
guard inside the upper-right hut, and Queen Eirene in the top-center hut.

When you're ready, it's time to go activate the final circuit.



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 10: Secrets in the Shadows                                       ||
++==========================================================================++
 ,___________,
 | Checklist  \_________________________,              ||||||||||||||||
 |                                       \             ||--||----||--||
 |  [] Activate the teleporter            |            ||||||||||||||||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #01          |        ||||----------------||||
 |  [] In case of emergency, break glass  |      ||------------------------||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #02          |    ||------||--------------------||
 |  [] Begin to learn the truth           |    ||----||----------------------||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #06          |    ||----||----------------------||
 |  [] Contact the ghostly orc            |    ||----||----------------------||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #04          |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Contact the ghostly mermaid        |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #20          |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Collect JOURNAL ENTRY #32          |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Defeat ALDA EGO                    |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Gain MUL OPCODE upgrade            |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Activate CIRCUIT #7                |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Deliver the final flower, if you   |    ||----------------------------||
 |     dare                               |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Gain BEEP OPCODE upgrade           |    ||----------------------------||
 |  [] Tell Phoebe about your latest      |    ||----------------------------||
 |     adventure                          |    ||--||--------------------||--||
 |________________________________________|    ||----||----------------||----||
                                                 ||------------------------||
Starting from the mermaid village, head          ||||||----------------||||||
through the upper-right passage to the hot       ||----||||||||||||||||----||
springs, and fall down the shaft back into       ||||||@@--||----||--@@||||||
the Volcanic Pit.  Here, take the passage        ||----@@--@@----@@--@@----||
on the left into a small room with a strange-
looking machine.  This machine was unusable before we activated circuit #6 in
the Ancient City, but now you can open up the console and program it as
follows:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF P<9  | 8:        |
        | 1:GOTO 0  | 9:        |
        | 2:IF B>0  |10:        |
        | 3:ACT     |11:        |
        | 4: ----   |12:        |
        | 5:        |13:        |
        | 6:        |14:        |
        | 7:        |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

The P register indicates the power level; it will count up from 0 to 9 after
you close the console and after each ACT instruction.  ACT will always drain
the power level to zero, but will have no other useful effect unless the power
was charged up all the way to 9, which is why the first part of the above
program loops until P is 9.  Then, it will wait until you press the B button
(thus making the B-Remote's B register non-zero) before ACTing.

Close the console and watch the register HUD in the top-left corner of the
screen until P is 9, then try pressing the B button.  The whole room will shake
as a huge lightning bolt crackles between the two halves of the machine.  To
proceed, you'll need to get yourself hit by that lightning bolt.  Climb up the
left side of the room until you're on top of the machine, and wait for P to
count up to 9.  Then jump down the center of the gap, and press the B button
just as you're right between the two halves of the machine.  If you did it
right, you'll disappear in a puff of smoke, the screen will fade to black, and
you'll find yourself in a new location.

Welcome to the Shadow Labs, and to the most difficult portion of the game.

The console to your right controls the teleporter machine on this end, but we
won't be needing that quite yet.  Read the computer terminal on the left, then
leave this room through the left-hand passage.  Don't fall into the green acid,
by the way; it's instant death if you do.

You'll find yourself at the bottom of a tall room patrolled by two fire bugs.
Jump across to the console on the left, which controls a lift machine embedded
in the center floor.  Enter the following program:

        +===========+===========+
        | 0:IF B>0  | 8:        |
        | 1:MOVE ^  | 9:        |
        | 2:TIL B=0 |10:        |
        | 3: ----   |11:        |
        | 4:        |12:        |
        | 5:        |13:        |
        | 6:        |14:        |
        | 7:        |15:        |
        +===========+===========+

Close the console, and stand in the center of the center floor.  When you're
ready, press and hold the B button, and the lift machine will carry you up.  At
any time, you can release the B button to stop the lift.  To get past the two
fire bugs without being knocked off, you'll have to jump in place to dodge
their fireballs, and move the lift up at the right times so that you aren't
