  |     | 7: ----   |15:        |            | 7:        |15:        |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | If you don't have the REST and SYNC opcodes yet, you can make do by    |
  | just programming each machine with a single-instruction "MOVE <"       |
  | program.  Then stand at the left-hand console, wait for the upper      |
  | rotor's platform to reach the very bottom of the wheel, then press the |
  | B button a couple times to open and close the console and reset the    |
  | lower rotor machine.  If you did it right, the two wheels will be      |
  | synced up such that their platforms pass very close to each other,     |
  | allowing you to jump between them.                                     |
  |                                                                        |
  | Either way, climb up the two wheels' platforms and approach the top.   |
  | You'll need to jump onto the ledge with the grub.  This can be little  |
  | tricky; you have to jump off while the upper rotor is still turning,   |
  | just as the platform is sliding under the ceiling.  But the grub might |
  | be in your way at that moment, so it might just take you a few tries.  |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you're up there, head left, jump over the spikes and grab the     |
  | flower, then jump back over the spikes.  This is another tricky jump   |
  | that may take a few tries to make without breaking the flower.  Once   |
  | you're back over the spikes with the flower intact, drop down while    |
  | holding Right on the D-pad.  You should land safely in one of the      |
  | alcoves in the bottom right.  From there, jump to the left-hand        |
  | console, and then down to the left.  Be careful leaving this room;     |
  | it's all too easy to whack your head on the side of the ledge just     |
  | above the exit passage and fall into the spikes.  To avoid that, make  |
  | a short jump over the spikes by tapping the A button briefly.          |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you're back in the factory elevator room, take the elevator down  |
  | and return to the mermaid village to deliver the flower.               |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

                     ,-,-,---------------------------,-,-,
  ,-----------------( ( ( #5: The Old Waterway Flower ) ) )----------------,
  |                  '-'-'---------------------------'-'-'                 |
  | To reach this flower, you must have already activated circuit #4, met  |
  | with Alex in the Rust Factory, and received help from Corra in the Old |
  | Waterway.  The below instructions will assume that you've also already |
  | been past that point and reached the eastern edge of the Ancient City. |
  | If not, see Section 08.                                                |
  |                                                                        |
  | Starting from the mermaid village, leave through the top-right passage |
  | and work your way up to the elevator room, then take the elevator      |
  | upwards to the Rust Factory.  From the Rust Factory elevator room,     |
  | take the lower-right passage.                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pass through this next room to reach the room with the underwater      |
  | lever.  Climb up this room and exit through the passage in the         |
  | ceiling.  In this small room, jump up the left side, climb to the top, |
  | and walk off the right side of the top-center platform, holding Right  |
  | on the D-Pad as you do, to land on the right-hand ledge and exit       |
  | through that passage.  Work you way to the right through the next      |
  | room, avoiding the many enemies, and take the right-hand passage.      |
  |                                                                        |
  | The multiplexer machine in this next room should already be programmed |
  | to allow you to reach the upper-right passage; if not, refer to        |
  | Section 08 to program it.  After taking that passage, the next room    |
  | with the valves should also already be programmed; refer to Section 08 |
  | if not.  With the right-hand pool filled with water, take the passage  |
  | on the right.  Finally, drop down to the bottom of this room, climb up |
  | the left side, jump across past the little waterfall, and climb up the |
  | right side of the room.  Once you make it up, take the passage in the  |
  | upper-right.                                                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be in a small room with a flower, as well as a page in the top  |
  | left.  Climb up to the console and enter the following program:        |
  |                                                                        |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |     | 0:MOVE v  | 8:        |                                          |
  |     | 1:TIL Y=0 | 9:        |                                          |
  |     | 2:MOVE ^  |10:        |                                          |
  |     | 3:TIL Y=5 |11:        |                                          |
  |     | 4: ----   |12:        |                                          |
  |     | 5:        |13:        |                                          |
  |     | 6:        |14:        |                                          |
  |     | 7:        |15:        |                                          |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | The trick here is that you need the water level lowered in order to    |
  | get down and make it around the bend, but if you just lower the water  |
  | level to its most shallow and leave it there, then you'll hit the      |
  | spikes when you jump in.  So instead, we've programmed the pump to     |
  | keep moving the water up and down; you can safely float yourself down  |
  | while staying at the surface of the water, then climb up the other     |
  | side.  Once up top, grab the flower, then go left to collect the page  |
  | if you haven't already.  Don't jump down from here, or you'll hit the  |
  | spikes and break your flower.  Instead, go back to the right, ride the |
  | moving water carefully down, then climb back up to the console, and    |
  | exit the room through the left-hand passage.                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back outside the flower room, drop down carefully along the right side |
  | of this tall room, and take the bottom passage in the floor.  From     |
  | here, drop down the side, avoiding the jellyfish, and take the         |
  | left-hand passage.  You'll be in the room with the lift machine that   |
  | can push you underwater; just jump across the top of the room and take |
  | the left-hand passage.  In the next room, the multiplexer machine      |
  | should still be programmed to help you get through, but if not, refer  |
  | back to Section 07 to program it; then take the left-hand passage.     |
  | Feed the ducks in the next room if you want (quack!), then keep        |
  | heading left through the next several rooms until you reach the        |
  | factory elevator room.  From here, take the elevator down and follow   |
  | the hopefully familiar by now route to the mermaid village to deliver  |
  | the flower.                                                            |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

                     ,-,-,---------------------------,-,-,
  ,-----------------( ( ( #6: The Ancient City Flower ) ) )----------------,
  |                  '-'-'---------------------------'-'-'                 |
  | To reach this flower, at a minimum you must have already activated     |
  | circuit #4, met with Alex in the Rust Factory, and received help from  |
  | Corra in the Old Waterway.  However, it's much easier to reach it if   |
  | you've also visited the outskirts on the far western edge of the       |
  | Ancient City and used the launcher machine to destroy the wall         |
  | blocking it off from the Power Core.  If you haven't done that yet,    |
  | you'll need to follow the instructions in Section 08 and Section 09 to |
  | reach the city center, then head west two rooms from there until you   |
  | reach the outskirts, climb the building there, and program the         |
  | launcher machine with "MOVE v, MOVE v, ACT, END" to open up the wall.  |
  |                                                                        |
  | If you've already broken open the wall in the city outskirts, then     |
  | starting from the mermaid village, leave through the top-right passage |
  | and work your way up to the elevator room, then take the elevator      |
  | upwards to the Rust Factory.  From the Rust Factory elevator room,     |
  | take the upper elevator up to the Power Core, then take the right-hand |
  | passage.  You'll be at the bottom of a tall room filled with tall,     |
  | narrow platforms, and bird enemies waiting to knock you off.  The best |
  | way up, for the most part, is to jump up quickly and confidently.  If  |
  | you do it right, the first two birds will rush at you just before you  |
  | reach them, and you can keep jumping past them while they're still     |
  | flapping their wings and settling back down.  When you're almost at    |
  | the top, the third bird will fly to the right as you jump onto the     |
  | upper-left-most platform.  You may need to jump at it to bait it to    |
  | fly to the left, so that you can jump past and through the upper-right |
  | passage to reach the city outskirts.                                   |
  |                                                                        |
  | Whichever route you took, you should now be at the city outskirts on   |
  | the far western edge of the Ancient City.  Climb up the inside of the  |
  | building with the launcher machine outside, jump to the right from the |
  | top of the building, and head through the passage to the right.        |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the room with the broken bridge and the enemies below,         |
  | carefully drop down, avoiding the enemies, the head through the        |
  | passage in the bottom-right.  You'll see another broken bridge, with   |
  | a bird enemy lurking underneath, and a deep pit below.  Get a running  |
  | start and leap over the gap in the bridge.  (If you miss the jump and  |
  | fall below, don't continue down the pit; instead, get the bird to fly  |
  | to the left side, then jump up on the little ledge on the right, just  |
  | below the bridge, then jump up and to the left and immediately turn    |
  | around in midair so you can land back on the bridge.  If you do fall   |
  | down the pit, you'll land back in the Old Waterway, and you'll have to |
  | work your way back up to the city; see Section 08 for help with that.) |
  | Once you're across the bridge, exit through the right-hand passage.    |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be in a small room with a squat building.  If you haven't       |
  | already collected the page atop the building, then before dropping to  |
  | the floor, jump from the passage ledge to the top of the building      |
  | where the giant toad is, landing on the very edge of the roof, just    |
  | short of the toad; there's just barely enough room there for you to    |
  | stand.  This may take you a few tries.  Once you make it onto the edge |
  | of the roof, wait for the toad to jump, then slip underneath it and go |
  | collect the page.                                                      |
  |                                                                        |
  | With or without the page, enter the building.  There's an orc grunt    |
  | just inside, but he won't attack you; in fact, you can talk to him.    |
  | Just beyond the orc is a flower on a crate.  Grab the flower, and the  |
  | orc will attack.  As soon as you grab the flower, jump up to the ledge |
  | on the right to escape the orc, then jump left to the platform, and    |
  | when the orc is clear of the door, drop down and exit the building.    |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now you need to climb back up to the upper-left passage without        |
  | breaking the flower.  This requires a tricky jump over the grub that's |
  | halfway up the building; as usual, stand right in front of it, and     |
  | jump as soon as it inches towards you.  Then exit through the passage. |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the room with the bird enemy, we'll need to drop below the     |
  | bird without getting hit (but don't drop down the pit yet!).  The      |
  | easiest way to do this is to first leap over the gap in the bridge;    |
  | this will most likely get the bird to fly across, and then while it's  |
  | still settling down and flapping its wings, you can safely hop down to |
  | the floor below it.  Once you're below the bird, get ready to drop     |
  | into the pit, holding Left on the D-pad as you fall.  You'll land on a |
  | small ledge where you can collect another page if you haven't already. |
  | Then drop the rest of the down the pit.                                |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll fall back into the Old Waterway, into the room with the         |
  | vertical multiplexer machine.  Drop again through the passage in the   |
  | floor here to land at the top of the next room, with two grubs and a   |
  | fish enemy below you.  Drop onto the little ledge on the left, wait    |
  | for the fish to be out of your way, then drop down into the passage in |
  | the bottom-left.  This will take you back to the duck pond room.  Head |
  | left, feeding the ducks along the way if you want (quack!), and exit   |
  | through the left-hand passage.  Head left through the next two rooms,  |
  | avoiding the two giant toads, and you'll return to the factory         |
  | elevator room.  From here, you can take the elevator down to the       |
  | mermaid vale, and return to the village.  Deliver the flower to Selene |
  | in the bottom center hut.                                              |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

                     ,-,-,---------------------------,-,-,
  ,-----------------( ( ( #7: The Hidden Crypt Flower ) ) )----------------,
  |                  '-'-'---------------------------'-'-'                 |
  | To deliver this flower, you must have activated circuit #3 in the      |
  | Hidden Crypt. The below instructions will assume that you've already   |
  | done that and then returned to the mermaid village.                    |
  |                                                                        |
  | The first step is to return to the Hidden Crypt.  Starting from the    |
  | mermaid village, leave through the top-left passage and continue west  |
  | until you're back in the Vine Garden.  In the huge garden room with    |
  | the tower, jump up through the trees and head out the top-right        |
  | passage.  Crawl through this winding tunnel, avoiding the grubs, and   |
  | continue east.  Back in the big room with the vine bug that blocks the |
  | way, jump onto the tree and then through the opening above the bug,    |
  | and head to the right-hand side of this room.  Climb up the far right  |
  | edge of the room to the very top, then jump up onto the uppermost      |
  | platform and head left past the bridge.  Leave this room through the   |
  | top-left passage.  You're now back at the "garden crossroads", where   |
  | you earlier solved a puzzle to squish a grub.  Walk left and exit out  |
  | the bottom-left passage.  Head left through this long hallway (see     |
  | Section 03 if you need help with the lift machine here) to return to   |
  | the Lost Temple.  Climb up the temple entrance and go through the      |
  | upper-right passage here to enter the temple foyer.  Use the carriage  |
  | machine to reach the upper-left passage in this huge room (see Section |
  | 03 if you need help), then go through to enter the "temple nave",      |
  | where you met with Alex earlier.  Use the crate he left behind to get  |
  | to the lower-left passage and go through, then jump down the pit in    |
  | the next room to enter the Hidden Crypt.                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll land on a small platform surrounded by spikes.  Leave through   |
  | the passage to the right.  In the next room, use the winch machine to  |
  | head downwards (see Section 05 if you need help), then take the        |
  | passage in the bottom-left.  Wind your way past the spiders and head   |
  | down through the passage in the floor, then drop down past the         |
  | spikeballs in the next room and take the lower-right passage to enter  |
  | the "crusher room".  Use the winch here to reach the upper-right       |
  | passage (see Section 05 if you need help) and go through to enter the  |
  | "trefoil room".  Work your way past the bats, and jump through the     |
  | ceiling passage in the upper right.  In this tall room, climb up past  |
  | the two bats to the upper half of this next room, jump over the little |
  | spiked walls, and avoid the spider on the ceiling as you head up and   |
  | through the upper-right passage.                                       |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll find yourself in a small room with another winch, and the       |
  | flower visible at the bottom-left.  Use the console to program the     |
  | winch as follows:                                                      |
  |                                                                        |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |     | 0:ACT     | 6:        |                                          |
  |     | 1:REST 1  | 7:        |                                          |
  |     | 2:MOVE ^  | 8:        |                                          |
  |     | 3:TIL Z=0 | 9:        |                                          |
  |     | 4:REST 2  |10:        |                                          |
  |     | 5: ----   |11:        |                                          |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | This will make the winch move its two platforms up and down, with some |
  | extra pauses to give you more time to make your jumps.  Jump onto the  |
  | upper platform; then, when the winch drops to the bottom, walk off the |
  | right side of the upper platform and then immediately hold Left on the |
  | D-pad to slip onto the lower platform.  Wait for the platforms to rise |
  | and fall again, then jump over to the flower and pick it up.           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Getting back up is a little trickier.  When the winch drops, jump onto |
  | its lower platform.  Wait for the platform to rise and fall, then make |
  | a short hop over to the floor to the right, and immediately walk over  |
  | to hide under the block on the right side, where the spider can't      |
  | reach you.  Wait for the spider to wander to the left, then quickly    |
  | run out and hop onto the block, then climb up the right side of the    |
  | room.  Wait for the bat to start moving up, then jump onto the floor   |
  | below it, and immediately jump onto the top platform of the winch.     |
  | Wait for it to rise to the top, then jump over to the console, and     |
  | leave the room.                                                        |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now at the top of the previous room, getting down safely from here     |
  | takes some care.  Slip past the spider and drop down to the left of    |
  | the three little spiked walls.  Trying to jump and land exactly        |
  | between the leftmost two walls is risky, but there's an easier way.    |
  | Hold Right on the D-pad to press up against the left side of the       |
  | leftmost wall, then keeping holding Right as you press and hold A for  |
  | a long jump.  You'll sail right over the first two walls and land      |
  | safely to the left of the third.  Then you can easily jump safely over |
  | the third wall.  From there, just avoid the bats as you drop down the  |
  | right side of the room and down through the passage on the right side  |
  | of the floor.                                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the "trefoil room", which is your next challenge.    |
  | Carefully avoid the rightmost bat as you drop down the right side.     |
  | When the center bat starts moving up, hop over the patch of spikes in  |
  | the middle, and hide under the little ledge here.  Wait again for the  |
  | bat to move up, then quickly slip out and jump onto the little ledge   |
  | you were hiding under.  Wait for the bat to move down, then jump over  |
  | it and climb up the center.  Finally, you'll have to make a tricky     |
  | jump over a wide patch of spikes.  If you tap the A button lightly     |
  | instead of pressing it down and holding it, you'll make a short hop    |
  | that avoids bouncing your head off the ceiling, which can make the     |
  | jump a little easier.  If you do fail the jump and break the flower,   |
  | stay here to practice a bit before heading back to get the flower      |
  | again.  Once you make the jump with the flower intact, leave through   |
  | the passage to return to the "crusher room".                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Leave the "crusher room" through the upper-left passage to return to   |
  | the room with the long chain of spikeballs hanging from a winch.  Use  |
  | the console here to reprogram the winch as follows:                    |
  |                                                                        |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |     | 0:MOVE ^  | 6: ----   |                                          |
  |     | 1:TIL Z=0 | 7:        |                                          |
  |     | 2:REST 5  | 8:        |                                          |
  |     | 3:MOVE v  | 9:        |                                          |
  |     | 4:TIL Z=4 |10:        |                                          |
  |     | 5:REST 5  |11:        |                                          |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | With the spikeballs moving up and down, you can now zigzag your way    |
  | up the room, waiting each time for the spikeballs to be in a favorable |
  | position before crossing the center of the room.  Once you reach the   |
  | top, jump up through the passage on the right side of the ceiling.     |
  | You'll be back in the small room with three spiders; maneuver your way |
  | through, avoiding the spiders and the spikes, and leave through the    |
  | right-hand passage.                                                    |
  |                                                                        |
  | In this tall room, use the lift machine on the right to reach the      |
  | upper-right passage and go through.  This next room is chock full of   |
  | enemies.  Swim through the pool of water to the right, avoiding the    |
  | fish, and jump onto the wider ledge and move right to hide from the    |
  | bat under the short ledge.  When the bat moves up, jump up onto the    |
  | short ledge, then jump over the bat when it moves down.  Head left,    |
  | then jump up onto the two little platforms on the columns.             |
  |                                                                        |
  | Above you are two bats.  The easiest way past them is to wait until    |
  | they're synced up close to each other and moving together to the       |
  | right, and then as they are moving past the edge of the right-hand     |
  | ledge, jump to that ledge and then immediately leap up and left to the |
  | other ledge, and then up to the rocks on the far left.  There you can  |
  | catch your breath and take a look at the two spiders on the ceiling.   |
  | Jump right and quickly past them, watching out for the spikes, and     |
  | head through the upper-right passage.                                  |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the Lost Temple.  Climb up and over the column       |
  | blocking your way, and leave through the passage to the right to       |
  | return to the temple entrance.  Drop down the left edge of this tall   |
  | room to safely avoid the enemies, then leave through the bottom-right  |
  | passage to return to the Vine Garden.  Pass through the long room here |
  | (see Section 03 if you need help doing this safely while carrying a    |
  | flower) to return to the bottom of the "garden crossroads" room.  From |
  | here, follow your minimap back to the mermaid village; be careful and  |
  | take your time so you don't hit any enemies!  When you reach the       |
  | village, deliver the flower to Selene in the bottom-center hut.        |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

                     ,-,-,--------------------------,-,-,
  ,-----------------( ( ( #8: The Shadow Labs Flower ) ) )-----------------,
  |                  '-'-'--------------------------'-'-'                  |
  | To deliver this flower, at a minimum you must have visited the Ancient |
  | City and activated circuit #6 there.  It is also very important to     |
  | have all the machines in the Shadow Labs, the Volcanic Pit, the Salt   |
  | Mines, and the Old Waterway set up to make the return journey as safe  |
  | as possible, so the below instructions will assume that you've already |
  | traversed the Shadow Labs and returned from there.  Be extra sure that |
  | the winch machine in the Salt Mines conveyor room is running so as to  |
  | allow you to take the shortcut up through the floor passage there.     |
  | (If you've been following this walkthrough, you'll have done that in   |
  | Section 07.)                                                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Starting from the mermaid village, leave through the top-right passage |
  | to return to the hot springs, and fall down the shaft back into the    |
  | Volcanic Pit.  Here, take the passage on the left into the teleporter  |
  | room.  Use the machine to teleport to the Shadow Labs (see Section 10  |
  | if you need help with this, or with any of the next paragraph).        |
  |                                                                        |
  | In the Shadow Labs teleporter room, go through the left passage, then  |
  | use the lift machine in the next room to ascend to the top and take    |
  | the upper-left passage.  Work your way through this room with the acid |
  | bug where the lights go out, and take the left-hand passage.  In the   |
  | next room, ride the minigun through the maze, and take the left-hand   |
  | passage out.  In the upside-down room, zig-zag your way down, and take |
  | the lower-left passage.  In the next room, slip past the acid bug and  |
  | take the upper-left passage to reach the flower room.                  |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll find yourself in a small room with some red and green goo, a    |
  | laser machine, and a flower in the bottom-left corner.  First we need  |
  | to clear away some of the goo, so head to the console in the top-left  |
  | and program the laser machine as follows:                              |
  |                                                                        |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |     | 0:IF B=2  | 8:GOTO 0  |                                          |
  |     | 1:GOTO 9  | 9:C=1-C   |                                          |
  |     | 2:IF B=4  |10:REST 1  |                                          |
  |     | 3:MOVE <  |11: ----   |                                          |
  |     | 4:IF B=6  |12:        |                                          |
  |     | 5:MOVE >  |13:        |                                          |
  |     | 6:IF B=8  |14:        |                                          |
  |     | 7:ACT     |15:        |                                          |
  |     +===========+===========+                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | To move the laser left or right, hold the B button and press Left or   |
  | Right on the D-pad; to fire, hold the B button and press Down on the   |
  | D-pad; to change the laser color, hold the B button and press Up on    |
  | the D-pad.  Remember that each goo has to be killed with the opposite  |
  | color of laser.                                                        |
  |                                                                        |
  | You won't be able to reach the leftmost green goo with the laser, but  |
  | go ahead and zap the other two.  You could try to lure the red goo     |
  | under the hole in the floor so you can zap it too, but it's easier to  |
  | just leave it alone and jump low under it with a quick A button tap.   |
  | Don't approach the flower just yet, though, because it's a trap: if    |
  | you get close, it will grow into a monstrous plant-beast and shoot     |
  | fireballs at you.  Instead, first position the laser near the left     |
  | side of the room, so that it's above the gap in the floor that's just  |
  | to the right of the flower.  It doesn't matter which color the laser   |
  | is set to for this part.  Then, carefully jump across the acid towards |
  | the flower.  As soon as the flower grows into a monster, fire the      |
  | laser to destroy it.  The monster will leave behind the real flower,   |
  | which you can then pick up and carefully carry back out of the room.   |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now for the hard part: getting back to the mermaid village from here   |
  | without getting hit.  It is very unlikely that you will succeed on the |
  | first try.                                                             |
  |                                                                        |
  | After leaving the flower room, run carefully under the acid bug, and   |
  | pass through the upper-right passage here to return to the upside-down |
  | laser room.  Weave your way to the top of the room, and exit out the   |
  | upper-right passage.  Back in the minigun room, jump up to the top     |
  | path and head to the right; half-way through the room, you'll need to  |
  | jump along the upper platforms to get back to and exit through the     |
  | right-hand passage.                                                    |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the small room with acid-spitting bug, where the     |
  | lights go out in the middle of the room.  Getting past the bug without |
  | getting hit requires a bit of luck, but your best bet is to climb up   |
  | the left side of the room (in the dark), wait for the bug to head to   |
  | the right, and then quickly move to the middle, drop down the center   |
  | of the room, and then move right under the red computer screen to hide |
  | under the platform there.  The lights should turn back on at that      |
  | point, making it easier for you to jump over to the right-hand passage |
  | when the coast is clear.                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the tall room with the two fire bugs.  Jump straight |
  | down the center of the room, and their fireballs should miss you, and  |
  | you should land on the floor and not in the acid.  From here, jump     |
  | right and through the bottom-right passage.  Back in the teleporter    |
  | room, use the field machine to return to the Volcanic Pit.             |
  |                                                                        |
  | Exit the Volcanic Pit teleporter room through the right-hand passage.  |
  | Drop down carefully along the left side of this room, then work your   |
  | way to the right while avoiding all the fireballs from above and       |
  | below, and take the bottom-right passage.  Jump over the gap in the    |
  | next room, and continue through the right-hand passage.  At the bottom |
  | of this next tall room, jump across the lava at the bottom, climb atop |
  | the crate, and exit through the lower-right passage.                   |
  |                                                                        |
  | In this large room, use the various boiler machines to propel you to   |
  | the top-right corner, and take the passage there.  Refer to Section 06 |
  | if you need help reprogramming these machines.  In the next room, take |
  | the lift machine to the top, and pass through the upper-right passage  |
  | to return to the Salt Mines.                                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | In the Salt Mines, climb up through the first room and head right to   |
  | return to the huge room near the bottom of the mines.  Climb to the    |
  | top of this room (refer to Section 07 if you need help with the        |
  | machines here), and take the narrow passage in the ceiling upwards.    |
  | Up in the conveyor belt room, hopefully you previously programmed the  |
  | winch to be able to carry you up through the shortcut here!  If not,   |
  | you'll have to take the long way through the mines; refer to Section   |
  | 07 for how to do that.  Either way, make sure that the conveyor belts  |
  | are properly programmed from before, then take the bottom-right        |
  | passage out of the conveyor belt room.                                 |
  |                                                                        |
  | Ride the hoist up to the top of this room (see Section 07 if you need  |
  | help programming the machines here), then leave through the upper-left |
  | passage.  Back in the conveyor belt room, flip the lever to reverse    |
  | the conveyors (assuming you programmed it that way), then jump across  |
  | the room to the left, and take the ceiling passage up into the Old     |
  | Waterway.                                                              |
  |                                                                        |
  | Climb past the enemies to the top of this room, let the lift machine   |
  | push you under the water, and avoid the jellyfish as you take the      |
  | upper-left passage.  In the next room, use the multiplexer machine to  |
  | get up to the top of the room (see Section 07 if you need help         |
  | programming it), then head to the left and out the left-hand passage.  |
  | Head left through the next room, feeding the ducks along the way if    |
  | you want (quack!), and left again through the room after that.         |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be in the room with the two giant toads.  You're almost there,  |
  | so don't mess up here!  Shimmy under the toads and head through the    |
  | left-hand passage to return to the factory elevator room.  Drop down   |
  | into the Mermaid Vale.  From here, head through the right-hand         |
  | passage, then right, down, and out through the bottom-left passage.    |
  | Once you reach the hot spring room, you should be home free; just head |
  | left to return to the village, then deliver the flower to Selene in    |
  | the bottom center hut.  Congratulations!                               |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

                     ,-,-,--------------------------,-,-,
  ,-----------------( ( ( #9: The Vine Garden Flower ) ) )-----------------,
  |                  '-'-'--------------------------'-'-'                  |
  | To deliver this flower, first you must have visited the Hidden Crypt   |
  | and activated circuit #3 there.                                        |
  |                                                                        |
  | Starting from the mermaid village, leave through the top-right passage |
  | and work your way up to the elevator room, then take the elevator      |
  | upwards to the Rust Factory.  From the Rust Factory elevator room,     |
  | take the upper-left passage.                                           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pass leftwards through the next room.  *Don't* jump down in the room   |
  | after that; first, jump over to the console, which is usable now that  |
  | we've activated circuit #3.  Make sure the rotor machine is programmed |
  | to move, ideally with a single-instruction "MOVE <" program.  Then     |
  | drop down, slip through the opening under the rotor wheel, and take    |
  | the bottom-left passage out of this room.                              |
  |                                                                        |
  | You find yourself back in the Vine Garden, in a small room with a      |
  | flower in the top-right and a page in the bottom-left.  Being careful  |
  | not to fall into the narrow pit in the middle of the room, hop over to |
  | the left and collect the paper here if you haven't already.  Then head |
  | back right and jump onto the bottom-right tree branch.  Watching out   |
  | for the enemies, jump leftwards until you reach the little ledge at    |
  | the top-left.  Wait for the uppermost grub to be on the right side of  |
  | its treetop, then jump up there with it.  Hop over it carefully as it  |
  | moves left, then stand on the rightmost edge of the treetop; this will |
  | goad the vine bug into sliding down, allowing you to jump over it to   |
  | the other treetop.  From here it's a simple matter to walk over and    |
  | jump up to the flower.                                                 |
  |                                                                        |
  | Unfortunately, we can't go back the way we came, so you'll have to     |
  | jump down through the narrow pit at the bottom of the room.  You'll    |
  | land back in the large room where you first met Corra, way back near   |
  | the start of the game.  From here, you can just follow your minimap    |
  | back to the mermaid village; see Section 02 if you need help getting   |
  | back.  Deliver the flower to Selene in the bottom-center hut.          |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

                    ,-,-,----------------------------,-,-,
  ,----------------( ( ( #10: The Mermaid Vale Flower ) ) )----------------,
  |                 '-'-'----------------------------'-'-'                 |
  | To deliver this flower, first you must have reached the mermaid        |
  | village.                                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | Starting from the mermaid village, leave through the top-right         |
  | passage.  You'll find yourself at the hot springs; pass through here   |
  | and leave to the right.  This next room is the eastern end of the      |
  | vale.  Head right, avoiding the fish.  Jump upwards, then around to    |
  | the left, avoiding the crabs and one more fish, and go through the     |
  | upper-left passage.  Continue to the left through the elevator room.   |
  | Finally you'll reach the room with the flower.  Climb up the left side |
  | of the room, jump over the the flower, and press the B button to pick  |
  | it up.                                                                 |
  |                                                                        |
  | Don't jump straight down from where you picked up the flower, because  |
  | if you do the bird enemy will probably hit you and break the flower.   |
  | Instead, jump back to the left and drop down on that side of the room, |
  | then swim back out the right-hand passage.  Pass through the elevator  |
  | room back to the big eastern room.  After jumping over the first crab, |
  | you'll see there's a little hole here before the fish pool.  If you    |
  | want, you can try to drop through it and hold Left for a bit to land   |
  | on the narrow island between the two fish down below, but if you mess  |
  | up you'll probably get hit by the fish and have to go back for the     |
  | flower.  If you don't want to risk it, then instead of dropping        |
  | through the little hole, just hop over the fish pond to the right and  |
  | past the crab, drop down the right-hand side of the room, and then     |
  | proceed out the bottom-left passage, avoiding the two fish.  Once you  |
  | reach the hot springs, you're home free; just keep going left into the |
  | mermaid village, head to the bottom-center hut, and talk to Selene to  |
  | deliver the flower.                                                    |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

                    ,-,-,----------------------------,-,-,
  ,----------------( ( ( #11: The Volcanic Pit Flower ) ) )----------------,
  |                 '-'-'----------------------------'-'-'                 |
  | To deliver this flower, first you must have visited the Salt Mines and |
  | activated circuit #4 there.                                            |
  |                                                                        |
  | Starting from the mermaid village, leave through the top-right passage |
  | and work your way up to the elevator room, then take the elevator      |
  | upwards to the Rust Factory.  From the Rust Factory elevator room,     |
  | take the lower-right passage.                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pass straight through this room and the next two rooms from left to    |
  | right; feed the ducks along the way if you want.  Quack!  After the    |
  | duck room, you'll need to climb up past the grubs (remember the trick  |
  | to get past them: standing right in front of them, and jump as soon as |
  | they inch towards you), then keep heading right and exit the room      |
  | through the right-hand passage.  This brings you back to the room with |
  | the lift machine that can push you underwater.  Jump up and to the     |
  | right to get to the top of the room, then drop down the right hand     |
  | side, and continue downwards to leave this room through the bottom     |
  | passage.                                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the conveyor belt room in the Salt Mines.  Jump      |
  | across the upper platforms to the right, and drop down the right side  |
  | of this room.  If you need to, refer to Section 07 to program the      |
  | conveyors and hoist here, as well as the hoist and lift machines in    |
  | the room to the east; you'll want all those programs in place before   |
  | you try to carry the flower back through here.  When you're ready,     |
  | leave the conveyor room through the vertical passage in the floor      |
  | below the hoist machine.                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | In this large room, drop carefully down the left side, avoiding all    |
  | the wasp enemies, and leave through the bottom-left passage.  In this  |
  | small room, drop down and leave through the left-hand passage to       |
  | return to the Volcanic Pit.                                            |
  |                                                                        |
  | Jump up and left, past the lift machine here, and take the upper-left  |
  | passage, watching out for the hothead above.  You'll find yourself in  |
  | a small room bristling with spikes, with a flower on the left side.    |
  | Pick your way past the spikes to the flower, and pick it up.           |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now we just have to get this flower back without breaking it, starting |
  | by getting out of this room.  Jump right twice to reach the right-hand |
  | ledge.  Next, you need to jump left onto the little platform; you'll   |
  | probably find you need to release the A button slightly early to avoid |
  | hitting your head and falling into the spikes below.  From here, jump  |
  | up and left, then up onto the top of the L-shaped platform.  From      |
  | here, make another low jump (with a short A button press) to the small |
  | platform to the right.  Next you need to jump right again, at first    |
  | holding Right on the D-pad to make sure you don't hit the ceiling      |
  | spikes on your way up and that you continue past the upwards spikes,   |
  | then reversing and tapping Left on the D-pad to make sure you don't    |
  | sail past the little platform below.  Finally, make a medium jump      |
  | (again releasing A slightly early) over the final spikes, and exit     |
  | this room.                                                             |
  |                                                                        |
  | Now, we backtrack.  Use the lift to reach the upper-right passage and  |
  | return to the Salt Mines.  Climb past the two wasps here to return to  |
  | the large room.  Climb over the wall here, then drop down and head     |
  | right, and use the hoist and trolley to travel up and left to the      |
  | ceiling passage in this room (see Section 07 if you need to reprogram  |
  | these).  Take the ceiling passage upwards.                             |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the conveyor room, take the hoist up and leave through the     |
  | passage to the right.  In the next room, ride the hoist up while the   |
  | lift machine protects you, and leave through the upper-left passage to |
  | return to the top of the conveyor room.  Use the lever to reverse the  |
  | conveyor belts (assuming you programmed it that way), and leap across  |
  | to the left side of the room before taking the ceiling passage back to |
  | the Old Waterway.                                                      |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the Old Waterway, climb past the grub and fish, let the lift   |
  | machine push you down into the water, and leave through the upper-left |
  | passage, avoiding the jellyfish.  Use the multiplexer machine to get   |
  | through the next room, and leave through the left-hand passage.  Feed  |
  | the ducks if you like (quack!), then keep heading left through the     |
  | next couple rooms until you return to the factory elevator room.       |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you're back in the factory elevator room, take the elevator down  |
  | and return to the mermaid village to deliver the flower.               |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'

                     ,-,-,--------------------------,-,-,
  ,-----------------( ( ( #12: The Salt Mines Flower ) ) )-----------------,
  |                  '-'-'--------------------------'-'-'                  |
  | To deliver this flower, first you must have visited the Salt Mines and |
  | activated circuit #4 there.                                            |
  |
  | Starting from the mermaid village, leave through the top-right passage |
  | and work your way up to the elevator room, then take the elevator      |
  | upwards to the Rust Factory.  From the Rust Factory elevator room,     |
  | take the lower-right passage.                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pass straight through this room and the next two rooms from left to    |
  | right; feed the ducks along the way if you want.  Quack!  After the    |
  | duck room, you'll need to climb up past the grubs (remember the trick  |
  | to get past them: standing right in front of them, and jump as soon as |
  | they inch towards you), then keep heading right and exit the room      |
  | through the right-hand passage.  This brings you back to the room with |
  | the lift machine that can push you underwater.  Jump up and to the     |
  | right to get to the top of the room, then drop down the right hand     |
  | side, and continue downwards to leave this room through the bottom     |
  | passage.                                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be back in the conveyor belt room in the Salt Mines.  Jump      |
  | across the upper platforms to the right, and drop down the right side  |
  | of this room.  If you need to, refer to Section 07 to program the      |
  | conveyors here, as well as the hoist and lift machines in the room to  |
  | the east; you'll want all those programs in place before you try to    |
  | carry the flower back through here.  When you're ready, leave the      |
  | conveyor room through the bottom-left passage.                         |
  |                                                                        |
  | Pass straight through this next room, avoiding the enemies, after      |
  | which you'll be back at the top of the room with the crane that picks  |
  | up and drops the metal cage.  If you need to, drop down and refer to   |
  | Section 07 to program the crane, then return to the top of the room.   |
  | When you're ready, jump from the top-right ledge of the room onto the  |
  | top of the crane machine itself (not the metal cage!) as it's moving   |
  | up, then quickly jump onto the upper-left platform before you get      |
  | crushed against the ceiling.  From here, go through the upper-left     |
  | passage.                                                               |
  |                                                                        |
  | You'll be a new, small room, with two hoist machines, a flower, and a  |
  | page on the left-hand side.  Each hoist has a limited range of motion, |
  | and you'll have to program them carefully to allow you to move from    |
  | one to the other without getting crushed.  Use the two consoles to     |
  | enter the following programs:                                          |
  |                                                                        |
  |         Left-hand console                    Right-hand console        |
  |         (left-hand hoist)                    (right-hand hoist)        |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |     | 0:MOVE v  | 8:        |            | 0:MOVE v  | 8:        |     |
  |     | 1:TIL Z=5 | 9:        |            | 1:TIL Z=3 | 9:        |     |
  |     | 2:SYNC    |10:        |            | 2:REST 4  |10:        |     |
  |     | 3:REST 4  |11:        |            | 3:MOVE ^  |11:        |     |
  |     | 4:MOVE ^  |12:        |            | 4:TIL Z=0 |12:        |     |
  |     | 5:TIL Z=0 |13:        |            | 5:SYNC    |13:        |     |
  |     | 6:REST 4  |14:        |            | 6:REST 4  |14:        |     |
  |     | 7: ----   |15:        |            | 7: ----   |15:        |     |
  |     +===========+===========+            +===========+===========+     |
  |                                                                        |
  | With these programs running, jump into the right-hand hoist cage when  |
  | it stops at its lowest position, then wait until the two cages sync    |
  | up.  Quickly step into the left-hand cage, and make sure you're        |
  | completely inside of it so that you don't get crushed when the two     |
  | cages start moving apart.  Let the left-hand cage carry you to the     |
  | top, then step out and grab the flower.  If you haven't collected the  |
  | paper here yet, then before dropping down, wait for the left-hand cage |
  | to move back down out of the way, then jump left to the ledge with the |
  | left-hand hoist machine, and from there drop down the left side so you |
  | can collect the paper.                                                 |
  |                                                                        |
  | Leave this room to return to the crane room.  At this point, you just  |
  | need to backtrack.  Hop across the top of the crane to leave through   |
  | the upper-right passage; pass through the next room (remembering that  |
  | the fire bug will only turn around at the top and bottom of its route, |
  | so you can jump behind it as it moves down to avoid getting shot at);  |
  | use the conveyors to leap over the spikes; ride the hoist up the       |
  | eastern room with the lift guarding you; use the conveyors at the top  |
  | to reach the ceiling passage to the Old Waterway.                      |
  |                                                                        |
  | Back in the Old Waterway, climb past the grub and fish, let the lift   |
  | machine push you down into the water, and leave through the upper-left |
  | passage, avoiding the jellyfish.  Use the multiplexer machine to get   |
  | through the next room, and leave through the left-hand passage.  Feed  |
  | the ducks if you like (quack!), then keep heading left through the     |
  | next couple of rooms until you return to the factory elevator room.    |
  |                                                                        |
  | Once you're back in the factory elevator room, take the elevator down  |
  | and return to the mermaid village to deliver the flower.               |
  '------------------------------------------------------------------------'



++==========================================================================++
|| Section 14: Notebook Pages                                               ||
++==========================================================================++

There are 45 notebook pages to collect in the game: 36 journal entries, and 9
field manual pages.  You can view and re-read them by pausing the game with the
START button, then pressing Down on the D-pad to reveal the notebook pages
window.  This section of the walkthrough lists every journal entry and field
manual page in the game, and where to find them.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #01] - Found in the northeast corner of the Shadow Labs, at the
top of the first room after the teleporter room, right next to the upper-left
passage.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in
Section 10.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #02] - Found at the top-center of the Shadow Labs region, in the
middle of the wide room with the minigun.  If you're coming from the right-hand
passage, you'll have to ride the minigun to reach it; if you're coming from the
left-hand passage, you can just jump up to the uppermost platform and then jump
down to grab this page.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect
this page in Section 10.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #03] - Found in the northwest end of the Old Waterway, in the
bottom-left corner of the wide room with the crabs.  If you're following the
walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 08.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #04] - Found in the center of the Shadow Labs, on the left end
of the wide room with the emitter machines.  To reach it, you'll have to
program the emitters to create a forcefield platform for you to stand on, just
under the note.  See Section 10 for details on how to program these machines.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #05] - Found near the center of the Old Waterway, halfway up the
tall room with the vertical multiplexer machine.  You'll have to program the
multiplexer to help you jump up and give you a place to stand just under the
note.  See Section 08 for details on how to program this machine.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #06] - Found in the southwest corner of the Shadow Labs, in the
bottom-left corner of the room with lots and lots of computer consoles.  Most
of the consoles are fake, but the one in the top-right corner is connected to a
hidden lift machine.  Just enter the program "MOVE ^", and the lift will rise
up from the lava, giving you a place to stand to collect this page.  If you're
following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 10.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #07] - Found in the center of the Rust Factory, at the bottom of
the small room with two birds in it.  You can just jump down and collect it
pretty easily; getting back up safely is a little more of a challenge.  If
you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 04.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #08] - Found in the center of the Hidden Crypt, at the top-right
of the small room with a big spike pit in the middle.  You'll have to jump
across the pit, climb up, and then jump back across to this page, avoiding the
bats.  The easiest way to make the second jump safely is to jump while the
upper bat is flying to the right, just before it reaches the right edge of the
gap.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section
05.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #09] - Found in the westernmost edge of the Salt Mines, in the
small room with the flower and two hoist machines.  See Section 08 for details
on how to program these machines to get you safely to the top of the room, then
head left and jump down onto the ledge with this page.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #10] - Found in the west end of the Volcanic Pit, at the
top-right corner of the huge room that you first fall into when entering from
the hot spring in the Mermaid Vale.  To reach it, you'll need to enter this
room from the upper-right passage, program the boiler machine to boost you up
to the top platforms (see Section 06 for details), then hop carefully across
the gaps to this page.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #11] - Found in the southeast corner of the Hidden Crypt, in the
center of the small spiral-shaped room.  If you're following the walkthrough,
you'll collect this page in Section 05.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #12] - Found near the center of the Vine Garden, on the
rightmost end of the very wide room that leads to the Lost Temple.  If you're
following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 03.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #13] - Found in the northwest corner of the Vine Garden, in the
right half of the room that you fall into when you first escape from the Prison
Caves.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in
Section 02.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #14] - Found in the northeast corner of the Vine Garden, in the
small hidden room with the flower.  This room can only be reached from the Rust
Factory, and only after activating circuit #3 by defeating Doomgaze in the
Hidden Crypt.  To reach this room, first go to the elevator room in the Rust
Factory (with the two jet machines), and leave through the upper-left passage.
Pass through the next room, and in the room after, jump over to the console and
program the rotor to turn the giant wheel.  This unblocks the path below so
that you can leave through the bottom-left passage to reach the hidden room
containing this page.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this
page in Section 05.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #15] - Found near the bottom center of the Prison Caves, in a
small room that connects to the bottom-left passage in the huge room where you
have to trap an orc in one of the prison cells on your way to rescue Alex and
your friends.  To reach this page, you'll have to jump back and forth over the
spike pit to climb up the left side, while avoiding the falling spikes dropped
by the slime above.  If you need to, you can bait the slime into dropping its
spike by jumping towards the slime and then quickly reversing direction to land
back where you started.  Then quickly jump across before it can reload.  If
you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 04.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #16] - Found in the Ancient City, in the middle of the inside of
easternmost building in the enormous room in the heart of the city.  If you're
following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 09.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #17] - Found in the Ancient City, in the middle of the inside of
the tall, narrow building just left of the center of the enormous room in the
heart of the city.  This page requires a bit of a precise jump to reach; get a
running start on the ledge just below and to the left, and don't jump until one
foot is already over the ledge.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll
collect this page in Section 09.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #18] - Found in the southwest corner of the Rust Factory, at the
top of the room with the crane.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll
collect this page in Section 04.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #19] - Found in the southeast corner of the Old Waterway, in the
middle of the wide room where you find the ADD/SUB opcodes.  To reach it,
program the multiplexer machine there to move the leftmost platform all the way
to the top and stop there so you can jump up; a program that consists entirely
of "MOVE ^" will do the job.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll
collect this page in Section 07.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #20] - Found near the southwest end of the Shadow Labs, at the
bottom of the tall room with the acid-shooting bug and the two fire-shooting
grubs.  To reach it, you'll first need to open the two gates by tagging the orc
and mermaid ghosts hidden elsewhere in the labs.  See Section 10 for detailed
instructions.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #21] - Found in the northeast corner of the Hidden Crypt, in the
middle of the tall room that leads back up out to the Lost Temple after you've
defeated Doomgaze.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this
page in Section 05.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #22] - Found in northeast end of the Old Waterway, in the small
room with the flower and the pump machine.  See Section 08 for instructions on
how to use the pump machine to get to the top of the room where the flower and
this page are.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #23] - Found just west of the center of the Power Core, in the
room with three lift machines blocking the way into the central core.  You'll
have to move the first two lift machines out of the way to reach this page,
which you can't do until you've activated circuit #5 in the Salt Mines.  If
you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 11.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #24] - Found on the western end of the Ancient City, inside of
the building that's on the right-hand side of the wide room on the outskirts of
the city.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in
Section 09.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #25] - Found at the top of the Salt Mines, in the upper-right
corner of the huge room with the conveyor belts.  To reach it, first reach the
upper-left corner of the room, either by jumping across the conveyors from
below (see Section 07 for details) or by dropping down from the Old Waterway
above if you've already passed through the Salt Mines before.  Jump onto the
platform just under the ceiling passageway, then hop carefully across two
platforms to the right to reach this page.  If you're following the
walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 07.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #26] - Found in the northeast corner of the Salt Mines, behind
the fire-spitting bug in the tall room with the hoist and lift machines.  To
reach it, program the lift machine to follow along with the hoist machine as
you ride it up (see Section 07 for details), and then, when the fire-spitting
bug isn't in the way, jump onto the lift and then jump over to this page.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #27] - Found at the bottom of the Volcanic Pit, in the small
room just southeast of the huge room you first fall when entering from the
Mermaid Vale.  To reach this page, you'll first need to collect the COPY
opcode, then use it program the boiler to send steam bursts through the bottom
pipe so that you can get a boost up to the ledge with this page on it; see
Section 06 for more detailed instructions.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #28] - Found in the upper half of the Lost Temple, in the small
room that's on the right-hand side of the spire.  To reach the passage into
this room, you'll first have to climb up to the top half of the tall room with
the plaque on the wall, then drop down the gap on the right-hand side and enter
the passage.  Once you enter the small room containing this note, jump across
to the right-hand side of the room, then jump up and left to the small center
platform, and from there up and left to the next small platform.  If you get a
running start, you can run across one-block-wide gaps without jumping, so take
advantage of that to run across the small gap to the left, then jump up to the
uppermost platforms, then run to the right across the small gap to reach this
page, avoiding the enemies along the way.  If you're following the walkthrough,
you'll collect this page in Section 03.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #29] - Found in the northernmost point of the Mermaid Vale, in
the side of the elevator shaft that leads up to the Rust Factory.  To reach
this page, program the elevator to move up and down (see Section 04 for
instructions), then ride it up, and jump off to the right just before reaching
the top of the room.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #30] - Found in the Ancient City, inside the crumbling building
just right of the center of the enormous room in the heart of the city.  To
enter this building, you'll first need to solve the semaphore puzzle to unlock
the center door in the rightmost building (see Section 09 for instructions).
Then enter the rightmost building, exit through the unlocked center door, then
cross the bridge to the left to enter the building containing this page.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #31] - Found in the south-center of the Power Core, at the
bottom of the tall room to the left of the elevator shaft.  If you're following
the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 05.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #32] - Found in the southeast corner of the Shadow Labs, just
outside the door to the boss room.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll
collect this page in Section 10.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #33] - Found in the southwest of the Ancient City, in the room
with a bird enemy just under a broken bridge, on a small ledge on the left side
of the one-way shaft that drops down from there back into the Old Waterway.
You'll have to jump past the bird and down the shaft, holding Left on the D-pad
so that you land on the ledge with this page.  If you're following the
walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 09.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #34] - Found in the southwest corner of the Lost Temple, at the
top of the one-way shaft that drops down into the Hidden Crypt.  You won't be
able to reach this room until you've first rescued Alex and then met up with
him in the temple (see Sections 04 and 05).  When you meet up with him, he'll
give you a boost so you can jump onto his arms and from there over the wall to
the passage leading into the room with this page.  Or, if you've already
entered the crypt and escaped again, return to the huge room in the center of
the temple where you met Alex, and he'll have left a crate for you to stand on
so you can re-enter the shaft room without him.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #35] - Found in the bottom-center of the Ancient City, on top of
the small building that contains the (initially) friendly orc and his flower.
To reach this page, jump from the passage ledge onto the very left edge of the
building roof, so that only your right foot is on the roof; this will allow you
to land on the roof without running into the giant toad enemy.  From there,
either jump over the toad, or walk under it when it jumps, to reach this note.
If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 09.

[JOURNAL ENTRY #36] - Found in the Prison Caves, in the cell that the orcs
throw you into at the beginning of the game.  If you failed to pick up this
note before escaping from your cell, you can return later when rescuing your
friends (see Section 04) or anytime after.  Just enter from the right-hand
passage, jump from the crate up to the lever, flip the lever to open the gate
into your former cell, and walk in to grab this page.  If you're following the
walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 01.

[FIELD MANUAL #1] - Found in the bottom of the Lost Temple, in the bottom-left
of the huge room that only has one carriage machine.  If you're following the
walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 03.

[FIELD MANUAL #2] - Found in the west end of the Prison Caves, on the left side
of the huge room that you pass through while escaping from your cell near the
start of the game.  If you failed to pick up this note while escaping from your
cell, you can return later when rescuing your friends (see Section 04) or
anytime after.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page
in Section 01.

[FIELD MANUAL #3] - Found near the center of the Vine Garden, in the middle of
the tall room with the lift machine that can be used to squash a grub enemy.
If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 02.

[FIELD MANUAL #4] - Found at the top center of the Volcanic Pit, on the right
side of the wide room where you find the COPY opcode.  To reach this page,
program the boiler machine to send steam jets upwards out the bottom pipe (see
Section 06 for instructions), then stand on the pipe and let the steam boost
you up, holding Right on the D-pad once it does, and you should land on the
high platform with this page.

[FIELD MANUAL #5] - Found in the western half of the Rust Factory, at the
bottom of the tall room that connects up to the Power Core area.  If you're
following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 04.

[FIELD MANUAL #6] - Found in the southwest corner of the Vine Garden, in the
tall room to the left of the huge room with the tower.  If you're following the
walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 02.

[FIELD MANUAL #7] - Found in the southeast of the Power Core, on the left side
of the elevator shaft that leads down to the Rust Factory.  To reach this page,
jump down from the top of the shaft (after moving the jet machine platform out
of the way) while holding Left on the D-pad.  If you're following the
walkthrough, you'll collect this page in Section 05.

[FIELD MANUAL #8] - Found in the Ancient City, inside the squat building on the
far left end of the enormous room in the heart of the city.  To reach this
page, you'll have to program the rocket machines to destroy the various crates
blocking your path.  See Section 09 for more detailed instructions.

[FIELD MANUAL #9] - Found in the northwest corner of the Lost Temple, in the
small room just to the left of the plaque that's mounted near the top of the
temple.  If you're following the walkthrough, you'll collect this page in
Section 03.
